| 132 | **Messaging — chat attachments reach the people they were sent to, and long messages collapse or open as a card (2026-10-04).** **(A) ATTACHMENTS (a bug, four causes).** Files uploaded and linked correctly but never rendered on `/messages/[id]` or `/messages/[id]/files`. (1) The DM read hard-coded `attachments: []` — the loader now lives in `projects/message-attachments.ts` (`fetchMessageAttachments(actor, messageTable, ids)`), shared by the DM and project reads and by both send echoes (which also used to return `[]`). (2) A chat upload lands in the SENDER's `personal` library (private), and `files.fn_can_read` had no arm for anyone else: measured on the local DB, the sender read 3/3 file rows and the recipient 0/3, so the Files tab silently dropped them. `fn_can_read` gains a message arm (attached to a message `comms.can_read_message` admits), and the `message_attachments` INSERT policy now requires `files.fn_can_read(attachment_id)`, so linking cannot reach somebody else's private asset (verified: refused with an RLS violation). (3) Stored files had no URL (`""` in the bubble, `"#"` in the explorer); both now address the private-object route `fileObjectHref` (`md` rendition in the bubble, `sm` thumbnail in the explorer). (4) Media tiles were `<button>`s with no action; they are new-tab links (the original for an image) or inert spans. **(B) TEXT CAP.** `ChatMessage.text` was `max(4000)` while sends accept 8000, and `insertDmMessage` STORED only the first 4000 characters — a long DM was truncated on the way in, unrecoverably. The read schema, both read clamps and both write paths now use 8000. **(C) LONG MESSAGES.** `core/message-length.ts`: over 750 characters or 10 author-written lines → four-line CSS clamp with Show more / Show less; over 4,000 → a long-message card opening a modal `Dialog` (portalled). Lines are author line breaks, not wrapped lines, so SSR and the island agree. `ChatFeed` observes rows with a `ResizeObserver` so an expansion re-measures the virtualized row. **VERIFIED** on the local Supabase as the recipient (a real local session): 3/3 attachments in the chat and the Files tab, the object route 302 → 200 bytes (xlsx, and the photo as its WebP rendition), the thumbnail loaded; a stranger still reads 0; the existing 4,000-character DM collapses and expands with 0 overlapping rows; on the mock server a 5,581-character message renders as a card, the dialog shows all 5,581 and closes on Escape with focus back on the card, and a 14-line short message collapses on the line rule. `check` / `lint` clean; `test:unit` 1838 passed, the 4 known theme-engine failures; new `message-length.test.ts`. **No new Dev Context axis.** **FLAGGED for a human:** (a) the `fn_can_read` arm and the INSERT policy were applied directly to the LOCAL database (`CREATE OR REPLACE` + policy swap) as well as edited into the migrations; (b) a voice memo's own file is also an attachment row, so after a reload it shows as an audio file tile next to nothing (there is no waveform column) — pre-existing for project channels, now visible in DMs too; (c) messages already stored truncated at 4,000 characters cannot be recovered; (d) the `web-wallet` launch config injects `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` with its quotes, so the object route cannot sign URLs under it (use `web-stripe`, which trims them). | `supabase/migrations/{00001160_functions_files,00002012_policies_comms}.sql` · `packages/types/projects/messages.ts` · `packages/backend/services/projects/{message-attachments,live-messages,live-writes}.ts` · `packages/backend/services/messaging/{live-queries,live-writes,live-workspace,MessagingBackendService}.ts` · `apps/web/features/projects/{core/message-length.ts (+ test),components/{MessageBody,MessageBubble,MessageMedia}.tsx,islands/ChatFeed.island.tsx,styles/chat-feed.css}` · `documentation/{business/PRODUCT_SPEC.md,design-system/DESIGN_SYSTEM.md,database/files/Functions.md,database/comms/Policies.md}` · Decisions #128 / #131 |
| 133 | **The Project Details lane gets a top tier of primary views, an archetype-specific body and a utility-only footer; every engagement's discussion moves to `/projects/[slug]/discussion` (2026-10-05). AMENDS DESIGN_SYSTEM §B.6.3 at the product owner's direction; REFINES Decision #121 (C).** The lane's views were icon-only buttons in its footer, folded into a kebab by a width-fit algorithm as the lane narrowed, ranked below the collapse toggle; the project-wide room sat in a GENERAL accordion. **(A) FOUR ZONES.** Header (Back · Star · kebab over the identity header) → **top tier** (`ProjectNavSection`: package `NavItem` rows, icon + label) → **contextual body** → **utility footer** (`LaneFooter`: the docked `LaneCollapseButton` + an icon-only Project details link, nothing else). The set is ONE function, `projectViewLinks(detail, sessionKind)`, shared by the top tier and the collapsed `ProjectRail` (48px `--shell-nav-block` squares, portal `Tooltip`, status in the name): **Task** Discussion · Files · Submissions · Members; **one-off** Discussion · Timeline · Files · Submissions · Members; **pipeline** Discussion · Board · Timeline · Files · Submissions · Members; **session** Discussion · Calendar · Files · Members. Status is a trailing dot from the projection's own facts (the room's unread · a stage's new ticket on Board · a revision on Submissions). Discussion and Submissions are SECTION links (`aria-current="true"` on pages beneath them). Details/settings leave the views for the footer; Attachments leaves for Files (its route already 308s there). Deleted: `ProjectViewNav`, `viewnav-fit` (+ test), `TaskMembersSection`, `taskCollaborators`, `taskDiscussionOf`. **(B) THE BODY.** Staged: quick filters + a STAGES `LaneSection` with the owner's inline ＋, then Teams / Private Messages when the viewer has something in them (Decision #120) — **no General folder**. Task: Overview (Status · Due · Owner · **Assigned**, the ticket holder from the board read, since the live roster often omits them) + the Tasks checklist; the roster is the top tier's Members. Sessions: their panels without a General list. **(C) THE DISCUSSION ADDRESS.** `DISCUSSION_REF = "discussion"` (`@projective/types/projects`) is the polymorphic channel segment's fifth self-describing form. One rule decides its room — `discussionRoomOf`/`discussionOf` (a Task's root stage by order, else a general room; any other engagement's first general = `project_all` room, never a stage) — and every layer reads it: `findStageChannel(…, { task })`, `expandChannelRef` (fixture matchers in messages/files/members/submissions/board), `resolveChannelRef`'s discussion arm live (reads `format` + `structure_variation`; a Task's root stage `stage_all` room, else `project_all`), `stageForChannel` (live board) and `resolveStages` (live submissions). Islands, the composer, the feed and every tab carry the WORD, so URLs stay canonical and the server resolves the room. `discussion.tsx` serves the bare address (303 to the engagement when it has no room). Old addresses of the room (a Task's `stg-…` slug or room id, a staged engagement's project-wide room id) 303 to `/discussion` + tab path + query from `[channelId]/_middleware.ts` (`legacyDiscussionRedirect` over `addressesDiscussion`); a staged engagement's stages keep their own addresses. `channelHeaderFor` heads it "Discussion" (a Task: its title, "Task discussion"). **(D) THE ROOM EXISTS.** The app's live create path (`insertProject`, a direct insert + `create_stage`) never opened a `project_all` room — measured: the newest local app-created project had none — so it now calls `comms.get_or_create_project_channel(project, NULL, 'General')` (executable by `authenticated`; verified as the owner in a rolled-back transaction, dedupes). The fixture corpus drops its second general room ("Announcements") to match the one-room live contract. **(E) PACKAGE.** `NavItem` gains an optional decorative `trailing` slot and a tri-state `active` (`"true"` → `aria-current="true"`); `LaneIconButton` gains `href` (renders an `<a>`). **(F) FIXED IN PASSING.** `live-writes` inserted `channel_files.channel_id` from the raw route segment — a `stg-…` (now also `discussion`) address failed that uuid insert silently; it uses the resolved room. `stageRoomOf` now filters to `stage_all`/`project_all`, ordered as `projects`' read-access rule does — the bare `stage_id` match could return a private room. **No migration, no new Dev Context axis (§5), no lifecycle change.** **Verified:** `deno task check` clean; 449 unit tests in the touched areas (+16 routing, +6 discussion link); live (minted local JWTs): pipeline + one-off top tiers, `/discussion` → the `project_all` room, old room id → 303 with tail + query, a newly created live **Task** got both rooms, its `discussion` resolved to the STAGE room for read AND write, its `stg-…` address 303'd; fixtures: Task/session sets, identical history via `discussion` and the room's own address; browser at 1440: the four zones, the collapsed rail (all 48px, all named, Tooltip-wrapped), the collapse/expand toggle at one point (83, 856) in both states, zero horizontal overflow at 280px. **FLAGGED — surface, do not silently resolve:** (a) the brief asked for a Project Details / Settings MODAL trigger; no such modal exists, so the footer links the engagement's root page (the owner's configuration workspace, a member's dashboard); (b) live projects created before (D) have no project-wide room and therefore no Discussion link — no backfill shipped (one local row: `prj-fw26ef9jya`); (c) sessions were not in the brief — they got the same treatment (Discussion · Calendar · Files · Members, General list removed) for consistency; (d) the quick filters and the Teams / Private Messages groups were kept (the brief named only the General tree for replacement; removing Teams would strand team rooms); (e) the fixture "Announcements" room carried the chat empty-state demo, which the fixture corpus no longer shows; (f) the Board link is labelled "Board" (the brief offered Board / Pipeline); (g) a staged engagement's `/discussion/tasks` and `/discussion/details` render their miss bodies (the header shows neither tab for a general room) rather than redirecting; (h) one verification project was created in the local dev DB (`prj-h2fzyu6v47`, "Lane refactor check (Task)", owner hannahcole, one message). | `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §B.6.3 · §C.1 · §D.4 · `ROUTING.md` · `PRODUCT_SPEC.md` §The Three Work Flows + §Sitemap · `database/comms/Tables.md` · `packages/types/projects/detail.ts` (+ `stage-routing.test.ts`) · `packages/backend/services/projects/{live-support,live-board,live-submissions,live-writes,detail-fixtures,messages-fixtures,files-fixtures,members-fixtures,submissions-fixtures,board-fixtures}.ts` · `packages/ui/navigation/{components/{NavItem,LaneChrome}.tsx,styles/{nav,lane}.css}` · `apps/web/features/projects/{core/{chat-context,channel-view,channel-header-slot,discussion-guard,task-project,task-lane,stage-details-footer-slot}.ts(x) (+ tests),components/{ProjectNavSection,ProjectRail,ChannelTree,detail-glyphs,NormalSessionPanel,GroupSessionPanel,task-lane/*},islands/ProjectSidebar.island.tsx,styles/{project-sidebar,task-lane}.css}` · `apps/web/routes/(dashboard)/projects/[projectSlug]/{discussion.tsx,[channelId]/{_middleware.ts,details.tsx}}` · Decisions #21 / #23 / #61 / #88 / #120 / #121 |

The tabs are `LaneTabs`, which gained an optional `panelId` (`aria-controls` + tab ids, §C.1); Requests carries the dot with its count spoken, never a printed count (§D.1, #128(c)) — the `mem-head__count` and `mem-invites__count` pills are gone; figures are muted context text. **(B) The card** is the explore profile card's anatomy (#74(C): banner seam, centred overlapping avatar, centred name/`@handle`, left-aligned headline · middot meta · quoted note · foot) at CONSOLE density (§A.3 — no photograph, so `--card-radius` and a solid accent-tinted banner rather than a masked cover). Roles and stages are TEXT (§B.11): the role is the headline with its access summary in a Tooltip; assigned stages are a middot line on a pipeline only; only an invitation's status and a session attendee's standing take a `Tag`. The owner/client carries the one distinct accent — a crown mark (Tooltip + `aria-label`) and an avatar ring. A plain click opens `MemberPreviewModal` (roster facts + the public profile via `ProfileService.overview`, "View full profile" in the header); a middle / ⌘ / Ctrl click on the stretched `<a href="/@handle">` opens the profile in a new tab (`isModifiedClick`, the explore guard). Every card and row carries an icon-only **Message** (the `dm-{handle}` thread in the global `DraggablePopover` messenger via `openPopout`; the inbox page on mobile) and a kebab (open full profile · change role [& stages] · assign/unassign · remove). **(C) Requests** — a new `MemberRosterPage.requests` (`MemberRequestSchema`, `requestsForScope` applied once beside `invitesForScope`), read live from `projects.project_applications` (pending, owner-gated, seat/role targets resolved to their stage) and derived on the stub. **Accept** is the existing `/api/projects/applications/accept`; **Reject** is new end to end: `projects.reject_application` (`00001130` §7b — owner-only, row-locked, `pending → rejected`, logs `application_rejected`, notifies `application.declined` deep-linked to the owner's DM; `EXECUTE` granted in `00002510`), `rejectApplicationLive`, `ProjectBackendService.rejectApplication`, `POST /api/projects/applications/reject`, `RequestService.rejectApplication`. Verified in a ROLLED-BACK transaction on the local DB: owner declines, stranger / repeat / `anon` refused, notification + activity written. The stub accept seats the applicant (`recordApplicationSeat`). Accept · Reject · Revoke · Dismiss are optimistic with rollback; Remove still follows the server (escrow, #116). `PRODUCT_MANAGEMENT.md`'s application row gains the owner's decline as a writer of `rejected`. **(D) Project type** (`memberContextFor`): a one-off hides every stage control (filter, picker, chips) and edits the role alone; a pipeline shows each member's stages; a session shows each attendee's standing (`ProjectMemberRow.attendance`) and a seat line ("8 of 12 seats · 4 open") from a new `MemberRosterPage.session` (live: `projects.cohorts.max_seats`, `cohort_memberships`, the next `session_events` sitting; the Dev Context Switcher's `serviceType` re-shapes 1-1 ⇄ group). (b) The brief asked for role and stage BADGES; §B.11 and the PR checklist forbid containers on non-actionable metadata, so they render as text — confirm. (c) The session seat read has no local data to exercise (no cohorts in the seed); it degrades to `null` on any failed read. | 137 | **The pop-out chat closes on arriving at a chat view, opens in the main input from a ghost icon, and floats a compact glass composer over its feed (2026-10-05). AMENDS Decision #103 (A) at the owner's direction; a scoped glass extension of §B.4.3 in the line of #131.** **(A) ROUTE LIFECYCLE.** `ChatPopoutHost` reconciles the store with each document (`syncPopoutRoute`): arriving at a full chat view — `/messages/[conversationId]` or a channel's Chat tab (`/projects/[projectSlug]/[channelId]`, its `/chat` alias, the discussion room included; the pure `isChatRoute` in `messaging/core/popout-route.ts`, unit-tested) — from any other path closes the window, because that page now shows the chat it duplicated. The state carries `lastPath`, stamped on open and on every host mount, so the page the window was spawned on, and a reload of it, keep it. A back/forward-cache restore skips the mount, so `pageshow` (persisted) and `popstate` re-run the same reconciliation; both listeners detach on unmount. This REVERSES #103's verified "present on `/messages/dm-juno`" for a window carried into the inbox: arriving at ANY chat view closes it, not only the popped-out conversation's own page. **(B) OPEN IN INPUT.** The header action that read "Return" / "Open in inbox" is now one ghost icon-only link (`aria-label`/`title` "Open in input", the maximize mark, transparent fill, `--text-secondary` ink, the close button's `--overlay-action-size`); a plain primary click closes the window and navigates to its page, a modified click opens a new tab and leaves the window alone. The Members preview's "View full profile" (`.mem-preview__full`) takes the same ghost icon-only shape. **(C) THE FLOATING COMPOSER.** The composer moved INTO the popout's scroller as its sticky last child, so the scroller reaches the window's bottom edge and the in-flow composer reserves exactly its own (variable) height under the last message — no measured padding. At the owner's follow-up direction the band (`.pop-chat__composer`) carries NO background and NO border; only `.chat-composer__bar` is glass, on a `::before` underlay (`isolation: isolate`, `z-index: -1`; the Plus menu is body-portalled, so no fixed panel is re-based): `--surface-1` at `--glass-veil-chrome` with `blur(12px)` — the owner's radius, deliberately not `--glass-blur` (18px). The bar keeps its hairline (strengthened on focus) and drops its elevation, and the band tightens to `--space-1`/`--space-2` padding and `--space-1` editor padding (measured 55px band, 46px bar; previously ~69px). The drop-zone overlay was raised above the sticky layer. The scroller's flex `gap` was removed, so popout rows space exactly as `/messages/[conversationId]` does (`.msg-row` 12px between senders, 2px within a group — measured identical on both); the 15-minute `GROUP_WINDOW_MS` stays, by the owner's choice. **(D) PICKER SEARCH.** `ContactList`'s search is now `@projective/ui`'s `InputText` (`filled`, `block`, bound to the `query` signal, the clear control in its `end` slot, the native search cancel hidden); `InputTextProps` gained typed `autoFocus` and `aria-controls` pass-throughs. A pre-existing paste had spliced a region comment into the `.msg-picker__search-input, …` selector list, leaving the raw input unstyled — repaired. Verified in-browser (mock mode): spawn + reload on the origin keep the window; `/explore` keeps it with the ghost action; `/messages/dm-mara/files` keeps it; back to `/messages/dm-mara`, to another conversation, and via history Back each close it and clear `sessionStorage`; the open action lands on the conversation with no window; the last row sits 4px above the composer at the bottom and messages blur beneath it when scrolled. **FLAGGED — surface, do not silently resolve:** (a) "Open in input" names an action the link does not literally take — it navigates to the conversation page; the unsent popout draft is NOT carried into the page composer (no draft persistence exists). (b) A 12px blur is a second glass radius beside `--glass-blur`, which the token block calls drift. (c) The mobile profile sheet renders the same `PopoutChat`, so it gains the floating glass composer too (a sheet, already licensed by §B.4.3). | 138 | **Universal backdrop dismissal and LIFO modal stacking (2026-10-05).** REFINES Decision #72(A). **(A) ESCAPE HAS ONE OWNER.** `useDismiss` no longer binds a `document` capture listener per instance; every open overlay pushes a layer onto `packages/ui/hooks/escape-stack.ts` in open order and one listener hands each press to the TOP layer only (`stopImmediatePropagation`). The per-instance listeners ran in registration order, so the five app modals that never passed `enabled: isTop` (`AttachmentPreviewModal`, `PreSubmitModal`, `ProjectCreateModal`, `MediaLightbox`, `SubmissionReviewModal`) could take a press meant for a layer above; their listeners also re-registered on every inline `onDismiss` change, so "order" meant "least recently re-rendered". `enabled` survives as a further gate on the top layer (a `BlockUI` above it makes Escape a no-op rather than falling through); a top layer with `closeOnEscape: false` lets the press travel untouched. `MediaLightbox` lost its second, window-level Escape handler. **(B) A MODAL WITH A SCRIM DISMISSES THROUGH THE SCRIM ONLY.** `TicketView`, `EventModal`, `AssetPicker` and `CommandPalette` wired both `closeOnOutside` (pointerdown) and a backdrop click; one press closed them twice, and a layer removed on pointerdown let the release land on the scrim of the modal beneath — the ticket → asset-picker chain could collapse on one click. All four now pass `closeOnOutside: false`; #72(A)'s ungated outside-pointer channel is unchanged for anchored popovers, which have no scrim. **(C) PRESS PRECISION.** `Backdrop` dismisses through the new `useBackdropPress` (`overlay/hooks/`, pure core `isDismissPress` in `overlay/core/backdrop-press.ts`): only a press that STARTS and ENDS on the scrim element itself, within `PRESS_SLOP` (6px), counts — a text selection or drag released over the scrim, a pan, or a press that began on a layer that has since unmounted never closes anything. Reused by the `Image` preview's empty stage (its panel covers its scrim, so it had no outside surface at all), the `CommandPalette` scrim and the marketing `SiteHeader` drawer scrim. **(D) THE DIALOG SCRIM WAS DEAD.** `.ui-portal > *` and `.ui-dialog` shared specificity (0,1,0); measured on `/projects/[slug]/board`, `portal.css` loads after `dialog.css`, so the full-viewport `.ui-dialog` positioner became `pointer-events: auto` and swallowed every `Dialog`/`ConfirmDialog` backdrop click. The portal default is now `:where(.ui-portal) > *`. **(E)** `CommandPalette` moved to the `modal` z-band; `MobileMenu` (aria-modal, unmounted in the app today) gained stack-managed Escape. Body scroll-lock stays the existing reference count in `useOverlayStack`, released only when the last locking overlay closes (verified across the ticket → picker chain). Verified in Chromium against `dev:mock`: Escape and backdrop each close only the picker over a ticket, a drag released on the scrim closes nothing, a dropdown inside the ticket takes the first Escape, a real `Dialog` scrim now receives its click. | `packages/ui/hooks/{escape-stack,escape-stack.test,useDismiss}.ts` · `packages/ui/overlay/{core/backdrop-press{,.test}.ts,hooks/useBackdropPress.ts,islands/Backdrop.tsx,styles/portal.css,mod.ts}` · `packages/ui/{utils/islands/CommandPalette,display/islands/Image,navigation/islands/MobileMenu}.tsx` · `apps/web/features/{projects/components/{ticket/TicketView,AttachmentPreviewModal,PreSubmitModal,ProjectCreateModal,SubmissionReviewModal},calendar/components/EventModal,files/islands/AssetPicker.island,view/components/MediaLightbox,marketing/islands/SiteHeader.island}.tsx` · `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §B.10.6, §C.1 |

Nobody is ever seated on every stage. **(D) STAGE INVITATIONS FOR EXISTING MEMBERS.** `POST /api/projects/members/stage-invite` (`StageInviteInputSchema`) → `ProjectBackendService.inviteMemberToStages`, which reuses the hire brief, `hireInvitationRefusal` (via `stageInviteAsHire`) and `insertInvitations` → `projects.invite_to_project` — one pending invitation per stage at the stage's configured terms; no intro DM and no seller intake (they joined once), not counted against the hire rate limit. `stageInviteRefusal` adds: the invitee must be on the roster, a `task` engagement has no further stage, a held stage or an open invitation is not offered twice. The freelancer answers through the existing `respond_to_project_invitation` path (the `/messages` context panel, `stage.invite` notification). `memberStagePicture` (`packages/types/projects/members.ts`) is the one resolver of a member's held · pending · offerable stages, read by the card, the kebab, the preview and the service. Stub path: an existing member's accepted stage invitation now merges into their row (`withStages` in `write-store.ts`) instead of being skipped. **(E) THE MEMBERS SURFACE.** The kebab moved onto `ActionMenu` with "Invite to stage ›" and "Remove from stage ›" cascades (a stage channel names its one stage); the model is pure (`core/member-menu.ts`). The card gains a `pending` slot: _Invited to {stage}_ in the meta register, the `Pending` lifecycle `Tag` and an inline **Cancel** (two shown, the rest folded to "+N more pending"; one line in the table). Held stages stay middot text (§B.11 — a stage name is metadata, not a status); removal is offered in the kebab and the preview, always behind `RemoveMemberDialog`. The preview gains a **Stages** section (`MemberStagePanel`) for a managing viewer: held stages with Remove, open invitations with Cancel invitation, and a checkbox picker that sends several at once. `MemberEditDialog` is role-only — its stage multiselect wrote seats nobody had accepted — and the stub-only quick Assign/Unassign toggle is gone for the same reason. **(F) `ActionMenu` (`@projective/ui/navigation`).** A Popover-contract kebab menu over the shared `MenuItem` model with cascading submenus: root and every flyout through `BodyPortal`, flyouts owned by their parent row (presses inside never dismiss), one overlay-stack claim, one escape-stack layer closing the deepest level first (#138), roving focus with Right/Left/Enter/Space/Home/End/typeahead, hover intent with an aim triangle, `right-start` flyouts that flip and clamp. `MenuItem` gains `danger`, `hint`, `disabledReason`, `emptyLabel` and is now exported from `@projective/ui/navigation`; pure logic in `navigation/core/menu-tree.ts` (+ the first `menu.test.ts`). TieredMenu / ContextMenu were NOT refactored onto it. **Verified:** a rolled-back psql transaction against the local database (draft acceptance parks one seat; a draft's type still changes; publish promotes seat and stage; the type change is then refused and an unchanged write passes; a single-stage whole-project invite takes its seat; a multi-stage one takes none); `deno task check` 0, `deno task lint` clean, `test:unit` 1,932 passed / 4 known `theme-engine` failures; (b) the roster shows stages only on a `pipeline` format (`memberContextFor`), so a multi-stage one-off gets no stage invitations on `/members`; (c) `MemberStageRef` carries no price, so an unpriced stage on a live project is offered and then refused ("Price {stage} on the project…") rather than shown disabled; The `loadOlder` prop contract is tightened: `null` means failure, exhaustion is `hasMore: false`. | 142 | **`/projects` step 5 — the project view loses its banner and gains the client's context (More by · Similar · the client's reviews), its stages expand smoothly, its lane docks clear of both top bars, the owner's preview is the real page, and `/projects` becomes a portfolio (2026-10-05). REVERSES Decision #44's "no cross-sell, no reviews" and #96(B)'s banner strip AT THE PRODUCT OWNER'S DIRECTION; REFINES #96 / #79.** **(A) NO BANNER.** `.evp-banner` and its rules are deleted; the seller line now shows the client's face (it was `avatar={false}` because the banner carried it) and its score jumps to `#evp-reviews`. **(B) THE LANE DOCKS WITHOUT INTERSECTING THE BAND.** Measured first (1440 × 900, authed, `prj-6ndtxa9u9x`): the lane was NOT mis-offset — it pinned at 72px and stepped to 120 when the band condensed, exactly like a service — but a project's frame is short, so the 804px viewport-locked lane was carried up by the frame's foot (sticky is bounded by its containing block) to y = 29 across a band spanning 48–96, and at `--z-sticky + 1` it painted OVER the band's seller/rating block. Fix: in the authenticated shell every `.evp-laneslot` sits at `--z-raised` (it passes UNDER the in-flow band; the guest shell keeps the higher level, its band is fixed and inset clear of the lane); a project lane docks at `top: calc(var(--shell-topbar-h) + var(--shell-midnav-header-h) + var(--space-4))` at all times (the owner's preview band never collapses, a short public brief may never condense — a band-state-dependent pin was right on one and wrong on the other), and is content-sized and clamped to `100dvh` minus that line and one step, as a flex column so the clamp reaches `.pf-lane__scroll` (a percentage height cannot resolve against `max-block-size`). Measured after: top 112px, `max-height` 772px, `z-index` 10. **(C) SMOOTH STAGE DISCLOSURE, BOTH ARCHETYPES.** `StageProgressLedger` (shared by `?type=services` and `?type=projects`) stays native `<details>` and a server component; `::details-content` is a one-row grid springing `0fr` ⇄ `1fr` on `--spring-standard`/`--dur-medium` with `content-visibility` `allow-discrete`, the detail wrapped in a padding-free `.evp-track__reveal` clip that fades with it, the caret on the same timing, both reduced-motion channels jump to final. The one track rule on the `<ol>` grows and shrinks with the list (measured 437 → 330px on collapse, never broken); no border, fill or radius is drawn on the expanding region. **(D) RAILS AND REVIEWS ON A PROJECT.** `showsCommercialRails` now includes `project`. `live-view.ts`: a project's "More by" is the owner's other public PROJECTS (not their catalogue); "Similar & recommended" ranks other clients' projects by shared skills (×2) then same classification, drops zero-overlap briefs and excludes the owner's own (already in More by); reviews are the owner's CLIENT track via `org.get_profile_reviews` (author role `freelancer`), which releases `context_title` only for a `public` project — verified as `anon` and, in a rolled-back transaction, that `unlisted` redacts it. `EntityReviewSchema` gains optional `contextTitle`; `ReviewsPanel` gains `title` · `subtitle` · `emptyText` and prints "Project · <title>" only when present; an unreviewed client reads "No freelancer has reviewed … as a client yet" rather than 0.0. "More by" renders two-up first, "Similar" three-up beneath it. **(E) THE OWNER'S PREVIEW IS THE PAGE.** `/projects/[slug]/preview` renders `EntityViewPage mode="preview"` from `ExploreBackendService.projectPreviewPage(slug, actor)`, which reads the project's rows fresh with the owner's token (`readProjectPreview` — drafts included, never cached, never merged into the shared catalogue) and maps them through the catalogue's own `assemble` over the loaded profile directory, then composes with the public `composeViewPage` (rails emptied, no reviews read). The project-row column lists became shared constants so the two reads cannot drift. Double guard in `define.handlers`: signed in, then `viewerIsClient && previewReady` ("isSetupComplete"), else 303 to `/projects/[slug]`. The frame's back-link row is a Meta-register `PreviewNotice` ("This is how freelancers and applicants evaluate your project brief." · Edit details); no rails, no reviews, no lane kebab (`LaneIdentity` now omits the trigger for an empty menu); the lane footer and apply bar render `ProjectPreviewCta`, which NAMES the applicant's Apply/Message actions instead of rendering disabled controls (§3 gate 11). A brief that cannot be composed gets its own empty state pointing back to Details, never Explore's "Item not found". **(F) `/projects` IS A PORTFOLIO.** `PagePlaceholder` replaced by `ProjectPortfolio` (server component, CSS carried by `PortfolioStyleAnchor`), fed by `resolvePortfolio` → `resolveProjectsFeed` → `ProjectBackendService.list` with its OWN query (`scope=global`, nothing else — the lane's facets must not narrow the index): status filter links on `?status=` (deliberately not the lane's `statuses`) with counts; the aggregate stage burn SUMS delivered ÷ planned stages across staged projects in view (not an average of percentages; unstaged rows excluded, not counted as 0%), drawn as a segmented `--track-meter` (no inline style, never animated) with the figure in words; hairline rows with title link, middot meta, lifecycle status and per-row burn; an empty portfolio's one action is `/projects?create=1`, which the lane already turns into the Quick-Init modal. Pure model + 6 unit tests in `portfolio-model{,.test}.ts`. **FLAGGED — for a human:** (a) **Payment timeliness and spec clarity are not rendered** — no column stores per-review sub-scores, and inventing them is what the catalogue's "never invent" rule forbids; adding them is a `reviews.entity_reviews` schema change (+ Zod + docs) for a human to approve. (b) The client reviews' `verifiedEngagement` is `false`: `get_profile_reviews` withholds the engagement link with a private title, so a review on a private engagement cannot be told apart from one with none. (c) The seed has NO user-targeted client reviews and no public business-owned project, so the review list on a project page was verified through the RPC (as `anon`, with redaction) and the empty state in-browser, not as rendered entries. (d) The portfolio spans every workspace while the lane beside it defaults to the active one — deliberate (an overview vs a navigator), but a product call. (e) "Burn" is stage progress, not money: `ProjectSummary` carries no spend, and a monetary burn needs a finance read the summary does not have. (f) The preview pane's throttled frame clock (≈1 rAF / 300ms) made the expand transition look stuck mid-flight; it completes, and reads correctly once settled. `deno check` on every touched route/feature file and the new tests are green. | `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §D.8.1 · §D.8.6 + gate · `PRODUCT_SPEC.md` §The Entity View · sitemap `/projects` · `ROUTING.md` preview row · `packages/types/explore/view.ts` · `packages/backend/services/explore/{live-catalog,live-view,ExploreBackendService}.ts` · `apps/web/features/view/{components/{EntityViewPage,project-view-parts,ProjectCtaRig,StageProgressLedger,lane-parts},islands/{ProjectLane,ProjectApplyBar,ReviewsPanel}.island,core/{entity-archetype,view-ssr},styles/{entity-view,view}.css}` · `apps/web/features/projects/{components/portfolio/ProjectPortfolio.tsx,core/{portfolio-model,portfolio-model.test,portfolio-ssr}.ts,islands/PortfolioStyleAnchor.island.tsx,styles/portfolio.css}` · `apps/web/routes/(dashboard)/projects/{index,[projectSlug]/preview}.tsx` · Decisions #44 / #79 / #96 / #97 |

The canonical models are: **Task** — `format = one_off` with a stage-less structure (`single_task`; a legacy `single_stage` one-off also reads as a Task), held by `fn_enforce_structure_variation` to one stage and one ticket, with no Timeline and no Calendar (#121); **One-off** — `format = one_off`, `structure_variation = one_off`: ONE master ticket carried through a run of milestone stages (the trigger's project-wide one-ticket cap IS the model, and `finance.fn_hold_ticket_escrow` holds each milestone against that ticket's current stage at that stage's price), visualised on the Timeline; **Pipeline** — the multi-stage Kanban engine (its lane drops Timeline, #134). Sessions remain a provider-side work flow, not a fourth project type. The spec now states this once above the three work flows, footnotes the Escrow-by-type table (a Task follows the One-Off row) and names all three types in the Logic Summary Table. No code, schema or Zod change: the code already implemented exactly this (`projectTypeOf` / `columnsForProjectType` / `createInputForType`). **(B) CREATION IS TWO STEPS.** `documentation/flows/Projects.md` §1–§5 described the retired six-step wizard and named symbols that no longer exist (`ProjectWizardStep`, `WIZARD_STEP_*`, the `FieldTier` taxonomy, `CreateProjectStageSchema`, `effectiveVisibility`, a `direct_deliverable` create format) and constraints that were never migrated (`ck_projects_title_len`, `ck_projects_currency`, `ck_projects_deadline_bonus_format`, an `nda_mode` column). It now documents the Quick-Init `ProjectCreateModal` (title trimmed to 3–160 per `CreateProjectSchema` · description · type · contextual invitee; `single` on the lane, `stepped` on a profile, #118) followed by continuous configuration on the draft's workspace (the `setup-sections.ts` registry, Save + autosave-on-blur, the setup ladder, publish through `set_project_status`), cites the real consolidated migration files in place of the non-existent `0119`–`0122` / `0303`, and records that the live create path is `insertProject` + `create_stage`, not `projects.create_project`. **(C) DECOMPOSITION — pure moves, no behaviour change.** `components/setup/SetupSections.tsx` (3,082 lines) becomes a 126-line dispatch module that re-exports every prior symbol, over `SetupBriefSection` · `SetupStagesSection` · `SetupStageFields` · `SetupStageEditors` · `SetupRolesSection` · `SetupLegalSection` · `SetupAttachmentsSection` · `setup-primitives` · `setup-format` (all ≤ 461 lines; checked by a line-for-line containment script). `islands/ChatComposer.island.tsx` (1,269) becomes 600 lines over plain components in `components/composer/` (`ComposerActions` · `ComposerAttachments` · `ComposerAudioRecorder` · `ComposerNotices` · `composer-send`), effect order preserved and no new island exports. `core/setup-state.ts` (1,083) becomes a barrel over `setup-signals.ts` and `setup-persistence.ts`; the save blocker `firstBlocker` joins the existing `setup-validation.ts` (that name already belonged to the blur-gated verdict module, so the blocker joined it rather than replacing it); the serialiser's shared state is one `saveSession` object, and restore→flush crosses the module boundary through `bindQueueFlush` to avoid an import cycle. `ProjectCreateModal.tsx` (638) becomes 534 + `ProjectCreateTypeCards.tsx`. `ChannelTabBody.tsx` (no importer) is deleted. **(D) TESTS — 237 new, all colocated `*.test.ts` (the repo convention; `__tests__/` folders would breach root §4's folder rule).** Backend (130): `live-{board,detail,files,members,messages,submissions,invites}.test.ts` drive the real supabase-js client against a fetch-level PostgREST fake, pinning schema-valid projections, viewer isolation (a freelancer's submissions query is narrowed to their own rows; a non-manager never queries invitations or applications) and error behaviour. These modules' documented contract is to THROW on a query failure, return `null` on a miss and `{ refusal }` on a refused write; the `ServiceResult` envelope is applied one level up, in `ProjectBackendService`'s `liveRead` / `liveWrite`, so the tests pin the throw rather than a `ServiceResult.fail`. Frontend (107): `board-model`, `submission-model`, `submission-access` (the real submission state machine, `resolveWorkflowActions`, as a full role × status matrix), `board-access`, `task-view-guard`, `discussion-guard`, `setup-state` (`firstBlocker`). SQL: the escrow-door authorization probe `supabase/probes/escrow_settlement_authority.sql` (another session's) already asserts `insufficient_privilege` (42501) for anon, a subject-less JWT and the assigned earner on `complete_ticket` · `delete_ticket` · `force_complete_stage` · `approve_stage` · `cancel_stage_fair_exit`, and that the escrow stays held; it was not duplicated. **(E) DATABASE DOCS.** `database/projects/Tables.md` documents all 28 tables (was 16), with full `tickets` and `stage_submissions` column specs and no `stage_type`; `Functions.md` documents all 83 `projects.*` functions (was 33; the brief's "58" undercounted) with grants, caller predicates and SQLSTATEs; `Policies.md` documents all 60 policies across 28 tables, plus a Findings section; `database/Schemas.md`'s enum listing is corrected (`archived`, stage `cancelled`, no `stage_type_enum`). **(F) ONE DEFECT FIXED IN PASSING:** `core/discussion-guard.ts` ran `decodeURIComponent` unguarded, so a malformed escape (`/projects/x/%E0%A4%A`) threw a `URIError` out of the middleware as a 500; it now falls through to the route's own miss. **VERIFIED:** `deno task check` clean; `deno lint` clean (1,951 files); `deno task test:unit` 2,175 passed, with only the four pre-existing `theme-engine` failures (#96(a)). In a driven browser on the fixture server, the composer hydrates (the Plus popover opens with Upload / Library and closes; typing swaps Mic for Send), and both create-modal pacings behave as #118 describes (radiogroup roving focus with arrow wrap; stepped neutral start, Task → "New task" with focus on the name and the read-only invitee, Back restores the marked card). The escrow probe passes every assertion when the in-flight migrations are applied inside a rolled-back transaction. No DB migration, no lifecycle change, no new `@projective/ui` primitive (no §C.1 change), no new Dev Context axis. **FLAGGED — surface, do not silently resolve:** (a) ~~the running local database predated the in-flight grant fixes~~ — CLOSED the same day: a definitions-only apply (user-approved, by the security session) brought the shared DB up to the migrations, and `escrow_settlement_authority.sql` re-run against it prints 23 PASS / 0 FAIL; (b) **anon-callable SECURITY DEFINER writes with no caller check** — `claim_ticket`, `fn_assign_ticket_core`, `fn_flag_bad_faith_report`, `fn_archive_stale_service_drafts` (plus the unguarded reads `get_stage_details`, `get_stage_staffing`'s unchecked stage-to-project link and the `fn_serialize_*` helpers), and the always-true `cm.cohort_id = cohort_id` in "View session events" — verified against the live catalog, written up in `Policies.md` ## Findings and handed to the follow-up task "Lock down anon-callable projects definer functions", which carries the user's claim rule (only people assigned to a stage may claim its tickets — "tickets are claimed per stage"); (g) four islands/components still exceed 600 lines — `SubmissionExplorer` (1,060), `ProjectBoard` (966), `TicketView` (769), `MemberRoster` (743) — left alone because other sessions hold uncommitted edits in all four;
