| 144 | **The engagement's root becomes its Overview — `/projects/[slug]` dispatches on the viewer's access and the project's lifecycle, configuration moves to `/projects/[slug]/details`, prospects are sent to the public listing, and Preview gates on publish state (2026-10-06). AMENDS Decision #84 (the root as the owner's setup surface) and #142(E) (the preview gate); REFINES #133 / #134 (the lane's view sets) and #140 (the lane kebab stays the one home of Share · Archive).** From a live audit of `/projects/[slug]` (Playwright against the live local database as the owner `hannahcole`, the staffed freelancer `marisdelacroix` and the non-member `juno`, on the active pipeline `prj-6ndtxa9u9x` and the draft Task `prj-h2fzyu6v47`), which found one address rendering three wrong surfaces: the owner of an active, staffed engagement landed on the setup form (type and staffed prices disabled, "86% complete"); the hired freelancer was offered "Apply to project" (`project-footer-slot.tsx` mounted the applicant rig for every non-client); the non-member got the member workspace ("Your earnings €0.00", a board of drop targets they could not use). **(A) ACCESS IS A DERIVED FACT, AND DISPATCH IS A PURE FUNCTION.** `ProjectDetail.viewerAccess: owner | participant | prospect` (`@projective/types/projects/access.ts`). Owner = the client side (`viewerIsClient`); participant = `projects.has_project_access`, the RLS predicate itself (owner, participant row, owning business, a live freelancer or team stage assignment), read as an RPC on the live path because `viewerRole` falls back to `member` for a stranger and cannot tell one from a hire — and, when the RPC fails, the seat signals the read already holds (an own participant row, a hired team, any reachable room), never a guess; fixtures resolve every corpus row as owner or participant (the corpus is the viewer's own). `landingFor(access, status)` → `overview` (an owner on any non-draft status, a participant on any status) · `details` (an owner on a draft) · `listing` (a prospect on a published project) · `missing` (a prospect on a draft). `previewAllowed(status, previewReady)`: a draft needs its setup ladder; a published project always previews, because the public can already see it. There is no `invitee` member: an invitation is not access until it is accepted, the two would resolve identically, and a value nothing can produce is dead code (the #84 reachability rule). **(B) THREE ADDRESSES.** `/projects/[slug]` is the **Overview** for the owner and every participant; `/projects/[slug]/details` is the owner's **configuration** (the setup surface moved here unchanged, frozen terms still drawn by #89's locks; a non-owner 303s to the Overview); `/preview` keeps its template and gains the publish-state gate (a non-owner 303s straight to `/view/[slug]?type=projects`, not to the root, which would redirect them a second time). Redirects: `/edit` 308 → `/details` (it pointed at the root), a new static `/settings` 308 → `/details`, and `discussion/details` 303 → `/details` for the owner and the Overview for anyone else. Every role- or state-dependent redirect is a **303**, never a cacheable 308. One `[projectSlug]/_middleware.ts` sends a prospect on ANY workspace path to the listing (GET/HEAD; `/preview` keeps its own two-sided guard), stores the resolved access on `ctx.state.projectAccess` so the page and both band resolvers read one answer per request, and gives every workspace page a real tab title ("Board · <project> · Projective") — they all said "Projective". **(C) THE OVERVIEW** (`components/overview/ProjectOverviewScreen.tsx`, replacing `ProjectMemberDashboard`) is composed by `ProjectBackendService.workspace(slug, actor, viewer)` from reads that already have live and fixture branches — the overview (hero · updates · rooms · assignments · finance), the board (stage run, ticket states), the roster (people, open applications) and, for the owner, the setup (stage prices) — through the pure `composeWorkspace` (`@projective/types/projects/workspace.ts`). No new SQL, and the base `overview()` keeps its single read for link previews and `/api/projects/overview`. It renders **Needs you** (owner: review submissions · decide applications · price stages, the last counted with the ladder's own `pricedStages`; participant: revise returned work · deliver claimed work), the **stage run** on one hairline track with outline rings exactly as `/view`'s ledger draws them (§D.8.1 — a filled disc reads as a button; the ring's colour carries in progress / delivered), the state in words only when the run holds more than one, the owner seeing each stage's price and a participant seeing ticket counts — and room links — on their own stages only (the overview's viewer-pertinence rule), then Recent updates, Messages and People; a participant also keeps Your work and Your earnings, and on a narrow container the price drops under the facts. A draft's participant and a closed project get a one-line notice. **The owner gets no money block**: the live overview finance is `neutralFinance` (all zeros), and an owner reading "$0 in escrow" on a funded project would be a false statement. **(D) BANDS.** Overview header: the engagement's name and the owner's Details and (when allowed) Preview links — the lifecycle status is NOT repeated there, the hero already spends the surface's one fill on it (§B.11). Overview footer: one filled control — the top Needs-you action, else Open discussion — and one outlined (owner: Invite people; participant: Open discussion when the filled one is an action), all `<a href>` in the button classes so the rig needs no island; on a phone-width band the outlined one steps aside so the filled label reads whole. **No footer kebab**: the lane header's More actions already offers Open · Share · Archive (#140), and an action gets one home. On `/details` the Details ⇄ Preview pair links `/details`, and a PUBLISHED project's completeness ring is replaced by its outstanding fixes in words ("3 stages need a price"), a control that jumps to the first — a live engagement is not 86% of anything. **(E) LANE.** The top tier leads with **Overview** — left out only on the owner's draft, whose root forwards to Details (the link would have pointed back at the page it was pressed on). The Task's Details link and the lane footer's Details utility point at `/details` and render for the owner only (a participant's "Project details" was the root, which is now the Overview the top tier already holds), and never twice on a Task; the collapsed rail follows. The context card's name is a paragraph, not an `<h1>` (the page owns the only one), token-sized at `--fw-semibold`; its "Show details" link (an `href` to the root) becomes an in-place disclosure, drawn only while the text is actually clamped. **(F) AUDIT FIXES.** Quill's Tab bindings (`tab` · `indent` · `outdent` · `remove tab` · the code-block pair) are disabled in `RichTextEditor`: `indent` is not an allowed format, so they trapped Tab and, with auto-save on, wrote `\t` into a stage's scope; Tab and Shift+Tab now move focus. An unresolvable room ref (`/projects/[slug]/anything`) 303s to the Overview with a `room-not-found` notice instead of rendering an empty room with a live composer (`core/room-guard.ts`, the same `resolveChannelMeta` rule that titles the header band). `PageCanvas` renders the canvas as the page's one `<main id="main-content">` and `AppShell` takes `skipTo`, so the user shell's first Tab stop is a Skip to content link; the wallet's and Explore's own `<main>` elements, which would now have nested, became a named `<section>` and a `<div>`. The setup stage editor drops its four-sided hairline and its default fill: closed stages are list rows separated by one hairline, the open stage is the one `--surface-1` region, a role row stays tinted (§B.4, §B.9.3). `PRODUCT_SPEC.md` (§Configuration and the sitemap), `ROUTING.md`, `flows/Projects.md` §1.2–§1.3 and `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` (§C.1 AppShell · PageCanvas · RichTextEditor; the Project Details lane) are updated with it. **(G) DEV CONTEXT (§5).** `projectAccess` (auto · owner · participant · prospect) and `projectStatus` (auto · the five statuses) in `DevOverrides` / `DEV_DEFAULTS` / `DEV_PROJECT_ACCESSES` / `DEV_PROJECT_STATUSES` / the panel's "Project landing" group / `reflect()` (set and delete of `data-dev-project-access` / `-status`). Because the dispatch is SERVER-side, both are mirrored into a `pj.dev.landing` cookie (`core/landing-sim.ts`) that `resolveProjectAccess` honours only under `IS_DEV`, and the control reloads the page. **NOT DONE, deliberately:** the audit's restored focus ring (commit `152a27a` "Remove tab focus" set `outline: none` in 189 places across 182 files — a deliberate owner choice that a base-rule revert would not even undo); moving the setup Sections column into the header band (the band cannot hold title · mode pair · fixes · five sections at tablet width, and with setup off the landing the column is an ordinary settings index); the members caption ("2 people" over one row for a guest) is BY DESIGN (`total` is the unfiltered team size, `live-members.ts` `visibleTo`), and prospects no longer reach the page. One audit correction: the setup canvas's 32px/700 `h1` is the visually-hidden page title (UA defaults on a clipped element), not a visible weight violation. **VERIFIED** on the live local database (Playwright, writes blocked at the network layer): owner on `prj-6ndtxa9u9x` → the Overview (one `h1`, 36px / 500; one `<main>`; Needs you "Decide on an application" → `members?view=requests` and "Price 3 stages" → `details#psu-stages`; six stages with prices and three "Needs a price"; footer Invite people + Decide on an application; no Apply anywhere); `/details` 200 with "3 stages need a price" and an unlocked Preview; `/preview` 200 (it 303'd before); `/edit` and `/settings` 308 → `/details`; `/overview` and an unknown ref 303 → the Overview with the room-not-found toast, parameter stripped; `discussion/details` 303 → `/details`; the draft Task's root 303 → `/details`, ring kept, no Overview link in its lane or rail. `marisdelacroix` → the participant Overview ("Submit your work" for her in-progress ticket; only Discussion and research linked and counted; no prices; footer Submit your work + Open discussion), `/details` 303 → the Overview, `/preview` 303 → the listing — which also proves `has_project_access` answers for `authenticated`. `juno` → every workspace path (root, board, files, members, discussion, submissions, details, preview, a stage, an unknown ref) 303 → `/view/prj-6ndtxa9u9x?type=projects`. Tab leaves the scope editor with no `\t` written and the draft still saved; the first Tab on the page is Skip to content and Enter lands focus on `<main>`; the lane disclosure toggles `aria-expanded` in place. The Dev Context panel's Prospect control wrote the cookie and the attribute, reloaded and was sent to the listing; simulated draft / participant / completed render their dispatch and notices; switching simulation off cleared both. 375px: no horizontal overflow, prices under the facts, one whole footer button; dark mode reads. No console or server errors. `deno task check` and `deno task lint` (1,970 files) clean; `deno task test:unit` 2,205 passed with only the four pre-existing `theme-engine` failures (#96(a)), including 9 new access tests, 4 landing-sim codec tests, 4 live `viewerAccess` tests against the PostgREST fake, and 7 composition tests asserting that the fixture corpus REACHES every Needs-you kind and that prices and foreign ticket counts never reach a participant. **FLAGGED — for a human:** (a) the focus ring (above) is the owner's call; (b) "Revise returned work" can never appear on the LIVE path — `live-board.ts` returns every card's `activity` as `NO_ACTIVITY_SIGNAL` (null) — so it is reachable in fixtures only until the live board derives the revision signal; (c) the live overview finance is still `neutralFinance`, so a participant's Your earnings reads £0 on the live path (pre-existing) and the owner gets no money block until a real owner books read exists — a finance decision; (d) an invitee resolves as a prospect and is sent to the listing; where an invitation is answered (notifications, Members → Invitations) was not re-confirmed; (e) a client-side participant with role `client` / `admin` / `manager` is treated as the owner (it is `viewerIsClient`), so they reach `/details` — confirm that enterprise admins who did not create the project should configure it; (f) a participant on a draft (a staged seat, #139) gets the Overview with a notice rather than the listing; (g) the Overview composes four reads (overview, board, roster, setup) per load — cached per tenant on the live path, but heavier than the one read it replaced; (h) every edited doc and four touched source files were already `deno fmt`-dirty at HEAD (tables, CRLF), so only the new lines were formatted. | `PRODUCT_SPEC.md` §Configuration + sitemap · `ROUTING.md` · `flows/Projects.md` §1.2–§1.3 · `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §C.1 + the Project Details lane · `packages/types/projects/{access,workspace,detail,mod}.ts` (+ `access.test.ts`) · `packages/backend/services/projects/{ProjectBackendService,live-detail,detail-fixtures,write-store}.ts` (+ `workspace.test.ts`, `live-detail.test.ts`) · `apps/web/routes/(dashboard)/{_layout.tsx,projects/[projectSlug]/{_middleware.ts,index.tsx,details.tsx,settings.tsx,edit.tsx,preview.tsx,[channelId]/{_middleware.ts,details.tsx}}}` · `apps/web/features/projects/{components/overview/*,components/{ProjectContextCard,ProjectRail,detail-glyphs}.tsx,islands/{ProjectSidebar,ProjectSetupHeader,ProjectPageStyleAnchor}.island.tsx,core/{project-access,landing-sim,room-guard,project-header-slot,project-footer-slot,overview-ssr,project-notice}.ts,styles/{project-dashboard,project-setup,project-sidebar}.css}` (deleted: `components/dashboard/ProjectMemberDashboard.tsx`, `islands/ProjectPreviewRig.island.tsx`, `styles/project-preview-rig.css`) · `apps/web/features/devtools/{core/dev-context.ts,components/DevContextPanel.tsx}` · `apps/web/utils/state.ts` · `packages/ui/{editor/islands/RichTextEditor.tsx,navigation/{components/{AppShell,PageCanvas}.tsx,mod.ts,styles/app-shell.css}}` · `apps/web/features/{shell/components/UserShell.tsx,wallet/islands/WalletHome.island.tsx,explore/islands/SearchDashboard.island.tsx}` · Decisions #84 / #89 / #133 / #134 / #139 / #140 / #142 |
| 145 | **Stage memberships, delegated staffing, stage invite links, and one share/invite layout (2026-10-06). REFINES Decisions #102 (the share modal's people picker), #136 (the Members tab explorer) and #139 (stage seats); EXTENDS #116 / #128 / #141.** From the product owner's feature brief "Stage Memberships, Invitations/Requests Filtering, & Redesigned Share/Invite Modal" and their answers to four questions on it. **(A) A STAGE'S ROSTER IS THE PEOPLE WHO CAN ACT ON IT.** Being on a project lets a person view its stages; it does not make them a member of each. On a stage channel `stageAssignmentOf(role, assignedHere)` (`@projective/types/projects/members.ts`) answers `contributor` for an assignee, `observer` for unassigned oversight (`OVERSIGHT_ROLES`: client · owner · admin · manager) and `null` for anyone else, who is left off the stage's roster — live (`live-members.ts`), fixtures (`members-fixtures.ts`, whose `withAssignment` had made EVERY freelancer and member a contributor on EVERY stage — fixed) and the stub overlay (`write-store.ts`: a joined member or a role change re-derives through the same rule). `total` on a stage page is now the stage's count. The previous intended behaviour (every project participant present on every stage, unassigned as `observer`, pinned by a test) is reversed at the owner's direction; observers remain the default VIEW access a project participant has, not a roster seat. Invitations and Requests were already stage-scoped (`invitesForScope` / `requestsForScope`); the brief's `pending_invite` / `pending_request` statuses are not introduced — the three lists keep their own schemas. **(B) STAFFING CAN BE DELEGATED — THE DATABASE WIDENED.** `projects.can_manage_project_members` (`00001100` §2): review authority, or a person-participant whose role is `admin`/`manager`. It gates `invite_to_project`, `invite_by_email`, `assign_from_application`, `reject_application`, the new `act_on_invitation` and the invite-link doors, and is the SELECT arm on `project_invitations`, `project_applications`, `project_application_targets` and `stage_invite_links`. Offering the `admin`/`manager` role stays `can_review_project` (a delegate cannot appoint a peer; the Invite modal hides those roles from a delegate), and neither door may target the owner. Cancel/dismiss moved from a direct owner-RLS UPDATE to the definer door `projects.act_on_invitation` (`00001135` §3), because a delegate holding UPDATE could rewrite a row's role or token; the owner-only `Owner manages invitations` write policy is unchanged. This closes the audit's finding that admins and managers were shown Invite and an Invitations tab the database refused. **(C) A STAGE INVITE LINK ASKS; IT NEVER SEATS.** New table `projects.stage_invite_links` (`active → revoked`, one active per stage — `uq_stage_invite_links_active`; 18-byte base64url token; `revoked_at`/`revoked_by`) and `project_applications.invite_link_id` (provenance), edited in place in `00000015`. Doors (`00001135` §4): `get_stage_invite_link(stage, rotate)` (mint on first use; reset revokes then mints in one transaction), `revoke_stage_invite_link`, `resolve_invite_link(token)` (the landing read; `{state:"invalid"}` for an unknown token) and `redeem_invite_link(token, message)`, which files the same pending application `apply_to_project` files — answered from Requests — without requiring a public listing or an open hire trigger, since the link is the managers' own invitation to ask. `fn_invite_link_state` is the ONE rule both doors read (revoked · closed · manager · member · invited · requested · no_profile · open), so the page never offers what the redeem refuses. Every mint/reset/revoke is audited. Verified in a rolled-back transaction against the local database (a manager mints, resets, revokes, invites a freelancer, cancels, reads and declines a request; cannot appoint a manager; a plain freelancer is refused; a stranger redeems once, is refused twice and on a revoked link). Routes: `GET|POST /api/projects/[id]/stages/[stageId]/invite-link` (GET never mints — reopening the modal after turning a link off does not turn it back on), `POST /api/projects/invite-links/redeem`, and the page `/invite/[token]` inside `(dashboard)` (a guest signs in and returns via `redirectTo`; `noindex`/`no-referrer`/`no-store`). Fat service `InviteLinkService` (live: `live-invite-links.ts`; stub: `invite-link-store.ts`, whose requests join the stub roster's Requests marked `viaInviteLink`). **(D) ONE SHARE/INVITE LAYOUT.** Three shared pieces in `features/share`: `ShareLinkBar` (link · Copy · shortcuts · the device sheet where `navigator.share` exists), `UserSearchPopover` (a WAI-ARIA combobox — 250 ms debounce into the ranked `/api/messaging/suggestions` read, an anchored body-portalled listbox, ArrowUp/Down/Enter/Escape, Escape owned while top so the Dialog stays open, an email row for an address) and `QuickAddRail` (a scroll-snapped rail of ranked contacts with a one-click action and idle/busy/done/blocked states, `people-picker.ts`). The Members tab's `MemberInviteModal` is replaced by `InviteShareModal` (role · stage · the stage's link · search · Quick add; members are never suggested; an open invitation to the chosen stage reads _Invited_; each address is sent alone through the roster's `onInvite`, so one refusal holds nothing else back and the person lands in Invitations at once). `ShareModalBody` now uses the same three pieces: the multi-select picker + single Send (#102) becomes one-click Send per person with an optional note that rides every send. LinkedIn and Email join the share targets (`INVITE_SHARE_TARGETS`: WhatsApp · LinkedIn · X · Email). The brief's "context badge" renders as inline meta text (`@handle · Following`) under §3 gate 7 (anti-tagification); rail people are spaced, not carded, inside the Dialog (gate 6). **FLAGS for a human:** (a) the brief's "connected contacts / address book" tier does not exist — Quick add uses the server's relationship ranking (shared workspace · mutual follow · follow · worked together · messaged); no phone/email address book is imported. (b) Workspace invitations cut join requests and share links on 2026-09-28 (PRODUCT_MANAGEMENT §3.5); stage invite links are a different domain and do not reverse that — confirm the two are meant to differ. (c) `remove_project_member` and `set_member_role` stay review authority while live `capsFor` still gives admins and managers `canRemove`/`canEditRoles` — those two controls are still offered then refused (pre-existing; out of this change's scope). (d) A link request notifies the owner only (as `apply_to_project` does), not the admins and managers who can now answer it. (e) The local database is not migrated — run `deno task db:reset`. (f) `showStages` stays pipeline-only (#139 flag); a one-off's link is offered on its stage page or when it has a single stage. (g) Stub link requests name the requester by `@handle` with no avatar. |
| 146 | **Project lane activity marks — every top-tier view carries a per-viewer mark of what changed since that viewer last opened it; Discussion, Submissions and Members print the figure as plain text, the rest a toned dot; opening a view clears it; the Overview highlights the regions that changed; the project-wide room is named "Discussion" at its source (2026-10-06). AMENDS DESIGN_SYSTEM §D.1 / §E dense-list rule 2 ("a dot, never a count") for the project lane's top tier only, at the product owner's direction.** The owner chose figures "as text playing with the font, not containerised" where the number is what the reader acts on (unread messages by others, submissions awaiting the viewer, membership changes) and dots everywhere else for consistency; figures cap at `9+`, are `tabular-nums` `--fw-semibold` `--primary` text with no fill or border (gate 7 holds: no container), and fold into the link's accessible name ("Discussion — 1 unread message"); the collapsed rail keeps a dot and carries the figure in its tooltip. Dot tones: accent (new), neutral `--text-secondary` (Board cards moved), `--warning` (Timeline deadline within 48 h). **Read state is server-side** (owner's choice over localStorage): `projects.view_reads` (user · project · `lane_view` CHECK · `seen_at`, own-rows RLS whose WITH CHECK also requires `has_project_access`), `projects.get_nav_activity(slug)` and `projects.mark_view_seen(slug, view)` — both SECURITY INVOKER in `00001145`, so RLS is what separates the roles (a reviewer counts `pending_review` work, a submitter counts verdicts; only the staffing side can read applications and invitations). `projects.fn_touch_updated_at` + `trg_projects_touch_updated_at` make `projects.projects.updated_at` honour its documented "any write touches it". Web: `ProjectNavActivityService` (never cached, quiet on failure), `POST /api/projects/nav-seen`, `resolveNavActivity` memoised on `state.projectNavActivity` so the Overview handler and the lane share one read; `projectViewLinks(detail, activity, kind)` is the one status source for the top tier and the rail (the rail now takes the island's link set); the current view never draws its own mark and the sidebar records the arrival once per page; `OverviewArrival` adds `.pjd-arrival` to `[data-pjd-region]` (title · status line · stage run) for `--dur-highlight` (new token, 1800 ms, 0 under reduced motion) and removes it on `animationend` or the timer. Verified on the live DB as owner and freelancer (counts, dots, clearing, role split, highlight mount/unmount in both motion modes, Files copy) and in a rolled-back SQL probe (RLS forgery refused, anon refused, unknown view refused). **FLAGS for a human:** (a) the Overview "details" signal reads `projects.updated_at`, which any write to the row now touches — title, description and settings cannot be told apart, and a status change also marks "details". (b) Board "new" reads `tickets.created_at` against `owner_user_id` (no `created` history row exists); a ticket the viewer created with no owner marks their own Board. (c) Members counts arrivals, pending applications and invitation outcomes; an accepted invitation surfaces as the arrival, a revoked one not at all, and expiry is read from `expires_at` because nothing flips the status. (d) A view never opened uses the viewer's first mark on the engagement (else now) as its baseline, so history before a first visit is never marked — a newcomer's Discussion starts at 0. (e) Calendar (session archetype) and Details carry no mark; the spec named neither. (f) No Dev Context Switcher axis was added: marks are computed for the real viewer and nothing new is simulated. (g) The local database was given the additive pieces by hand during verification; run `deno task db:reset` for a clean rebuild. |
| 147 | **Contact & financial leakage prevention — a three-tier pipeline (in-process edge pre-filter → `comms.mask_pii` database triggers → self-hosted Presidio NLP) for protected-phase messaging, and a permanent prohibition of contact and payment details in public copy that the Projective Unlock never lifts (2026-10-06). EXTENDS PRODUCT_SPEC §Messaging 1 "Platform Integrity" and §3 "The Handover State"; documentation only — no migration, no Zod change.** From the product owner's brief "Anti-Disintermediation / Contact & Financial Leakage Prevention". **(A) THREE TIERS, ONE AUTHORITY.** Tier 2 — the shipped `BEFORE INSERT` triggers `comms.tg_mask_message_pii` / `comms.tg_mask_dm_message_pii` over the `IMMUTABLE` `comms.mask_pii` (`00001300` / `00001840`) — is the floor; Tier 1 (in-process in the fat send services: confusable folding, `linkify-it` / `tldts` URL and registrable-domain extraction, Luhn on 13–19 digit runs; refuses with `422`) and Tier 3 (a Dockerised Microsoft Presidio analyzer, spaCy NER + custom recognisers, 2 GB RAM, ~$10–15/month, fed after commit by a worker; never edits or delays a message, writes only review-queue evidence) may add detection but never relax it. Any category Tier 1 adds must land in `comms.mask_pii` in the same change. Tiers 1 and 3 are **planned**; today Tier 1 is the composer's advisory twin `packages/types/comms/pii.ts`. **(B) ZERO-SAAS.** Sift, Hive Moderation and peers rejected: per-message pricing on the busiest surface, every protected-phase body shipped to a third-party processor, and synchronous review on the send path. **(C) PUBLIC SURFACES ARE NEVER UNLOCKED.** `org.users_public.headline` / `bio`, `marketplace.service_blueprints` copy, `catalogue.products` / `catalogue.listings` copy and `projects.projects.title` / `description` may never carry banking or card details, off-platform payment links or handles, or direct-contact redirects, regardless of `handover_unlocked_at`; checked at create/update in each owning write door and **refused** field-named (`422`), never masked. **Planned — no check exists yet.** **FLAGS for a human:** (a) **CONTRADICTION — structured links.** The brief bars off-platform redirection links in public copy "at all times", while `org.profile_links` stores social/portfolio links (`github`, `linkedin`, …) for both profile types, `org.enterprises.website` stores a corporate domain, and article bodies are free text; the docs scope the rule to free-text copy and leave these three undecided. (b) Tier 1 **refuses** where the shipped contract **masks and stores**: a refused message leaves no `pii_masked` row, so the evidence the Consequences ladder reads is lost unless refusals are logged — confirm the refusal UX and whether refusals are recorded. (c) **LIVE GAP — edits bypass Tier 2.** Both mask triggers are `INSERT`-only; `comms.project_messages` carries `edit_own_messages` (`00002012`, no column restriction) under `GRANT ALL` on `comms`, so a sender can rewrite a stored body — or `pii_masked` — after masking. Fix needs a migration (`BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF body` on the mask trigger and a guard pinning `pii_masked` / `pii_categories`); `comms.dm_messages` has no `UPDATE` policy. (d) **CONTRADICTION — shadow-banning.** The brief has Tier 3 feed "shadow-banning"; PRODUCT_SPEC §Notifications rule 1 makes moderation decisions mandatory notifications, which a silent restriction violates. Docs route Tier 3 to manual review only. (e) Tier 2's `phone` pass masks every 8+ character digit run, so a card number is masked as `phone` and a long numeric reference is masked too — the false positive Luhn is meant to avoid persists at the authoritative tier until `comms.mask_pii`, `pii.ts` and `pii.contract.test.ts` change together. (f) Brief vocabulary corrected to the schema: `org.users_public` has `bio` (jsonb `{text}`), not `description`; `comms.mask_pii` is a pure function called by two trigger functions; `[… hidden]` is four category placeholders. (g) Tier 3's candidate queue, review-queue table, worker and analyzer environment key are undesigned; each lands with its migration, Zod schema, database doc and Environment Variable Contract line, and PRODUCT_SPEC §Data Privacy should name the analyzer once it processes message bodies. | `PRODUCT_SPEC.md` §Messaging 1.A / 1.B · `SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE.md` §Messaging services → Contact & financial leakage prevention · `documentation/database/{comms/{Functions,Tables},org/Tables,catalogue/Tables,marketplace/Tables}.md` · Decisions #128 / #145 |
| 148 | **Content moderation — the Two-Zone model: a proactive Public Glass Front (profiles, showcase, catalogue, service cards, public project briefs) screened before publication, and a reactive Private Collaborative Workspace (channels, DMs, deliverables, private files) moderated only from user reports under a creative safe harbor; verified violations feed `security.penalties` → `org.fn_recompute_standing` (2026-10-06). EXTENDS PRODUCT_SPEC §Messaging 1 (new 1.C) and Decision #147; documentation only — no migration, no Zod change.** From the product owner's brief "Content Moderation Architecture and Tiered Safety Lifecycle". **(A) THE GATE SITS AT PUBLICATION, NOT IN QUARANTINE — corrected from the brief.** The brief places the classifier in the `quarantine` validation step "if the file's target bucket is public"; no upload targets a public bucket — quarantine always admits into a private library (`personal` / `workspace`) and a public object is only ever a later rendition (`MediaBackendService` → `avatars` / `showcase`). Screening in quarantine would scan the private zone the model exempts, so the gate runs on the decoded pixels at the publish step, before the WebP tiers. **(B) A FLAGGED IMAGE IS REFUSED, NOT `quarantined` — corrected from the brief.** Marking the library original `quarantined` would hide the owner's private file from every read (`status = 'uploaded'` only), punishing private use the safe harbor allows; the publish answers `422` and a correction notice is sent instead, and `quarantined` stays reserved for hostile bytes. Classifier unavailable → fail closed (`503`). **(C) STACK.** Self-hosted CPU container: NudeNet (nudity) + the Decision #147 Presidio service (Image Redactor OCR for contact/payment details in images); OpenAI moderation / Sightengine as an optional, off-by-default fallback that only ever sees public-bound images. **(D) REPORTING.** `POST /api/reports` → a reports fat service that checks the reporter can read the subject under their own JWT and builds the `evidence_snapshot` server-side; `ops.reports` (`pending` · `dismissed` · `actioned`); review by `ops.admin_users`; `actioned` → `security.penalties` → `org.fn_recompute_standing` → possible `demoted` event; mandatory notification. **(E) SAFE HARBOR BOUNDS** — stated by this pass, confirm: never covers illegal content (incl. any sexual content involving minors), harassment or threats at a participant, or explicit material sent to someone not engaged on the work. **FLAGS for a human:** (a) **`ops.reports` is not migrated.** The brief asks for its schema in `documentation/database/ops/Tables.md`, but `documentation/database/CLAUDE.md` forbids documenting unmigrated schema there; the proposed shape is in SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE and the ops doc carries a pointer only. Landing it needs the migration (in place in `00000013`), RLS (reporter reads own rows, admins all), a definer resolution door, the Zod schema and the docs together. (b) **LIVE GAP — two public buckets accept direct client writes.** `"Owners can write their public assets"` and `"Sellers can write their catalogue media"` (`00002017`) are `FOR ALL TO authenticated` on `auth.uid() = owner`, so any signed-in user can PUT an unscanned, unscreened file into `public_assets` / `catalogue`, bypassing quarantine and any gate — the hole closed for `avatars` on 2026-09-22. (c) **NOT WIRED — penalties do not reach Standing.** `org.fn_recompute_standing` subtracts `org.entity_standing.penalty_severity`, and `org/Tables.md` calls that column the "active `security.penalties` aggregate", but nothing writes it: it stays at `DEFAULT 0`. An aggregation (trigger on `security.penalties` or a step inside the recompute) is required before any penalty — report, dispute or PII — can demote a rung. (d) `security.penalties.source_type` CHECK has `workload_report` · `dispute` · `no_show` · `pii_violation` · `manual`; a report-sourced penalty needs a new value (e.g. `content_report`), added in place in `00000012`. (e) The brief's public bucket list omits `showcase`, which is public and carries full-length video; it is in the public zone here, and video screening is poster + sampled frames only. (f) The private zone stays under the Decision #147 contact filter — leakage masking is platform integrity, not content moderation, so "no automated screening" means no explicit-content screening. (g) The correction-notice notification type, the report reason vocabulary and per-category thresholds are undesigned. | `PRODUCT_SPEC.md` §Messaging 1.C · `SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE.md` §Storage Lifecycle step 4 · §Backend Services → Content moderation · `documentation/database/{files/Storage,ops/Tables}.md` · Decisions #147 / #84 |
| 149 | **The header account popover rebuilt — a profile-completion ring with a derived presence pip, an "Acting as" row over a person · Teams · Businesses · Enterprises switcher, a go-live setup nudge, light ⁄ dark ⁄ system theme, and display currency moved out (2026-10-06). AMENDS Decision #69 (the header currency switcher) and #47(C); SUPERSEDES the 2026-07-23 manual status picker.** At the product owner's direction. **(A) Completeness is facts + one rule.** `GET /api/user/setup` → `UserBackendService.setup` returns `AccountSetup` (`@projective/types/user/profile-setup.ts`): the PERSON's handle (from `org.get_party_cards` — `UserContext.handle` is the ENTITY's slug while acting as one) and FACTS read through the doors other surfaces already trust — `org.get_profile_view` (uploaded photo, headline, story, skill count, published hours, Standing) and `finance.my_verification_status` (`payout_ready`). It never ships a percentage; `calculateProfileCompleteness` (pure, unit-tested) is the one rule: a seller's steps are photo · headline + story · ≥ `GO_LIVE_MIN_SKILLS` (3) skills · payout · published hours, the first three being the go-live baseline; a buyer's are photo · headline + story · hours, with no go-live. A fact the server cannot check (finance not live → `payoutReady: null`) drops its step from the count instead of counting it either way, and an unreadable setup is `setup: null` → NO ring, never a ring at 0%. **(B) The ring.** `ProfileProgressAvatar` (`features/shell/components`): two circles on `pathLength=100` so `stroke-dashoffset` is the missing share; `--primary` → `--success` at 100%; `--ease-standard` offset transition, removed under both reduced-motion channels; `--outline` track under high contrast; `aria-hidden`, because the trigger's label speaks it ("Your account, profile 80% set up, away") and the nudge is the real `progressbar`. The box is the avatar plus `--space-1` a side in `rem`, so the ring scales with text zoom, and it is the same size with or without data — no layout shift when the read lands. **(C) Presence is derived, never asserted** (`PRODUCT_SPEC.md` — availability "derived, never asserted"): the pip and the identity block's presence line come from the PUBLISHED working hours and the clock through the profile's own `availabilityAt` / `hoursSummary`, re-derived each minute; the line links to `/[handle]/edit/availability` and its `Tooltip` is the schedule ("Mon–Fri, 8:30 AM – 4:30 PM · GMT+1"). Available = solid `--success`, Away = hollow `--warning` ring (hue AND fill, for the CVD overlays). The Online ⁄ Away ⁄ DND ⁄ Invisible picker and `LocalKeys.ACCOUNT_STATUS` are deleted — no presence service exists, and a hand-set pip could contradict the calendar. **(D) Acting as + switcher.** The identity block's `⇄` icon becomes an "Acting as · Personal · Freelancer ⁄ Team · Name ⁄ …" row (the panel's one tonal fill — it is a control) opening a sectioned switcher: the person, Teams (gated like the sidebar — freelancer or team context), Businesses, and Enterprises from a new `GET /api/context/enterprises` → `ContextBackendService.enterprises` (owned, or an ACTIVE membership; archived excluded; filtered explicitly because the `org.enterprises` policy also admits platform admins to every row; `ActingEnterpriseSchema` in `@projective/types/workspace`); an enterprise switch lands on `/{handle}`. `UserBackendService.me` now names the acting entity by its display name (RLS read of `org.teams` / `org.business_profiles` / `org.enterprises`) instead of its slug. **(E) Structure** (`label="Account"` → a named `dialog`, so the stray `role="menuitem"`s outside any `menu` are gone; first focus on the Acting-as row so the presence tooltip never opens on every open; view changes move focus to the new view's entry control): identity → acting as → setup nudge (below 100%: "Profile setup 60% · 2 steps left", "to go live" while a seller's baseline is unmet, a slim `ProgressBar` and an inline checklist disclosure linking each pending step — `/[handle]/edit`, `/settings/verification`, `/[handle]/edit/availability`) → View profile · Become a Freelancer (buyer context; now `/become-partner` per the spec, replacing the 2026-07-23 `/settings` stub) · Availability & hours · Wallet & payouts · Settings → footer: an icon-only theme `Button` and Log out (`Button` text ⁄ danger). The role badge and email are removed (the Acting-as row carries the persona; Standing is inline meta text, never a badge, §B.11). **(F) Theme preference.** `@projective/ui/system` gains `ThemePreference` (`light` · `dark` · `system`), the `themePreference` signal and `setThemePreference` / `cycleThemePreference`; `system` removes the stored `"theme"` key (the inline pre-paint script already reads absence as "follow the OS") and `bindRootTheme` repaints on a live `prefers-color-scheme` change while the preference is `system`. `setMode` / `toggleMode` keep working and now also record the preference. **(G) Dev Context Switcher:** new `profileSetup` axis (`auto · new · live · complete`; `data-dev-profile-setup`, absent = auto) substitutes FACTS — the shipping rule still decides the percentage — and `complete` publishes Mon–Fri 9:00–17:30 in the browser's zone so the pip has a schedule; `EffectiveContext` carries it. **(H)** `.ui-popover` now honours `:root[data-motion="reduced"]` as well as the OS query (no slide, no fade). **Verified** live on the local stack with minted local test sessions (juno: 80%, "1 step left" — payout, Away with Lisbon hours; theo: buyer 67%, no pip, Become a Freelancer; all four dev positions; mobile sheet; theme cycle; Escape returns focus to the trigger; zero console ⁄ server errors), the enterprise filter's PostgREST syntax against the real API, and its membership semantics in a rolled-back transaction (the seed has no enterprises). **Flagged (surface, do not silently resolve):** (a) **Display currency leaves the popover**, reversing #69's header switcher at the owner's direction; it stays switchable from the wallet hero tools and the checkout stepper, but `/settings` is still a placeholder, so a viewer must visit `/wallet` to change it — decide whether `/settings` gets a Preferences section. (b) The brief's "L3 · Trusted" mixes two ladders — the `L1`–`L3`⁄Architect VERIFICATION tier and the earned STANDING rung; only the rung is shown ("Trusted standing"). (c) `GO_LIVE_MIN_SKILLS = 3` is new: the spec said "skills" with no count, and NO server-side go-live gate exists — publishing, premium services and workspace applications do not yet check this milestone anywhere; the popover is its only reader. (d) The brief asked for the popover's transform on `--ease-standard`; the shared `.ui-popover` keeps `--spring-standard` (over-damped, no overshoot — §B.5 assigns springs to menus) rather than diverging one popover from the rest. (e) The theme preference is device-local (`localStorage`); `org.user_preferences.theme` exists and is still not read or written by any theme control (cf. #74(d)). (f) `/api/user/setup` adds one request (three RPCs) per authenticated hydration; cacheable later if it shows up in timings. (g) No tooltip on the HEADER avatar — it would sit over the popover it opens; the schedule tooltip lives on the popover's presence line and the trigger's label speaks presence. (h) `/become-partner` is still a `PagePlaceholder`. | `PRODUCT_SPEC.md` §Additive, Unlockable Personas · `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §A.2 (mode vs preference) · §C.1 · Part D.1 · Part D.3 · `SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE.md` §Auth (account popover) · §acting context · `ROUTING.md` (`/api/user/setup`, `/api/context/enterprises`) · `packages/types/user/profile-setup.ts` · `packages/types/workspace/workspace.ts` · `packages/backend/services/{user/UserBackendService,context/ContextBackendService}.ts` · `packages/ui/system/{core/context,types/mod}.ts` · `packages/ui/feedback/styles/popover.css` · `apps/web/features/shell/{islands/UserActions.island,components/ProfileProgressAvatar,core/{account-setup,AccountService,effective-context,nav-icons}}` · `apps/web/routes/api/{user/setup,context/enterprises}.ts` · devtools `profileSetup` axis |
| 150 | **"Become a Partner" end to end, and four org-layer correctness/security fixes — the freelancer unlock goes through the one session-context writer, `org.enterprises` loses its bare UPDATE policy for `org.update_enterprise`, enterprise handles join the shared namespace, and the acting entity's name is read through a seat-checked definer (2026-10-06).** CLOSES Decision #149 flag (h). **(A) `org.enable_freelancer_profile` (00001020).** Step 3 upserted only `active_profile_*`, so a person acting as a team or enterprise held two slots and `ck_session_context_one_slot` failed the whole conversion; it now calls `security.fn_set_session_context('freelancer', uid, NULL, NULL, uid)`, which clears both slots (and audits `session.switch_context`). Starter skills are `org.skills` SLUGS — trimmed, lower-cased, de-duplicated in order, at most ten, every one known, else `22023`. It now returns the person's `handle`. `REVOKE … FROM PUBLIC, anon` + `GRANT … TO authenticated` are stated per function (00002510). **(B) `org.enterprises` has no client UPDATE policy.** The old policy had no column list and no WITH CHECK: any admin could rewrite `legal_name` · `registration_number` · `corporate_email` · `billing_email` over PostgREST, unvalidated and unaudited. `org.update_enterprise(p_enterprise_id, p_payload)` is the one door: owner, active admin member or platform admin; an allow-list of keys (platform keys → `42501`, unknown → `22023`, never ignored); every value bounded as `UpdateEnterpriseSchema` (`@projective/types/org`, `.partial().strict()`) states; the LEGAL identity (legal name · registration number · corporate email) owner-only and FROZEN while `verification_level` is `kyb_pending` or `verified` (`55000`); every edit audited `enterprise.updated`. `trg_enterprises_immutable` stays as defence in depth. **(C) `public.create_enterprise` (00001010)** trims and lower-cases the handle and runs `org.fn_handle_refusal` before the insert (taken → `23505`, otherwise `22023`, the probe's own sentence), so an enterprise can no longer take a person's `@username` or a team/business slug. Its EXECUTE is now `REVOKE`d from `PUBLIC`, `anon` and `authenticated` — it trusts `p_owner`, and as a `public` function it was callable by any signed-out client. **(D) `org.get_acting_context_details(p_context_type, p_context_id)`** answers `{ name, handle }` only for an entity the caller holds an active seat in (an enterprise's owner counts); `UserBackendService.me` reads the acting name through it, because `org.business_profiles` has no client SELECT policy and a business showed its slug. **(E) The funnel.** `@projective/types/user/freelancer-conversion.ts` (`EnableFreelancerInputSchema` 1–10 slugs, `FreelancerConversionResultSchema`, `StarterSkillOptionSchema`, `MAX_STARTER_SKILLS`) → `POST /api/user/freelancer` (thin; actor from `readActor`, never the body) → `UserBackendService.enableFreelancer(actor, input)` (takes the ReadActor, not a bare id: the RPC needs the caller's JWT; `28000` → 401, `42501` → 403, `22023` → 422 keyed to `skills`, else 503 — `freelancer-unlock.ts`, unit-tested) and `starterSkills()` (the taxonomy, anon read). `/become-partner` re-exports `features/profile/routes/BecomePartnerScreen.tsx`, which reads `UserBackendService.setup` (the stored seller fact + the person's handle) and the skills server-side; `BecomePartnerWizard.island.tsx` renders the pitch on the canvas, what unlocks as a type-carried list, and one solid `--surface-1` action region with an `aria-pressed` pill cluster (the only contours on the page) capped at ten, a live `n of 10` count, and "Unlock Freelancer Suite". It runs the `useContextSwitch` sequence (`partner-service.ts`): unlock → `POST /api/auth/refresh` → `location.assign('/[handle]/edit')`; a failed refresh is reported ("unlocked — reload to finish"), never navigated past. A person who already sells sees "Your freelancer suite is unlocked" and a link to their editor. The popover / nav / Create gates needed no change: they already read `isFreelancer` from the re-minted claims. The unlock grants no money capability — payouts still pass `finance.fn_freelancer_payout_ready` (the No Forever-Escrow gate, #125/#126). **Verified:** 16 assertions in a rolled-back SQL probe (team-context conversion, skill refusals, grants, the business name for a member and NULL for a stranger, three handle collisions, every `update_enterprise` refusal, a raw client UPDATE matching zero rows, the KYB freeze); then, with the owner's approval, the definitions applied to the shared local DB and a scripted Playwright journey as the seeded buyer `theo` (real session): 31 pills, three chosen, `POST /api/user/freelancer` 201 → `/api/auth/refresh` 200 → `/theo/edit`; the popover flips Client → Freelancer and drops "Become a Freelancer", the rail gains Services and Teams; revisiting shows the unlocked state; no overflow at 390px; zero console errors; theo then restored to buyer-only. 13 new unit tests. **Flagged (surface, do not silently resolve):** (a) The KYB legal-identity freeze and owner-only identity edits are NEW rules (recorded in PRODUCT_SPEC) — confirm, and decide how a verified enterprise corrects its legal name (support path?). (b) `org.update_enterprise` has no service, route or UI yet: enterprise settings do not exist. (c) A refused enterprise handle fails AFTER `AuthBackendService` admin-creates the owner's GoTrue identity, leaving it orphaned (already true for a duplicate enterprise handle; now also for a person's username) — the `/join` enterprise step has no availability probe; add one (`org.check_handle`) and/or clean up the identity. (d) Enterprise handles are now stored lower-case; mixed-case input that used to be stored verbatim is normalised (the seed has no enterprises). (e) The RPC still accepts an EMPTY skill list (idempotent re-activation); only the Zod schema requires one. (f) Converting while acting as an enterprise moves the person into their freelancer persona — intended by "immediately activates", but they leave the enterprise context. (g) The Playwright MCP browser was held by a peer session, so the journey ran as a scripted `playwright-core` run against the same dev server; the conversion left two `security.audit_logs` rows for theo (immutable, kept). (h) `setup` + `starterSkills` add four reads to the `/become-partner` render. | `PRODUCT_SPEC.md` §Additive, Unlockable Personas · §Enterprise onboarding · `SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE.md` §Auth (persona unlock, account popover) · `ROUTING.md` (`/api/user/freelancer`) · `database/org/{Functions,Policies}.md` · `supabase/migrations/{00001010,00001020,00001895,00002010,00002510}` · `packages/types/{user/freelancer-conversion,org/enterprises}.ts` · `packages/backend/services/user/{UserBackendService,freelancer-unlock}.ts` · `apps/web/routes/{(dashboard)/become-partner.tsx,api/user/freelancer.ts}` · `apps/web/features/profile/{routes/BecomePartnerScreen.tsx,islands/BecomePartnerWizard.island.tsx,core/partner-service.ts,styles/become-partner.css}` |
| 151 | **The two-phase Settings engine — one registry, a contextual modal any page opens (`?settings=<section>` · `openSettings()`), the `/settings` console with its lane and attention dashboard, appearance & accessibility stored per person and painted from the first byte (`pj.a11y`), and `org.user_emails` hardened behind a token handshake (2026-10-07). CLOSES Decision #149 flags (a) (display currency gets a home outside the wallet: Settings → Language & region) and (e) in part (the theme column is now written from Settings).** At the product owner's direction ("Phase 4 Settings"). **(A) One taxonomy.** `features/settings/core/settings-registry.ts` (pure, isomorphic, unit-tested) holds the eleven sections — keys owned by `@projective/types/settings` (`account · profile · workspaces · language · appearance · notifications · messaging · scheduling · billing · verification · integrations`) — and every searchable entry with its `anchor`, keywords, `surface` (`modal` · `page-only` · `status-escalate`) and an optional `gate(ctx)` read against the EFFECTIVE context. Search requires every word to match ("dark mode" → Appearance › Theme only); the modal's tree, the lane's tree, the dashboard's marks and the phone drill-down all read the registry. **(B) Phase 1 — the contextual modal.** `SettingsModalHost` (mounted in `UserShell`) owns `?settings=`: arriving opens it, a gear's `openSettings()` (the cancelable `pj:settings-open` event; unclaimed → navigate to the console) PUSHES one entry so Back dismisses it, moving between sections REPLACES it, closing strips the parameter and nothing else; every entry carries `fClientNav: false` (the #95 recipe — pushState is safe; DESIGN_SYSTEM §B.10.9(3)'s replace-only rule governs modal-STACK chains, which this is not). `Dialog` at `--overlay-w-xl` × `--overlay-h-lg` through `BodyPortal`, header "Settings · {section}" with Expand to full page (`location.replace(/settings/{section}#{anchor})`) and Close; body a `Splitter` (`stateKey="settings-modal-split"`, 28% · 20–40%) with `LaneSearch` + `TreeNav` beside the lazily imported section (the eleven sections and their CSS ship only on first open). Below a 40rem container the split becomes a drill-down. A `page-only` section (Scheduling) and the `status-escalate` ones (Verification, Integrations) show their status and ONE escalation ("Configure Full Schedule in Console →"), never hidden or disabled. Unsaved form drafts ride `SessionKeys.SETTINGS_DRAFTS`, so Expand loses nothing. **(C) Phase 2 — the console.** `/settings/[section]` (unknown → 303 `/settings/account`) and `/settings` (attention dashboard: identity check · payout account · business KYB · broken connectors · unconfirmed emails, derived from FACTS by the pure `attentionItems`; the phone drill-down below 767px) re-export controllers from `features/settings/routes/`; `settingsLaneFor` plugs into `(dashboard)/_layout.tsx` `laneFor` with toned section marks (glyph + word, never hue alone). One read per section (`resolveSettingsSection`) serves the page's SSR AND the modal's `GET /api/settings/[section]`. **(D) Keyboard.** `/` focuses the search unless typing (`isFocusSearchShortcut`); Enter opens the best match and moves focus INTO it; `TreeNav` gives arrows · Home/End · Enter/Space; `Cmd/Ctrl+K` stays the command palette's. **(E) Appearance & accessibility.** `org.user_preferences` gains `contrast` · `font` · `cvd` · `motion` (CHECKed; `theme` CHECKed to `system·light·dark`), edited in place in `00000011`. The `pj.a11y` cookie (`utils/a11y-context.ts`; also carries `dir`) is the per-device mirror the middleware reads and `_app.tsx` writes onto `<html>` — only overlays that are ON, so `standard` leaves the OS media queries in charge; `hydrateConfigFromDom` reads them back so hydration never repaints the defaults; `:root[data-motion="reduced"]` gets the global duration collapse; a pre-paint script restores a cleared cookie from `LocalKeys.A11Y_PREFERENCES`. `PATCH /api/user/preferences` (strict) splits display vs appearance in `SettingsBackendService` (separate statements, so an older database degrades Appearance to "this device only" rather than breaking currency) and answers `Set-Cookie: pj.a11y`. **(F) Sections.** Account (identity read-only with support links; emails add · resend · make primary · remove; password reset through GoTrue) · Profile (visibility + the three page switches through the profile editor's OWN route) · Workspaces (rosters + `useContextSwitch`) · Language (locale → session renewed; currency through `commitDisplayCurrency`; direction) · Appearance · Notifications (`GET/PUT /api/user/notifications`, `NotificationCenterBackendService`: four masters that reset their column (`resetChannels`), the 8 × 4 matrix, locked always-on rows summarised from the catalog's mandatory types, quiet hours, pause) · Messaging (the former `MessageSettingsModal`, deleted; `POST /api/messaging/settings` is now a LIVE write, the inverse of the reader; quiet hours / mute-all edited only under Notifications) · Scheduling (accepts calls, courtesy length · weekly cap · cooldown, buffers, notice, through `PUT /api/profile/[handle]/availability`; hours summarised and linked) · Billing (saved cards via `/api/cards`) · Verification / Integrations (status overviews; their static consoles render in the same lane). The inbox gear, the calendar's working-hours control (its read-only dialog deleted) and the wallet hero gear now call `openSettings("messaging" · "scheduling" · "verification")`. **(G) `org.user_emails` hardened.** The table had own-row INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE policies (UPDATE without WITH CHECK) under a blanket grant, so any user could forge `verified_at` and read invitations addressed to someone else's address. Now SELECT-only to clients; writes are definers in `00001050` (`get_my_emails` · `add_user_email` · `remove_user_email` · `set_primary_email` · `confirm_user_email`); the token mint `security.issue_email_verification` is service-role only, stores only SHA-256, TTL 24 h; an INVOKER guard trigger (`00001815`) backs it up; unique `(user_id, lower(email))` and one-primary indexes; `org.email_verification_tokens` (RLS, no policy). The link leaves through `services/mail/verification-mailer.ts` (no transport: dev logs it, production reports `unavailable`). Verified: 66 rolled-back probe checks; the shared local database was then rebuilt by another session and carries these definitions. **(H) Dev Context Switcher:** new `settingsAttention` axis (`auto · clear · all`; `data-dev-settings-attention`) substitutes the attention FACTS — the shipping rule still decides. **(I) `Dialog` gains `labelledBy`** (a templated header with controls is otherwise named by its whole region). **Verified live** (isolated Playwright 1.56 against the local stack, minted juno session): deep link opens on Messaging; an unsaved edit survives Expand to `/settings/messaging`; gear push → section replace → Back closes in the same document with focus back on the gear; `/` → "dark mode" → Appearance only → Enter focuses Theme → Escape closes and strips `?settings=` (a bug where the Dialog's own signal write pre-empted the strip was found and fixed here); Splitter clamps at 20/40 by drag and Home/End and the ratio persists across pages; High contrast sets `data-contrast="high"`, the PATCH answers `Set-Cookie: pj.a11y=contrast-high…`, the next document is server-rendered with `data-contrast="high"`, then restored; console pages, the unknown-section redirect and the phone layouts (drill-down, narrow modal) behave; zero console errors. 87 unit tests. **Flagged (surface, do not silently resolve):** (a) **The brief's citations do not match the log** — notification precedence is #57 (not #56), retiring duplicate preference columns is #57(d), and no "Phase 4 Settings Rulings" exist; built against the cited intent, logged here. (b) **`org.remove_user_email` hard-deletes** a secondary address, against PRODUCT_MANAGEMENT §3.5 rule (2) / §5.4 "nothing is hard-deleted"; a soft delete needs a `removed_at` column and every reader updated — a human call. (c) **`comms.fn_resolve_channels`, `fn_is_quiet_hours` and `fn_ensure_notification_prefs` are SECURITY DEFINER and EXECUTE-able by `anon`/`authenticated`** (the comms schema never revokes the PUBLIC default): anyone can read another user's routing outcome or whether they are in quiet hours. Not fixed here. (d) Messaging's "Mute all" is the PLATFORM-wide snooze (`notification_prefs.muted_until`), stored as a far-future instant (Notifications reads it as "until you resume"); "group activity" and "service inquiries" share one stored switch (drawn as one toggle); the message toggles govern in-app delivery only.
