Closing, cancelling or removing a choice revokes its object URL. **(D) PRESENTATION.** In the picker `.fx-card__meta` drops the reserved hover-date row and sits on `--card-gap` / `--card-gap-tight` (62 → 51px); the hub's grid keeps its 62px constant. `InspectPanel` (shared by the picker drawer and `/files`) moves Open in new tab beside Close and Download onto the preview's top-left as icon-only ghost `Button`s with tooltips and `.ui-hit` floors; Delete stays labelled. **(E) VERIFIED.** `deno task check` 0 errors; lint clean; `deno task test:unit` 2,534 passed, only the four `theme-engine.test.ts` failures (#96(a)); `media-pick.test.ts` covers toggle-on/off, the ceiling, single-slot replacement, circular indices and slot assignment. Browser, live stack, owner `chloewinters` (showcase restored to its empty seed afterwards): an empty slot opened "Add to your showcase" with a ceiling of 6 (3 once three were filled); Continue disabled at zero; two dropped files staged as `blob:` URLs with `files.items` unchanged (9); a library card selected, re-clicked off, and the ceiling blocked three cards with the notice; flags hidden on selected cards; Continue → Crop with two chevrons 8px from the stage, next ×3 = 1→2→3→1, previous from 1 → 3, a tray chip switched to item 2; a 1.46× crop survived switching away and back; still no write; Back → Browse → Continue; Save & apply wrote exactly 2 uploads + 3 renditions and filled slots 1, 2, 3; a staged file then Cancel wrote nothing; 390px: no overflow; `/files` drawer: header [Open in new tab, Close], Download 32px at (8, 8) on the preview. **FLAGGED — surface, do not silently resolve:** (a) a multi-slot save applies slots one by one — if one fails, the earlier slots stay applied (named in the editor's notice) and the picker stays open with the error; (b) a staged upload that succeeded before a later failure is now a library row, and a retry reuses it rather than re-uploading; (c) the reserved hover-date row is dropped in the picker only, so its cards no longer show the date — the drawer still does; (d) the inspect-drawer change applies to `/files` too, because the component is shared; (e) the Download disc on the preview is a solid `--surface` disc with `--elevation-low`, not a transparent ghost, because a ghost icon over a photograph has no contrast to promise; (f) the crop phase has two ways back (the bar's icon and the footer's Back); (g) selection order decides slot order apart from slot 1's still rule — there is no drag-to-reorder; (h) a staged file's size is the BROWSER's decode; the server's decode wins on save (`rescale`), and a format the browser cannot decode (HEIC) is refused at the drop; (i) the attach picker's footer is unchanged — `.apk__why` moved left in media mode only. | `PRODUCT_SPEC.md` §The Public Profile · `SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE.md` §The Public Profile · `database/files/Storage.md` §The upload pipeline · `apps/web/features/files/{core/media/media-pick.ts + test,hooks/use-media-pick.ts,components/{InspectPanel.tsx,media-crop/{MediaCropPicker,MediaCropWorkspace,SignInPictureSource}.tsx},islands/AssetPicker.island.tsx,styles/{asset-picker,files-hub,media-crop}.css}` · `apps/web/features/profile/components/{edit/MediaSection,OwnerAvatarEdit}.tsx` · `apps/web/features/messaging/{components/ContactPicker.tsx,islands/ConversationHeader.island.tsx}` · Decisions #158 / #159 |
| 161 | **The shell-free File Inspector and the streaming media proxy — `/inspect/[fileId]` opens one stored file full-window, with the canvas that suits it and a details panel, and reads its bytes only through `GET\|HEAD /api/media/proxy/[fileId]`, which streams private objects through the app after the existing read gate (2026-10-09).** At the product owner's direction; EXTENDS the files read gate (`objectFor`, `live-objects.ts`, unchanged) and the share-response rules of Decision #67; takes explicit, FLAGGED exceptions to Decision #88 (flag (a)), DESIGN_SYSTEM Part D / §D.6 (flag (b)), the middleware's "a route may only tighten" CSP rule and Decision #126 (flag (c)) and guardrails §3 (flag (d)). Decision #132 (chat media tiles → `fileObjectHref`) is unchanged. **(A) STREAMING PROXY.** `MediaBackendService.streamAsset(actor, fileId, request)` → `services/media/stream.ts` `streamAssetFor`; transport `core/storage-stream.ts` `fetchStoredObject` reads storage's INTERNAL origin with the service key (`/storage/v1/object/authenticated/…`, never the public URL, never a client-supplied path). A non-uuid id is a `404` with no database round trip; then the unchanged `objectFor(actor, id, { tier, share })` gate. `Range` / `If-Range` / `If-None-Match` / `If-Modified-Since` and the abort signal are forwarded; 200 / 206 / 304 pass through, 416 is bodiless with `content-range: bytes */<size>`, every other upstream status (storage's 400-for-missing included) is the same bodiless `404`; `HEAD` has no body. Headers are an ALLOWLIST: from storage only `content-length`, `content-range`, `accept-ranges`, `etag`, `last-modified`; the proxy's own `content-type` (an inline-safe type kept, `image/svg+xml` kept, text-like → `text/plain; charset=utf-8`, else `application/octet-stream`, never `text/html`), `content-disposition` (`inline` only when inline-safe and not `?download=1`; the display name as ASCII + RFC 5987 `filename*`, never the path), `content-security-policy: default-src 'none'; sandbox` on everything not inline-safe, `cache-control: private, no-transform, max-age=3600` (`private, no-store` for a share read) + `vary: cookie`, `nosniff`, `cross-origin-resource-policy: same-origin`, `x-robots-tag: noindex, nofollow`, `referrer-policy: no-referrer`; nothing names a host, bucket or path. A 1,200-a-minute sliding window keyed `s:<slug>` / `u:<user>` (anonymous public reads unkeyed) counts only fresh reads (no `Range`, or one from byte 0) → `429` + `retry-after`; storage unreachable → `503`; every refusal `no-store`. The `/api/files/object/[id]` redirect route stays for every other surface; the inspector uses only the proxy. `mediaProxyHref` / `MEDIA_PROXY_ROUTE` in `@projective/types/files` `inspect.ts`. **(B) PAGE ROUTE BY UUID.** `/inspect/[fileId]` (`fileInspectHref`, `INSPECT_ROUTE`) addresses a file by its `files.items.id` uuid, `?share=<slug>` optional. Controller `features/inspector/routes/InspectScreen.tsx` → `core/inspector-ssr.ts` → `FilesBackendService.inspect(id, { share }, actor)` (`live-inspect.ts`; pure `toInspectAsset` in `inspect-dto.ts`): `objectFor`'s three read branches, `source = 'supabase'` + `status = 'uploaded'` + not deleted, the canvas from the pure `resolveViewer(name, mime)` (extension first, stored type second; HEIC / TIFF / PSD draw their `lg` rendition or fall back), `access` owner · member · share · public, `canManage` / `shareUrl` / `downloadCount` server-derived and managers-only, the owner from `org.profiles_index`, and EVERY address a same-origin proxy or page route (`AssetItem.url` / `thumbnailUrl` are never carried). `InspectAssetSchema.safeParse` at runtime (mismatch → `503`); every refusal one calm `404` page that never says whether the file exists; `noindex, nofollow`, `no-referrer`, `private, no-store`. **(C) SHELL-FREE.** The page lives in a new route group `routes/(standalone)/` with NO `_layout.tsx` (only `_app.tsx`: tokens, a11y, `DevMount`) and a `_middleware.ts` that renews the session without redirecting (the `[handle]` model). The page owns its frame: a solid `--surface-1` top bar (mark → `/`, name, `--text-secondary` middot meta, Download, full screen, panel toggle), the canvas, and a details panel in a horizontal `Splitter` at `--bp-md`+ / a floating bottom sheet below it (open by default on desktop, persisted in `pj.local.inspector.panelOpen`; closed on mobile). **(D) PAGE-SCOPED CSP.** `apps/web/utils/csp.ts` gains `CspRelaxations` (`wasm`, `blobConnect`) and `cspHeaderFor(profile: "app" \| "inspector")`, cached per profile; the global middleware sends `cspHeaderFor("app")` (unchanged behaviour); only `/inspect/*` sends `inspector` = the app policy + `'wasm-unsafe-eval'` in `script-src` (pdf.js decoders, Draco / Basis / meshopt) + `blob:` in `connect-src` (GLB embedded textures), nothing else. `x-frame-options: DENY`, `object-src 'none'`, `frame-ancestors 'none'` still hold, so a PDF is drawn on a canvas, never framed. **(E) EXTERNAL ENGINES, THIS PAGE ONLY.** `three@0.186.0` (+ `@types/three`), `pdfjs-dist@6.3.289` (legacy build), `highlight.js@11.11.1`, `dompurify@3.4.15`, `docx-preview@0.3.7` (+ `jszip`), with `marked` already mapped — root `deno.json` `"$comment:inspector"`; engines only, never their CSS (pdf.js's text layer rules rewritten in tokens), reskinned with tokens; client-only, through a memoised dynamic `import()` inside an island effect (the `quill-loader.ts` pattern), never at module scope. Decoder / font trees are served same-origin at versioned `/vendor/<lib>/<version>/…` paths by the `vendorAssets()` plugin in `vite.config.ts` (`features/inspector/core/vendor-paths.ts`). Video and audio stay on `VideoPlayer` / `AudioVisualizer` (#107). **(F) COUNTING.** The proxy never counts a download; the inspector's Download records through `POST /api/files/download-record` for a signed-in viewer, as `FilesHub` does. **(G) RESERVED HANDLE.** `inspect` added to `RESERVED_HANDLES`, `org.fn_is_reserved_handle` (`00001020`, edited in place; applied to the local DB with `create or replace` only) and `features/auth/core/options.ts`. **(H) GESTURES.** Pinch / pan on a canvas follow #159: `touch-action: none` on the canvas element only, never the page. Entry points (inspectable stored files only; links and connector mounts unchanged): `FilesHub`'s Open in new tab, the attachment preview's header, and the ticket Attachments / Submissions tabs, each a new tab with `noopener,noreferrer`. 24 registry glyphs added (`DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §B.7.1). **(I) VERIFIED SO FAR.** Backend: `deno check` on the 9 touched files; lint clean on the 7 created; `stream.test.ts` + `inspect-dto.test.ts` 24 passed; a live script against the local stack printed ALL PASS twice — `chloewinters`' private file 200 to her (30,316 bytes, inline, `private, no-transform, max-age=3600`) and 404 to `danielokafor` and to a guest; a public file 200 to a guest; a project file 200 to `danielokafor` / `tomaszwojcik` and 404 to `chloewinters`; `bytes=0-99` → 206 + `content-range: bytes 0-99/30316`; HEAD; `If-None-Match` → 304; `bytes=999999-` → 416 `bytes */30316`; `tier=lg` → `image/webp`; `download=1` → attachment; a non-ASCII name encoded; a share slug 200 `private, no-store`, a wrong / foreign / revoked slug 404; over HTTP through the real handler the same, with a leak check (Supabase host, `:54321`, `kong`, `/storage/`, bucket names, owner path) clean on every response and on the DTO JSON; `inspect` returned owner / member / share / public as specified and parsed with `InspectAssetSchema`. Platform: `csp.test.ts` + `workspace.contract.test.ts` 13 passed; every library type-checks (a negative probe gave the four expected TS2322s); the `vendorAssets()` probe served wasm / bcmap / pfb / ttf / js / icc with their types in dev, refused traversal, and a real `vite build` emitted 205 byte-identical files; `inspect` reserved in the live DB. The canvases, the whole-tree gate, a production `serve` of `/vendor/…` and the browser pass belong to the integration stage and are NOT recorded here. **FLAGGED — surface, do not silently resolve:** (a) **uuid page route vs #88** — root `CLAUDE.md` §4 says uuids do not route; `files.items` has no slug and `/api/files/object/[id]` already takes a uuid, so the page follows the API; the alternative is a `fil-` slug (column, `security.fn_slug_guard`, `SLUG_PREFIXES`, Tables.md, contract tests); (b) **a fifth shell mode** — Part D resolves every page to one of four profiles and §D.6 says a page is not a focus candidate because it "wants to feel focused"; `(standalone)` constructs no shell at all, recorded in Part D as an owner-directed deviation, not a profile; (c) **a page-scoped CSP LOOSENING** — the middleware docblock says a route may only tighten, and #126 is the CSP decision; `'wasm-unsafe-eval'` permits WebAssembly compilation only (not JS `eval`), and `blob:` in `connect-src` lets the page fetch blob URLs it minted; (d) **external npm libraries vs guardrails §3** ("no UI-library dependencies") — read here as engines only, client-only, no vendor CSS or chrome; whether §3 should say so is for a human; (e) **a share link's download limit does not bound viewing** — `download_count` moves only through `files.fn_record_download`, so a `download_limit = 1` link can be opened and streamed without limit; pre-existing on the redirect route, made visible by a full inspector (PRODUCT_SPEC §Assets "Deduplication and download history"); (f) **same-origin XSS** — the redirect served bytes from the storage origin, the proxy serves them from the APP origin beside the session cookie; mitigations: never `text/html`, `attachment` + `default-src 'none'; sandbox` on everything not inline-safe, `nosniff`, `cross-origin-resource-policy: same-origin`, text-like files read with `fetch()` and rendered as text, Markdown through `marked` → `DOMPurify`, SVG drawn through `<img>` only (a top-level navigation to it meets the sandbox CSP); the residual surface is any future renderer that injects proxy bytes as HTML; (g) **worker occupancy** — the upload path keeps large bodies off request workers by design, the proxy holds one for a whole read (a long video for as long as it plays); the rate window bounds starts, not duration; large-file streaming and Deno's legacy `request.signal` abort behaviour were only seen with small objects; (h) **service placement** — `streamAsset` sits in `MediaBackendService` (ROUTING: `/api/media/*` → media services) while the gate lives in `services/files`; the media `CLAUDE.md` scope ("the only way bytes reach a public bucket") does not describe a read door; (i) the owner is read from `org.profiles_index` (the public profile door), not `org.users_public` as the spec said, so an owner who chose a private profile is `null` on the page — even to themselves — and the avatar is `null` when the directory has no public avatar; `shareUrl` adds `created_by = viewer`, equivalent while `canManage` means ownership; (j) **the service worker stored `no-store` documents** — `sw.js` `handleNavigation` cached every `200` navigation regardless of `cache-control`, so `/inspect/[fileId]` (like `/share/[slug]`, `/exit`, `/invite/[token]` already) was replayable offline on that device; RESOLVED in the integration pass: a document whose `cache-control` contains `no-store` is never stored (the cache name is unchanged, as no inspector page was ever served before the fix); (k) `features/auth/core/options.ts`'s username denylist still lacks `file`, `files` and `share` (pre-existing drift; only `inspect` was added); (l) pinch on a canvas follows #159 against #75(c)'s "two-finger gestures belong to the browser"; scoped to the canvas, the page still zooms; (m) **no Dev Context Switcher axis** — the page branches on server facts only (access, kind, `canManage`); its client fallbacks (no WebGL, a failed decode) were given no dev axis, which the #152 precedent may argue for; (n) the vendor paths carry the package version (`/vendor/pdfjs/6.3.289/…`), not the spec's unversioned paths, because Fresh serves client-build files as immutable; `.bcmap` is probably served `text/plain` in production (pdf.js reads it as an ArrayBuffer); (o) **integration pass** (a `deno task dev` server and a `deno serve` of the production build, headless Chromium under the real inspector CSP): 17 fixtures uploaded as `chloewinters` through upload-init → PUT → upload-complete (one per canvas, plus an HTML file and a 24 MB binary) render in light, dark and mobile with no console error and no request to the storage origin; the svg / text / binary content-type and sandbox policy, Range / HEAD / 304 / 416 and the 404 matrix hold over HTTP; `/vendor/…` is served by the production build; the `.docx` canvas failed in the production build only (jszip bundled from CommonJS) and now resolves jszip's browser bundle (`browserEntries()` in `vite.config.ts`). Still not exercised: the `429` path. The live verification left about four REVOKED share rows on `chloewinters`' private file, and the integration pass left one live `link` share on her `kickoff-notes.txt` fixture. | root `CLAUDE.md` §2/§3/§4/§6/§8 · `ROUTING.md` §Route groups / §Special files / §Dynamic segments / §Reserved-handle precedence / §Profile and media API · `SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE.md` §Asset Management → The file inspector and the streaming proxy, §The Public Profile (media pipeline), §Runtime & API Security (CSP), §Offline handling · `PRODUCT_SPEC.md` §Assets & Attachments → The File Inspector + §Sitemap · `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` Part D intro, §D.6, §B.7.1, §C.1 (icons) · `database/files/{Storage,Functions}.md` · `database/org/Functions.md` · `packages/types/files/inspect.ts + test` · `packages/backend/{core/{storage-stream,storage-url}.ts,services/media/{stream.ts + test,MediaBackendService.ts},services/files/{live-inspect.ts,inspect-dto.ts + test,FilesBackendService.ts}}` · `apps/web/routes/{api/media/proxy/[fileId].ts,(standalone)/{_middleware.ts,inspect/[fileId].tsx},_middleware.ts}` · `apps/web/features/inspector/**` · `apps/web/utils/csp.ts + test` · `vite.config.ts` · `deno.json` · `packages/types/profile/reserved.ts` · `supabase/migrations/00001020_functions_org_entities.sql` · `apps/web/features/auth/core/options.ts` · `packages/ui/icons/core/paths.tsx` · Decisions #67 / #75 / #88 / #107 / #126 / #128 / #152 / #159 |
| 162 | **The file preview modal rebuilt on the inspector's canvases, and asset bytes addressed only through the streaming proxy — `AttachmentPreviewModal` (`.fx-modal`) mounts the `/inspect` canvas in place with a facts aside, a source-message section and "Go to message"; every stored asset's preview and grid thumbnail reads `/api/media/proxy/[fileId]` (2026-10-10).** At the product owner's direction; AMENDS Decision #161(A) ("the redirect route stays for every other surface") and #132(A)(3) (chat tiles and explorer thumbnails on `fileObjectHref`); supersedes #34(C)'s best-effort `#m-` anchor; follows #67(A)/(C), #65, #138, #88, #107, #127, #146, #159. **(A) PROXY EVERYWHERE FOR PREVIEWS AND GRIDS.** `AssetItemSchema` gains `assetId` (`AssetIdSchema.nullable().optional()`, the `files.items` uuid; `assetIdOf()` in `@projective/types/files` `assets.ts`) — distinct from `id`, which on a project, channel or conversation row is the `comms.message_attachments` link id. `MessageAttachmentSchema` gains `assetId`, `mimeType`, `blurhash`. The producers — `asset-row.ts` `assetAddress` (hub), `projects/live-files.ts`, `messaging/live-workspace.ts`, `projects/message-attachments.ts` (chat tiles) — emit `url = mediaProxyHref(assetId)` and, for images, `thumbnailUrl = mediaProxyHref(assetId, { tier: "sm" })` (chat tiles `md`) for every stored, uploaded file, public buckets included (the hub used to link those straight to storage; so no storage host, bucket or path appears in any URL); project and conversation rows now also carry the stored width, height and metadata (BlurHash) through `withMediaFacts`. Fixtures, web links and connector files keep their own URLs (fixtures omit `assetId`). The client resolver is `apps/web/features/files/core/asset-media.ts` — `assetMediaSrc(asset, tier?, share?)` (the proxy when `inspectable` and a uuid `assetId ?? id`, else the row's own `thumbnailUrl` / `url`, `"#"` → null), `assetMediaSrcset` (`sm 320w, md 1280w, lg 2560w`, raster images with stored bytes only) and `assetPlaceholder` (`imagePlaceholderOf`) — feeding `ProgressiveImage` in `FileCard`, `FileTable`, the modal tray and the legacy `FilePreview`. The brief's `?variant=thumb` is mapped to the existing tier vocabulary (`FILE_OBJECT_TIERS`, `?tier=sm`); no second parameter exists (flag (d)). `/api/files/object/[id]` stays only for its remaining consumers: the owner's library picker (`files/core/media/media-pick.ts`) and the share-folder listing (`live-library.ts`). **(B) HAND-ROLLED MODAL, NOT `Dialog`.** `BodyPortal` → `.fx-modal` (z-index from `useOverlayStack` through `--fx-z`) → `Backdrop` (rendered from the first paint) → `.fx-modal__panel` (`role="dialog"`, `aria-modal`, `aria-labelledby` at the file name, `data-ins-key-scope`), `--overlay-w-full` × `--overlay-h-lg`, `--overlay-radius-lg`, `--hairline` seams; `useFocusTrap` (initial focus on the stage, moved onto the canvas once it loads unless the person has already clicked or typed), `useDismiss` enabled only while the modal is the top layer (`closeOnOutside: false`, the scrim dismisses through `Backdrop`), focus returned to `returnFocusTo` or the trigger one macrotask after close. `Dialog` is not used, per #67(C): it caps width at `--overlay-w-lg`, pads its body and settles at `scale(1)` (a containing block, §B.10.4) — wrong for a two-pane workspace (flag (e)). Same export, every existing prop kept; new optional `frame`, `context`, `onGoToMessage`, `returnFocusTo`. Code: `features/projects/components/AttachmentPreviewModal.tsx` + `components/preview/` (`PreviewShell` · `PreviewSession` · `PreviewHeader` · `PreviewStage` · `PreviewAside` · `SourceMessage` · `PreviewTray` · `PreviewAction`, pure rules in `preview-model.ts` + test, `use-preview-data.ts` page-lifetime signal caches capped at 48); `attachment-modal.css` rewritten token-only — the old card-in-card `.fx-audio` / `.fx-doc` boxes (§B.9.2) and the hand-rolled `position: absolute` menu (§B.10.4) are gone. **(C) MIME IS INLINE META, LABELLED "FORMAT".** Header meta line `Format · Size · W×H \| duration` in `--text-secondary` middot text (`tabular-nums` on changing figures), facts in the aside — never a badge (anti-tagification, root `CLAUDE.md` §3.7, §B.11.2 names file formats), and never called "verified": upload-complete sniffs the bytes (`live-uploads.ts`) but stores the sniffed type or the declared one without recording which (flag (a)). **(D) VIEWER COVERAGE.** A stored file loads its `InspectAsset` through the new `GET /api/files/inspect` (`FilesService.inspect`, null on 404) and mounts the inspector's own viewer through `useInspectorHost(dto, { signedIn, embedded: true })` → `InspectorEmbedStage` + `InspectorChipBar` (aside: `InspectorEmbedControls`, `InspectorEmbedFacts`), keyed by asset id; only the active file mounts a viewer (no carousel). Embedded shells run `InspectorShellOptions { urlSync: false, print: false, embedded: true }`: no `#L` hash writes or reads, no print dialog, Ctrl+P or Print button (printing lives on `/inspect` only), keys scoped to the `data-ins-key-scope` element instead of `document`, the stages' own floating bars replaced by the chip bar. Image, SVG, video, audio, code, text, Markdown, table, font, PDF (pdf.js runs without its wasm decoders under the app CSP and falls back) and DOCX render in the modal; **3D models hand off** — the stage shows a model card with "Open in inspector" (`embedModelHandoff`), because the app CSP lacks `'wasm-unsafe-eval'` and `blob:` connect, which #161(D) keeps page-scoped to `/inspect` (flag (f)). A file with no stored bytes (fixtures, links, connectors) falls back to the legacy `FilePreview`, now proxy-correct with working controls (`ProgressiveImage`, a real `VideoPlayer`, `AudioVisualizer` with stored peaks only, real text capped at 1 MB / 2,000 lines, a working Download or "Open link", no button when there is neither). Image adds a rule-of-thirds overlay (`.ins-image__thirds`, key `G`, a Controls toggle and a chip) and chip rotate / reset, on `/inspect` too. **(E) STACKING — a second modal REPLACES the first (§B.10.9).** Inside a ticket chain the preview is a `"file"` frame on `ticketStack` (it replaces the ticket; pop restores the ticket with its tab and scroll); `SubmissionExplorer` gets its own `createModalStack<"review" \| "file" \| "presubmit">()` so review, preview and PreSubmit render only as the top frame; `SubmissionReviewModal`'s state moves into frame state so a draft survives a file frame; `PreSubmit`-over-preview becomes a frame. The modal caches `page`, `asideOpen`, `sheetOpen`, `shortcutsOpen`, `noteDraft`, `notes`, `asideScroll` per frame uid. `useOverlayStack` releases the body scroll lock deferred (`createScrollLock`, `packages/ui/hooks/scroll-lock.ts` + test: two animation frames + a timer watchdog, cancelled by a claim in between) so a frame swap never unlocks for a frame. The `AssetPicker`-over-ticket pile (#138, owner WIP) was out of scope here and still stacked (flag (b)); the (E2) amendment retires it for the ticket chain and PreSubmit. **(F) MOBILE (< `--bp-md`).** The panel is fullscreen (`block-size: 100dvh`, radius 0, no shadow) and the scrim is not drawn beneath it; the header drops the category glyph and "Open in new tab"; the aside becomes an in-panel `InspectorSheet` (non-portalled, its own Escape layer, swipe-down to dismiss with pointer capture only after a 6px slop, half / full snap by drag or an Expand button, solid `--surface-1`, a solid tonal scrim — no second blur — focus in and back) opened by a floating Info button; on-canvas chips (zoom out · level · in · fit · rotate · reset for image / SVG; page n/N + zoom for PDF); pinch and twist on the canvas only (`touch-action: none`, #159 / #161(l)). The fullscreen geometry sits outside the §B.10.1 size tokens, scoped to this modal on the #161(C) inspector precedent (flag (e)). **(G) SOURCE MESSAGE AND "GO TO MESSAGE".** The aside shows the message a file came from (`UserAvatar`, name, time, the stored — already masked — plain-text excerpt, ≤ 280 chars cut at a word) whenever the file has message provenance: painted at once from the row's own sender / message fields, then refreshed by the reverse lookup `GET\|HEAD /api/messaging/attachment-source?assetId=&conversationId=` → `MessagingBackendService.attachmentSource(assetId, actor, { conversationId })` → `live-attachment-source.ts` `fetchAttachmentSources` (RLS through `view_attachments_if_member` / `comms.can_read_message`; deleted messages excluded; newest first; a guest or any failure → `[]`; fixture branch in mock mode), backed by the new `idx_message_attachments_attachment` index (`00004006`, edited in place). The lookup runs in every context but `submission`, so a hub file shows a source when one exists. `AttachmentSourceSchema` lives in `@projective/types/projects` `messages.ts`. "Go to message" closes the chain, then on the next task either jumps in the same feed (`onGoToMessage`) or navigates to the source's `href`: `/messages/<thread>?m=<id>` for a DM; `/projects/<prj-…>/<stg-…>/chat?m=<id>` for a stage room (a Task's discussion room by its stage slug), `…/discussion/chat?m=` for the project-wide room, and the room uuid for a private team or business room or an extra project-wide room, which have no other address (flag (h)); the client builders `conversationMessageHref` / `channelMessageHref` (`chat-context.ts`, `MESSAGE_ANCHOR_PARAM = "m"`) emit slug paths only and answer `null` for a uuid segment. `ChatFeed` reads `?m=<messageId>` (and the legacy `#m-<id>`) on mount, skips open-at-bottom, strips the parameter with `replaceState`, jumps and flashes. The conversation-scope "Go to message" bug (`/projects/<conv>/<conv>/chat`) is gone: a DM row links `/messages/<conv>?m=`. Chat media tiles open the modal (amends #132); middle / Ctrl / ⌘-click still opens `/inspect` in a new tab. **(H) HEADER ACTIONS.** Star, Download (`mediaProxyHref(assetId, { download: true })`, recorded through `POST /api/files/download-record` first, as `FilesHub` does), Copy link (the absolute `/inspect/[fileId]` address, a "copied" state + live-region message), Open in new tab (icon-only ghost `<a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">`, stored files only), the aside toggle (`panel-right`; persisted in `pj.local.preview.asideOpen`, `LocalKeys.PREVIEW_ASIDE_OPEN`) and Close. The kebab "Report" is removed — there is no backend for it (gate 11). Desktop aside: `Splitter` `stateKey="fx-preview"`, 70 / 30, aside 18–45; ← / → page through files only while focus is in the header or the tray. **(I) VERIFIED SO FAR.** Data: `deno check` + `deno lint` clean on 19 touched files; `packages/backend/services/{files,projects,messaging}` + `packages/types` 1,073 passed; the index applied to the live local DB; a live script printed ALL PASS — DM, project and hub rows carry `assetId` and proxy URLs (`?tier=sm`, chat tiles `?tier=md`, 2400×1600 / 2560×2560), a non-participant gets 404 / empty, DM and project sources resolve to `/messages/323bdb0f…?m=2266062d…` and `/projects/prj-67ss4hwneu/stg-3f4gvyycwv/chat?m=e8d7c7f7…`, filtered, foreign and anonymous lookups give `[]`, the two routes answer 200 / 400 / 404 over HTTP, and no storage host, `:54321`, `/storage/`, `/api/files/object` or bucket / path appears in any result. Inspector embed: `deno check` on the island, the page and every embed file; `deno lint` 145 files clean; inspector tests 150 passed. Modal: `deno check` on 14 files + the six mount sites; lint clean; `preview-model.test.ts` 14 passed. Nothing in this row had been seen in a browser when it was written: the stack (E) and feed (G, `?m=` / tiles) work and the whole-tree gate + browser pass were still running in parallel, so their sentences above state the contract, not a measurement. **(I2) INTEGRATE VERIFIED (2026-10-10).** Whole tree: `deno task check` and `deno task lint` (2,376 files) exit 0; `deno task test:unit` 2,804 passed, 4 failed (the known `theme-engine.test.ts`, #96(a)); `deno task build` exit 0. Browser (headless Chromium, live dev server + `dev:mock` + the production bundle): every stored image tile on `/files`, `/messages/<conv>/files` and the submissions explorer loads (`naturalWidth > 0`) from `/api/media/proxy/` with zero `/storage/v1/` or `:54321` file requests; all 17 fixture viewers open in the modal on desktop light, dark and 390×844 with one `.ui-backdrop` and one `[aria-modal]` dialog, header, facts, aside collapse, tray paging, chips and the thirds overlay (drawn exactly over the image box); on mobile the Info button opens the sheet, the first Escape closes only the sheet, a touch swipe closes it, the second Escape closes the modal; a ticket's attachment or submission file replaces the ticket (one scrim, ticket unmounted, `?tkv=` kept) and Escape restores the same tab, scroll offset and focus on the tile, on the board and on the standalone host; Back from a pushed ticket with a preview on top closes the chain; a review's "Open preview" replaces the review and Escape restores it with the typed note and the annotation; "Go to message" from `/messages/<conv>/files` lands on `/messages/<conv>` with `?m=` stripped and the message flashed, in-feed jumps flash in place, a middle-click on a chat tile opens `/inspect/<asset>`. Fixed in this pass: (1) the scroll lock is claimed in a layout effect — with the claim in a post-paint effect a long task between a frame swap and paint (400 ms injected) let the deferred release win and unlocked the body for 13–87 ms; (2) a `?m=` landing pinned the target under the sticky header (estimated offsets of unmeasured rows), now pinned at the clearance once the scroll has run, cancelled by the reader's wheel, touch or key; (3) an unknown `kind` crashed the header icon and blanked the ticket chain (the board fixtures emitted `kind: "document"`); the icon falls back and the fixture uses the real kinds; (4) the header meta line takes the canvas's discovered resolution or duration when nothing stored has one; (5) a fallback preview with no control in it now focuses the stage, not Star; (6) table cells keep a third of `--measure-narrow`, so a narrow phone no longer crushes text columns to a few characters. **(E2) AMENDMENT (2026-10-10) — THE ASSET PICKER REPLACES THE TICKET. AMENDS #138; RESOLVES flag (b) for the ticket chain.** At the product owner's direction, with explicit permission to edit the owner-WIP `AssetPicker.island.tsx` (the owner's `openRaw` → `/inspect` and Continue-hidden-at-`max` 1 hunks kept; `asset-picker.css` untouched). #138 verified the ticket → picker chain as a LIFO pile of two modals (Escape and the scrim closing only the picker, the scroll lock held across both); that chain is now ONE modal at a time: "From library" pushes a `"picker"` frame onto `ticketStack` (`TicketFrameKind` gains `"picker"`; `pushTicketPicker(ticketUid)` copies the ticket's `ticketId` / `slug` / `standalone` / `mode`, records `TICKET_LIBRARY_TRIGGER` = `"control:library"` under `FILE_FRAME_TRIGGER`, and refuses unless that ticket is on top and a host renders picker frames for its posture through the existing `registerTicketFileHost` registry), and `TicketPickerFrame` (`{ uid }`) renders `AssetPicker` with the new `frame?: PickerFrameRef` and `title?: string` props. A framed picker is always open, its `Backdrop` is the only scrim, and its dismissal pops the frame (`back()`, else `close()`); #138's single Escape owner and scrim-only dismissal still govern it as the one layer. The picker is a leaf frame (inspect and crop live in its own pane, "open raw" is a new tab): its browsing state is not frame-cached. **Result delivery:** the ticket is unmounted when the picks arrive, so `onPick` writes them into the ticket frame's own cache (`handOffPicks(stack, uid, assets)` under `PICKER_HANDOFF_KEY` = `"picker:handoff"`, de-duplicated by id; `features/files/core/picker-frame.ts` + test) and the restored Attachments tab takes them once on mount (`takePicks`, read-and-clear) and stages them; a module-level map keyed by uid was rejected (uids are per stack and collide across `ticketStack`, the explorer stack and `eventStack`; entries would leak when a chain closes first), as was a reactive signal (the frame cache is non-reactive by §B.10.9 property 1). The tab's sort order moved to frame state (`attachments:sortKey` / `attachments:sortDir`) so it survives the round trip. **Focus return — one mechanism:** the (E) file-frame trigger was extended rather than a second one added: a trigger is now a file id **or** a control token, and `useFileTriggerFocus` resolves a token through `useFileTiles`' new `locateTrigger` option; the designed `FRAME_FOCUS_KEY` / `useFrameFocusReturn` / `ModalFrameRef` (an `initialFocusRef` path in `packages/ui`) was not built, so `packages/ui` is unchanged. A ticket being created keeps focus off its title while a trigger is waiting. **URL:** `addressingFrame` now skips picker frames as it skips file frames — `?tkv=` stays while the picker shows, Back with the picker open closes the chain, and a same-slug popstate does not re-open (and wipe) the ticket; the designed `ticket-chain.ts` (`nearestOfKind`, `closesOnMissingTicketParam`, `chainTicketFrame`) was therefore not needed, and review frames stay excluded as in (E). `TicketPickerFrame` finds its ticket with `ticketStack.beneath` (a picker is only ever pushed straight over a ticket). Board, Timeline and the deep-link host render the picker frame beside `TicketFileFrame`; `TicketStandalone` re-exports it so the lazy chunk carries the picker and its CSS (the CSS edge moved from `TicketAttachmentsTab` to `TicketPickerFrame`). **AssetPicker hygiene:** awaited uploads and link attaches capture a session token and drop their pick (not the upload) once the picker has closed, and the hub's `selection` / anchor are restored and finished uploads swept when the picker closes **or** unmounts while open — fixing a pre-existing bug where `/files?tkv=` left the hub selection cleared. **Save in flight:** `repointFrame` re-points only a ticket on top, so while a new ticket's create is pending `TicketView` takes `saving` and "From library" is disabled, and an attachment opens in a new tab instead of a file frame. **Gate change:** "From library" renders only when `isClient && canEdit` (was `isClient`) — `commit()` already returned early without `canEdit`, so the control did nothing on a read-only ticket (root `CLAUDE.md` §3.11); live board access sets `canEditTicket === isClient`, so the board is unchanged, and the basket ticket (`BasketTicketModal`, `canEdit={false}`, which renders nothing for a non-ticket top) loses the control. Verified: `deno task check` exit 0; `deno task lint` exit 0 (2,379 files); `deno test` over `packages/ui/`, `features/projects/` and `features/files/` 974 passed, 4 failed (the known `theme-engine.test.ts`, #96(a)); new tests 6 (picker handoff) + 2 (`ticket-view`). **PreSubmit (same amendment):** `SubmissionExplorer`'s stack gains `"picker"` (`PRESUBMIT_PICKER_ID`); `PreSubmitModal` drops its own picker mount and `openPicker` and takes `onOpenLibrary?` (the control renders only when it is given) and `frame?: TileFrame`; the host records `PRESUBMIT_LIBRARY_TRIGGER` on the PreSubmit frame and pushes the picker, whose picks land in the host's `libraryPicks` with no handoff; the selected rail row moved to frame state (`selectedFileId`), its reset skipped once on restore. **Browser-verified (2026-10-10;** `dev:mock` board, timeline and `/messages?tkv=` with file reads forwarded to the live stack, live `/files?tkv=` as `noor`; 1440×900, 1280×460 and 390×844**):** exactly one `.ui-backdrop` and no `.tkv` / `.subm-review` on every sampled frame while the picker shows; `?tkv=` kept; body `overflow: hidden` on every rAF across each swap with zero body-style writes; Escape restores the Attachments tab at its scroll (80 px) with focus on "From library" ("Add from your files" in PreSubmit); a two-file pick raises the cached `attachmentCount` by 2 with a Details title edit kept, and PreSubmit's rail by 2 with the selected row kept; the backdrop pops only while nothing is chosen; Back with the picker open closes the chain; create mode keeps the title draft and focus on "From library"; "From library" is disabled while a create's save is held open and works after the answer; the `/files` hub selection is restored after Escape, after a pick and when the chain closes under an open picker (a Back on `/files` clears it through the hub's own `popstate` location sync, exactly as with no picker); the (E) preview frame and the review → preview frame re-ran clean. The basket ticket was not driven (neither data set holds a basket ticket); its control is absent by construction (`canEdit={false}`). **FLAGGED — surface, do not silently resolve:** (a) **"verified" MIME has no stored fact** — the sniff result is not recorded, so the modal says "Format" only; a truthful verified mark needs a `files.items` column (e.g. `mime_verified`) written at upload-complete, a schema change for the owner; (b) **the `AssetPicker`-over-ticket pile (#138) — RESOLVED by (E2)** for the ticket chain; PreSubmit → picker resolved too (flag (s)); (c) **proxy egress and worker occupancy for grids** — every grid thumbnail is now a proxied read that holds a request worker (#161 flag (g)) and counts against the 1,200-a-minute window per identity, so a long `/files` grid scrolled fast can meet a `429`; responses are `private, max-age=3600`, `vary: cookie`, and the service worker never caches `/api/*`; there is no CDN in front of the proxy; (d) **`?variant=thumb` was mapped to `?tier=sm`** — the brief named a parameter the API does not have; the `sm` tier is the 320px rendition, and an image with no `item_variants` row (an SVG, an image the upload pipeline could not decode, the seeded project-bucket images) is served its ORIGINAL, so "thumbnails" on those rows are full-size reads; (e) **`Dialog` is not used (#67(C))** and the mobile panel is a fullscreen `100dvh` / radius-0 geometry outside the §B.10.1 token set — scoped here on the #161(C) precedent, but the §B.10 merge gate reads it as new geometry; (f) **3D hands off to `/inspect` because of the CSP** — loosening the app CSP for the modal would amend #161(D) / #126, so it was not done; (g) **jump reach** — `ChatFeed` pages back at most `JUMP_PAGE_BUDGET` = 8 pages (≈ 30 + 8 × 30 messages on a DM) while the Files tab scans the newest 400 messages (`MESSAGE_SCAN_CAP`), so "Go to message" on an older file ends at the existing "too far back" status unless an `around=<messageId>` anchor is added to the thread read; (h) **room uuids in message links** — a private team or business room and any extra project-wide room have no slug, so their `?m=` links carry the room uuid against #88; (i) sender avatars still link straight to the public `avatars` bucket (from `org.get_party_cards`, outside this change); (j) the share-folder listing (`live-library.ts`) still builds `/api/files/object` pages as well as photography (§B.4.3 case 3 covers imagery only); (n) `MediaLightbox` reuses `.fx-modal` / `.fx-modal__panel` / `.fx-modal__act` with its own sheet, so a page loading both sheets could
