| 163 | **The workspace Overview redesigned, a Task and a Session presented as one room, and review authority configuring the engagement (2026-10-10).** At the product owner's direction (the "Refactor Project Workspace Overview, Channel Topology, and Single-Task Navigation" brief), with three conflicts against logged decisions put to the owner and answered the same day. **(A) ONE ROOM, PRESENTED.** A Task or a Session presents exactly one shared room, named "Discussion" (`isSingleRoomEngagement`, `discussionRoomId`, `SINGLE_ROOM_NAME` in `detail.ts`): the File Explorer tree, the Overview's Messages list and the files index list that room (relabelled) and the viewer's private threads, never a stage room or the spare project-wide room. The stored topology is UNCHANGED — the owner chose presentation over a data-model change — so a Task's Discussion is still its root stage's room (#121(C), #133) and its project-wide room (#135) still exists, unlisted. **(B) THE TYPE-SWITCH SANITIZER.** `projects.sanitize_single_room_topology(project)` runs at the end of every live save that leaves the project a Task or a Session (and its stub twin trims the stored stages): a Task keeps its root stage (lowest `sort_order`) and retires every other stage with its rooms; a Session keeps its stages — they are its sessions — and retires their stage-bound rooms; the project-wide room is never touched. Retiring is a HARD delete only when the row carries no history at all (`fn_stage_has_history`: tickets current or required, ticket history, submissions, seats, open seats, invitations, invite links, every finance row, and room history; `fn_room_has_history`: messages, channel files, scheduled events) and otherwise an ARCHIVE — the owner's rule, which RESOLVES the conflict with guardrails §5 ("nothing is hard-deleted") for this path. New soft-removal columns `projects.project_stages.archived_at` and `comms.project_channels.archived_at`; the room SELECT policy hides an archived room (not `has_channel_access`, so its messages and files stay readable as history); the stage product reads (lane, board, overview, feed counts, members, submissions, setup, ticket commit, discussion resolution, wallet funding queue, personal calendar) filter it; `fn_enforce_structure_variation`, `set_project_status`, `approve_stage`'s handover check, `get_nav_activity` and `get_stage_channels` ignore it; `get_stage_channels` no longer provisions a Session's stage rooms. A save of a Task reconciles only its root stage, so a retired stage is never re-created against the one-stage cap. The two history probes are revoked from every client role. **(C) REVIEW AUTHORITY CONFIGURES.** `/details` (the editor; `/edit` stays #144's 308 to it) and `/preview` admit `projects.can_review_project` — the owner, or an active member of the paying client business — mirrored as `ProjectDetail.viewerCanConfigure` (`resolveReviewAuthority`, fail-closed) and `ResolvedProjectAccess.canConfigure` (a dev-simulated access overrides it); everyone else gets a 303 to `/projects/[slug]` — AMENDS #144, whose `/preview` sent a non-owner to the public listing. A business member is the client side, so their `viewerAccess` is `owner`. The write path widened to the same predicate: `projects.projects` UPDATE and a reviewer SELECT arm, `project_stages` and `stage_staffing_roles` FOR ALL, `project_attachments` SELECT/INSERT/DELETE, `stage_assignments` SELECT (so the onboarding lock sees the seats that freeze prices), and the owner checks inside `create_stage`, `delete_stage`, `reorder_stages` and `set_project_status` (messages keep "Only the project owner" for `refusalFrom`). `trg_guard_project_ownership` keeps `owner_user_id` and `client_business_id` the owner's alone. The stage Details tab and its guards read `viewerCanConfigure`. **(D) NAVIGATION.** A Task's top-tier "Details" view link is removed (REVERSES the Task half of the #121 view-link rule); every type's way into the configuration is the lane footer's icon-only "Edit project" control, for review authority only, drawn as a solid `--primary` / `--on-primary` fill while `/details` is open (`LaneIconButton` `fillWhenActive`, `.ui-lane-iconbtn--fill-active`; the collapsed rail's `.proj-railbtn--edit` likewise). The Overview hero's owner identity row (`.pjd-hero__identity`) is removed. **(E) THE OVERVIEW.** Main column: Needs you · Brief (description as prose; format · deadline · budget model as a middot line; skills as middot text, never tags — §B.11) · Inclusions & terms (a hairline ledger: NDA none / Projective standard / the client's own, IP ownership, portfolio, location, languages, deadline bonuses) · Project files (the client's reference files, searchable and filterable, previews through `/api/media/proxy/:fileId`; `ProjectAttachments` island) · the stage run · Recent updates. Aside: a participant's Active submissions (their own units by review state — in revision, pending review, approved) and Claimable tasks (`todo`, described, unclaimed, on a seated `open_pull` stage, within `max_concurrent_intensity` after the work they hold), Your work, Your earnings · Messages, hidden when it would list only the Discussion the lane already links · People (avatar, name, handle, Owner / Client member / Freelancer, stages except on a Task, hired team, role in the paying business — `ProjectMemberRow.team` / `.business`). The setup is now read for every viewer; only the composed brief reaches a participant, prices stay the owner's (`composeWorkspace`). FLAGS for a human: (a) **the SQL has not been through `db:reset`** — every changed migration parses (`@libpg-query/parser`, PL/pgSQL included) and a rolled-back probe on `prj-67o7d44y6x` passed (a client-business member converts, sanitizes: 2 stages + 2 rooms deleted, 1 stage archived; re-pointing the client business, a stranger's sanitize and a direct history-probe call all refused; a second sanitize is a no-op; the Task cap refuses a new stage); (b) the brief's `.ui-nav-item__dot` does not exist — `NavItem`'s `dot` prop renders no element despite its docblock — so the Overview keeps its pulsing `.pjd-dot`; (c) the brief's `in_revision` / `approved` / `ready` are the schema's `revision_requested` / `accepted` / `todo`, worded at the surface rather than renamed; (d) "one room" was applied to shared project rooms: private threads and a group session's cohort sub-groups (#48) are still listed; (e) the sanitizer keeps a Session's stages (deleting them would delete its sessions) — the brief said "stages and channels"; (f) the history test counts any seat, invitation or finance row, so most real extra stages will archive rather than delete; (g) ~90 definer and label reads (finance labels, profiles, search, explore) still see archived stages — classified as history, not filtered; (h) `files.fn_can_read` still has no project-attachment arm, so a client's privately stored reference file stays hidden from freelancers in Project files (needs the attachment INSERT policy tightened first, flagged earlier the same day); (i) a reviewer's stub edits land in their own write-store bucket, so the owner does not see them in `dev:mock`; (j) the People list folds `member` into Freelancer and omits `guest`; (k) the brief named `UserAvatar` "via `@projective/ui`" — it is the app's `@web/components/UserAvatar.tsx` over `@projective/ui`'s `Avatar`. | root `CLAUDE.md` §1/§2/§3/§5/§6/§8 · `PRODUCT_SPEC.md` §Tasks (one conversation) · `PRODUCT_MANAGEMENT.md` (stage and room archive) · `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §C.1 (`LaneIconButton`), §B.4, §B.9, §B.11 · `ROUTING.md` · `database/projects/{Tables,Functions,Policies}.md` · `database/comms/{Tables,Policies}.md` · `supabase/migrations/{00000015,00000016,00001100,00001130,00001145,00001150,00001300,00001820,00002011,00002012,00002510}` · `packages/types/projects/{detail,files,members,workspace}.ts` · `packages/backend/services/projects/{live-detail,live-support,live-writes,live-files,live-overview,live-members,live-board,live-queries,live-submissions,detail-fixtures,files-fixtures,overview-fixtures,members-fixtures,write-store,ProjectBackendService}.ts` + `workspace-compose.test.ts`, `live-files.test.ts` · `packages/ui/navigation/{components/LaneChrome.tsx,styles/lane.css}` · `apps/web/routes/(dashboard)/projects/[projectSlug]/{index,details,preview,_middleware}.tsx`, `[channelId]/details.tsx` · `apps/web/features/projects/{core/{project-access,files-ssr,channel-header-slot,stage-details-footer-slot}.ts(x),components/{detail-glyphs,ProjectRail}.tsx,components/overview/{ProjectOverviewScreen,OverviewBlocks,BriefBlocks,WorkSignalBlocks}.tsx,islands/{ProjectSidebar,ProjectAttachments}.island.tsx,styles/{project-dashboard,project-sidebar}.css}` · Decisions #48 / #61 / #89 / #121 / #133 / #135 / #144 |
| 164 | **The Overview's single-task and layout pass — a Task's lane loses its body, the Overview stops speaking of stages for a Task, Inclusions & terms moves to the aside above People, the Project files grid tightens, and the lifecycle pill becomes a solid fill (2026-10-10).** Owner directive. **(A) THE TASK LANE.** `TaskLanePanel` (its Overview section — status · due date · owner · assignee — and its Task lists section) is removed from the Project Details lane for a Task; the lane ends at its top tier and drops the divider above the absent body. The panel, its sections, `core/task-lane{,-ssr}.ts` (+ test) and `styles/task-lane.css` are deleted, and the dashboard layout no longer pays the board read that fed it. **(B) NO STAGE TALK ON A TASK.** `fetchProjectOverview` omits the "N stage(s)" hero meta for a Task (`isTaskProject`), and the participant's Your work block draws no stage meter for one (`workspace.task`). **(C) LAYOUT.** Inclusions & terms renders in the aside directly above People (its rows stack in the two-column layout, where the aside is ~19rem); the Project files grid's track minimum drops from `9.5rem` to `7rem` with a `--space-3` gap — 4 cards where 3 fitted in a 494px main column (a 930px Overview), and one more per row at most widths (`FileCard` has no padding and ellipsizes its name, so nothing clips). **(D) THE STATUS PILL.** `.pjd-status` moves from a 16% tint to a SOLID fill under the role's `--on-<role>` ink, as §B.11.6 asks of a lifecycle status: Active = success, Draft (setup incomplete) = warning, Cancelled = danger, On hold and Completed = neutral gray (`--on-surface-variant` 80% over `--surface`, `--surface` ink). The raw semantic pairs measure ~3.17:1 in light mode, so each role fill is mixed 30% toward `--on-surface` — darker in light, lighter in dark — measuring ≥ 5.15:1 (neutral ≥ 5.30:1) in all four mode/contrast states; pinned by `status-fill.test.ts`. **FLAGS:** (a) a Task's task lists (checklist and delivery steps) are no longer surfaced anywhere in its chrome — a Task has no Board (#134), so they are reachable only inside the ticket view; (b) the brief's "Inactive / Archived" has no `ProjectStatus` of its own — mapped to `on_hold` + `completed`, and `cancelled` keeps its danger role as a solid fill; (c) the dev-mock overview fixtures never emitted a stage count, so (B) is observable only on the live path. | root `CLAUDE.md` §3 (gates 5, 6, 7, 12) · `PRODUCT_SPEC.md` §Tasks · `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §B.4.2, §B.11.3, §B.11.6 · `packages/backend/services/projects/live-overview.ts` · `apps/web/routes/(dashboard)/_layout.tsx` · `apps/web/features/projects/{islands/ProjectSidebar.island.tsx,components/overview/ProjectOverviewScreen.tsx,styles/project-dashboard.css,core/status-fill.test.ts}` · Decisions #121 / #133 / #134 / #144 / #163 |
| 165 | **The work-start gate: no hold, no work — a freelancer may claim or start a ticket only once its escrow is held; a refused hold refuses the claim and moves the ticket to Needs funding (2026-10-10). Product-owner ruling, decision only — no code in this change.** **(A) THE SKIP BECOMES A REFUSAL.** `finance.fn_hold_ticket_escrow` (`00001200_functions_finance_core.sql`) today returns NULL and lets the claim succeed when there is no price, no payee, no payer wallet in the project's currency, or an INDIVIDUAL payer cannot cover the hold (a business that cannot cover it already refuses through `fn_wallet_debit`, and the spending-limit check already raises). Every one of those cases becomes a refusal: the claim fails and the ticket moves to **Needs funding**, off every freelancer's board (consistent with §Ticket Movement's "freelancers never see an unpaid ticket"), until the client clears it with a wallet hold or a card Buy Now. Chosen over keeping a reservation for the refused claimant (no reservation concept exists; the claim TTL would need a second meaning). The "already held" early return is not a skip and is unchanged. **(B) LOCK TRIGGERS** follow PRODUCT_SPEC §Escrow, Wallets & Finance #2: One-Off / Task upfront (when the project or first stage begins — its seat is hired by then), Pipeline at **claim** (§Escrow Lifecycle for Tickets is authoritative; the #2 table's "In Progress" wording is amended to say so), Sessions at booking. **(C) THE STATE MACHINE** `Draft → Needs funding → Payment processing → Funded → In progress → In review → Released`, `Failed` (retryable) and `Refunded` (terminal per escrow), with who acts and what unlocks each step, is written to `PRODUCT_MANAGEMENT.md` §3.6 as a §3.5 domain lifecycle — never a board column. **(D) `finance.escrows.payer_business_id` IS NULLABLE** — checked against `00000017_tables_finance.sql`: since Decision #126 (2026-09-29) the column has no NOT NULL and `ck_escrows_one_payer` requires exactly one of `payer_business_id` / `payer_user_id`, which is what lets `fn_hold_ticket_escrow` hold for an individual client. The contrary statements in `decisions-044-056.md` (#56 flag (a)) and `SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE.md` ("Paid calls have no escrow path yet") were stale and are corrected; paid calls are still blocked, but by `project_stage_id NOT NULL` alone. **FLAGGED — for a human:** (a) Needs funding has no storage yet — planned as a `needs_funding` member on the payment axis (`payment_status`, enum edited in place + `@projective/types`), decided when the gate is built; (b) a Pipeline ticket paid for ahead of a claim leaves its money in the payer wallet UNEARMARKED, so a later claim can still be refused if the wallet was spent elsewhere — an earmark/reservation is a separate decision; (c) the Sessions booking lock works only for project-backed sessions while standalone calls have no escrow row (D); (d) this lifecycle's `Draft` is local to it — it is not the §3.1 `Draft` nor `project_status = 'draft'` — and the UI label for a prepaid, unclaimed Pipeline ticket is undecided; (e) `projects.claim_ticket`, `fund_stage`, `assign_ticket` and the automation claim all route through the hold today and will all start refusing together. | `PRODUCT_SPEC.md` §Escrow, Wallets & Finance #2 + §Escrow Lifecycle for Tickets · `PRODUCT_MANAGEMENT.md` §3.5 (row) + §3.6 · `SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE.md` (paid-call escrow note) · `decisions-044-056.md` #56(a) (stale marker) · Decisions #56 / #94 / #125 / #126 |
| 166 | **Single-ticket Buy Now — a purchase can fund ONE ticket, not only its whole stage (2026-10-10). Product-owner ruling, decision only — no code in this change. AMENDS #140(C).** PRODUCT_SPEC §Purchase Methods promises "single-ticket purchase and escrow lock", but `finance.begin_card_payment`'s `escrow_lock` funds a stage (every assigned, unpaid ticket in it) and a `project_ticket` basket line is keyed on (project, stage) — which is why #140(C) sent the board's Buy now to the `?project_id=` checkout instead of adding lines. **(A) SCHEMA (planned, lands with its Zod and `documentation/database/finance/*` in one change):** a nullable `ticket_id uuid REFERENCES projects.tickets (id)` on `finance.basket_items`, `finance.order_lines` and `finance.inbound_payments` (edited in place in `00000017_tables_finance.sql`), which must belong to the row's stage (`stage_id` / `project_stage_id`). **(B) SEMANTICS:** an `escrow_lock` with a `ticket_id` holds escrow for that ticket alone when it has a payee (claimed, or its stage seat held); when it has none — an unclaimed Pipeline ticket — the settled money stays credited to the payer wallet, the ticket's funding scope is set, and the hold happens at claim under #165. Stage funding stays a separate action. Chosen over offering Buy Now only once a payee exists. **FLAGGED — for a human:** (a) the no-payee path is a prepayment, not an escrow lock, and inherits #165 flag (b) (no earmark); (b) once built, #140(C)'s board Buy now can add a per-ticket line instead of the project-wide handoff; (c) `inbound_payments.locked_escrow_ids` keeps working unchanged (one id for a single-ticket lock). **BUILT (2026-10-10).** **Schema** (edited in place, `00000017`): `ticket_id` on `finance.basket_items` (FK CASCADE; CHECK `basket_item_ticket_is_project_work` — project kinds only, `quantity = 1`), `finance.order_lines` (FK SET NULL) and `finance.inbound_payments` (FK SET NULL; CHECK `inbound_payments_ticket_only_for_locks`); the dedupe key `uq_basket_items_live_ticket (basket_id, ticket_id)` over live lines (`00004005`). **Doors:** new `projects.fund_ticket(project, ticket)` (`00001150`; `authenticated` only) — fund_stage's payer rule, then the PRODUCT_SPEC §5 purchasing gate (no description → `PU422`), unpaid/`needs_funding` and no held escrow, an unfinished stage, a price, a payee, an individual's wallet cover; exactly one `fn_hold_ticket_escrow`; **the stage's status is not moved**. `finance.begin_card_payment` gains `p_ticket_id DEFAULT NULL` (amount = that ticket's price, fund_ticket's gates checked before any charge; the stage path is untouched; NULLs are skipped in the request hash so older keys replay) and `settle_card_payment` runs `fund_ticket` as the payer when the payment names a ticket. `finance.place_wallet_order` settles a ticket line by `fund_ticket` (escrow hold from the same wallet, no seller credit, no fee until release, `order_payment` debit only for products, `fulfilment = 'engagement'` with `ticket_id`); the basket's owner must be the project's client (`PU422`); a promo with a ticket line is `PC409`; the `PS501` refusal remains for every other non-product kind — so a card checkout of tickets is the #153 top-up then the webhook's `place_wallet_order`, idempotent on the order key as before. **App:** `AddBasketItemSchema.ticketId` / `BasketItem.ticketId` / `InboundPayment.ticketId` / `CreateEscrowLockIntent.ticketId`; `BasketBackendService.addTicket` (kind from the project's format, price from the ticket, re-adding re-selects the line); the basket read prices a ticket line from its ticket and says before Pay why one cannot be bought (`ticketPurchase`, the same gates); `CheckoutBackendService` drops `WALLET_SETTLES` for `walletSettles()` and the `no_provider` blocker for ticket lines; the board's "Checkout N" adds the unfunded tickets first (#140(C) RESOLVED). **VERIFIED:** `deno task db:reset` clean; `supabase/probes/single_ticket_purchase.sql` (rolled back) on the reset DB — three tickets in one stage: `fund_ticket` held only B (25,000 from the stage price), wallet −25,000, A and C unpaid, stage status unchanged; a second fund `PC409`; no description `PU422`; no payee `PU422`; a card lock naming A with a wrong expected amount `PC409`, then credited and held exactly 10,000 with `locked_escrow_ids` = A's escrow and C still unpaid; a second live basket line for one ticket `23505`; a wallet order for C debited 30,000 as escrow (no `order_payment` debit) with one `engagement` line carrying `ticket_id`; 8/8 PASS. `ticket-purchase.test.ts` 6/6 (one of three funded → only its price held, the other two still buyable at theirs). **FLAGGED — for a human:** (d) **the no-payee prepayment (B) is NOT built** — an unclaimed ticket with no team seat is refused at payment ("it can be bought once it's claimed") and shown so on its basket line, because #166(B)'s "funding scope is set" needs storage nobody has decided (#165 flag (a)); (e) a single freelancer's stage seat is not a payee yet — `fn_hold_ticket_escrow` resolves only a claimant or an accepted team seat, so "its stage seat is held" is built for team seats only; (f) a card checkout tops up the WHOLE charge (as #153), not only the wallet's shortfall — charging the shortfall would make the rail's "charged" figure disagree with its copy, a #153 surface change; the wallet route is offered whenever it covers; (g) a promo code is refused at Pay (`PC409`) on an order with a ticket line, not before it — no pre-Pay blocker yet; (h) `fund_ticket` funds a ticket in any unfinished stage status (including `open`), unlike `fund_stage`'s `assigned`-only rule. | `PRODUCT_SPEC.md` §Purchase Methods · `PRODUCT_MANAGEMENT.md` §3.6 · Decisions #125 / #140 / #153 / #165 · `SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE.md` §Integration Blueprints 1.1 · `documentation/database/{finance/Tables,finance/Functions,projects/Functions}.md` · `supabase/migrations/{00000017,00001150,00001210,00001230,00002510,00004005}*` · `packages/types/finance/{basket,payments}.ts` · `packages/backend/services/finance/{live-basket,BasketBackendService,CheckoutBackendService,PaymentBackendService,live-payments}.ts` (+ `ticket-purchase.test.ts`) · `apps/web/routes/api/basket/item.ts` · `apps/web/features/checkout/core/BasketService.ts` · `apps/web/features/projects/islands/ProjectBoard.island.tsx` · `supabase/probes/single_ticket_purchase.sql` |
| 167 | **Paid revisions — `basket_items.revision_id` targets `projects.stage_revision_requests.id`, and a new purchasable kind `paid_revision` is priced from the project's source blueprint (2026-10-10). Product-owner ruling, decision only — no code in this change. CLOSES #64–#69 flag (f) once built.** **(A) TARGET:** `finance.basket_items.revision_id` gets its FK to `projects.stage_revision_requests (id)` (a client-requested revision round on a ticket), not a submission revision. **(B) KIND:** `finance.purchasable_item_kind` gains `paid_revision`, appended in the enum and `@projective/types/finance` member-for-member in the same order. **(C) PRICE:** `projects.projects.source_blueprint_id → marketplace.service_blueprints.extra_revision_price_cents`; offered only after the blueprint's `free_revisions` are used; `0` = extra rounds free; NULL price, or a project with no source blueprint (owner-authored), cannot sell paid revisions. Chosen over `catalogue.listings`' copy of the same column. **(D) MONEY:** the purchase is held as escrow on the ticket (payee = the ticket's payee) and released when the revised work is approved, like any ticket escrow (#165's machine, `In review`). **FLAGGED — for a human:** (a) `extra_revision_price_cents` and `free_revisions` exist on BOTH `marketplace.service_blueprints` and `catalogue.listings` and can disagree — the blueprint is now canonical; the listing copy should be removed or derived; (b) `projects.stage_revision_requests.project_stage_id` has no FK and `status` / `request_type` are free text with no CHECK; (c) the count of revision rounds used is not stored anywhere today; (d) a paid round cannot be bought before the free rounds are exhausted, by this ruling. **AS BUILT (2026-10-10).** **(E) TERMS ON THE STAGE — AMENDS (C).** `project_stages.file_revisions_allowed` (now `NULL` = unlimited, the owner-authored default), `extra_revision_price_cents` and `revisions_used`; `projects.fn_stage_revision_terms` (BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF the terms) copies the seller's terms on a project with a `source_blueprint_id` — blueprint, else its newest published listing, with `revisionsOf`'s display fallbacks (once either is declared: free → 0, price → the stage's `unit_price_cents`) — and freezes them, so every creation path (create_project, create_stage, hire, purchase) is covered by one trigger and neither the buyer nor a reprice rewrites the purchase's terms. Chosen over reading the blueprint live, which (C) said: a live read lets a seller's later reprice change an engagement already bought. **(F) THE COUNT.** `review_submission` locks the stage row; a round is free while unlimited, `used < allowed` or the price is `0` (then `revisions_used + 1`); otherwise `PR402`, `DETAIL {code: 'paid_revision_required', price_minor, currency, used, allowed}`, before anything is written. A bought round does not consume the allotment. **(G) THE PAID ROUND.** `stage_revision_requests` gains the stage FK, `submission_id`, `price_cents` and CHECKs (`status` `awaiting_payment` · `open` · `resolved` · `cancelled`; `request_type` `revision` · `paid_revision`; price iff paid; `resolved_at` iff resolved); `request_paid_revision` raises one round `awaiting_payment` per submission at the snapshotted price; its `paid_revision` basket line (`revision_id` FK, `= item_id`) is paid through Buy Now / Basket Checkout → `place_wallet_order` → `fund_paid_revision` → `finance.fn_hold_revision_escrow` (#165's PF402 shape), and only then is the work sent back; `submit_deliverable` resolves open rounds and writes `revision_of`; a verdict cancels a round still awaiting payment. **(H) NOTIFY + READ.** `submission.revision_requested` to the submitter ("Paid revision, funded, you can start." on a paid round) and `ticket.revision_requested` to the ticket's assignee when someone else; "View revisions" admits review authority, the ticket's assignee and the submitter. **(I) SPELLING.** The database keeps `revisions_requested`; the Zod `revision_requested` and the one mapper `toSubmissionStatus` stay (PRODUCT_MANAGEMENT §3.7) — renaming would touch the CHECK, standing, nav-activity and the seeds for no behaviour change. **(J) UI.** `SubmissionReviewModal` "{used} of {allowed} free revisions used" and "Buy a revision · {price}"; the Create Submission tickets are read (`SubmissionListPage.tickets`), not hash-generated; Dev Context Switcher `revisionAllotment` (auto · exhausted · paid). CLOSES flags (b), (c) and #64–#69(f). **FLAGGED — for a human:** (e) the allotment is per STAGE as asked — on a Pipeline stage every ticket shares one pool, where a listing's "N free revisions" may have meant per ticket; (f) flag (a) stands: the listing copy can still disagree with the blueprint, and the copy now prefers the blueprint; (g) the live blueprint→project instantiation does not exist yet (`draft-store.ts` is in-memory), so in practice only seeded or future blueprint projects carry terms; (h) an owner-authored stage with a finite allotment and no price can never be sent back past it (#167(C) forbids selling); (i) a paid round's escrow is a second held row on the ticket, so a Fair Exit splits it with the work's escrow; (j) `project_stages` grants still let an owner write the two terms on an owner-authored stage after work started. | `PRODUCT_SPEC.md` §Purchase Methods · `PRODUCT_MANAGEMENT.md` §3.6 / §3.7 · Decisions #64–#69 (f) / #165 / #166 |
| 168 | **Monthly business invoices are issued on the business's `billing_day` (1–28, default the 1st); wallet statements stay on the 1st (2026-10-10). Product-owner ruling, docs only.** PRODUCT_SPEC §Escrow, Wallets & Finance #4 and `finance-model.md` §15 said "on the 1st", while `org.business_profiles.billing_day` allows 1–28 and is already collected (`InvoicingSetup`) and written (`finance.set_invoicing_terms`). The two "1st"s were different documents: PRODUCT_SPEC's is the consolidated **invoice** (`finance.invoices`, `consolidated_monthly`), finance-model's is the wallet **statement** (`finance.statements`). Ruling: the invoice runs on `billing_day`, the 1st when NULL; statements stay on the 1st. The 28 cap keeps every month's run on a real date. Chosen over a fixed 1st (which would have dropped a shipped control). **FLAGGED — for a human:** (a) nothing issues invoices on `billing_day` yet — no job reads the column; the invoice run must when built; (b) a change of `billing_day` mid-period — whether the current period stretches or splits — is undecided. | `PRODUCT_SPEC.md` §Escrow, Wallets & Finance #4 · `finance-model.md` §15 |
| 169 | **The funding status UI — the client is told when and how to pay, and both sides when work can start (2026-10-10).** Built on #165's server-derived ticket funding state (`deriveFundingState`, shipped on `BoardCard.funding`), which no island derives. **(A) COMPONENT:** `FundingStatus` (`features/projects/components/`; copy, actions and the dev preview in `core/funding-ui.ts`, tested) — a lifecycle tag (solid role fill on the `.pjd-status` recipe), one sentence (`aria-live="polite"`, `tabular-nums`) and at most one decision's controls: client Buy now (`accent`) + Add to basket (`neutral` outlined) · Review · Retry from wallet; payee Start work, rendered DISABLED with the sentence as its description while the ticket is not funded (§3 gate 11). A `processing` line re-reads `GET /api/projects/ticket?slug=` every 3s. The Dev Context Switcher's `fundingState` axis (plumbing by the #165 change) previews every state. **(B) PLACEMENTS:** the board card (compact: the tag for Draft · Needs funding · Processing · Failed, an icon check for Funded, nothing the column already says), the List view, the ticket modal's footer — moved into the Finances tab while that tab is open, one home at a time — `StageInspector` (status only, plus the stage's "{n} need {total} before work can start"), the board header (`InlineNotice` "{n} tickets need {total} to start." + Fund all → #166's per-ticket basket hand-off) and the owner's Overview "Ready to start?" (`readinessOf`: brief written → freelancer assigned → first stage funded → work started; gone once work starts). The card's Draft chip, its Unpaid/Paid marks and the board island's own `needsFunding` predicate are removed. **(C) BUY NOW:** `BuyNowTarget.ticket?` adds the line by ticket (#166), narrows the session to the project, states "Held in escrow, released only when you approve." and the server's wallet balance / shortfall / who pays (`CheckoutWallet`); a card charge needing collection mounts Stripe's Payment Element in the modal (publishable key from the server handoff), waits for the webhook, then places the wallet order under the same attempt key — for listings too. On the ticket chain it is a REPLACING `buy` frame (`pushTicketBuy`, §B.10.9); success re-reads the ticket, so the line says Funded in place. **(D) CONFIRMATION:** "What happens next" lists each ticket line with "{payee} has been notified and can start now" (new read field `OrderLine.payeeName`, resolved from the ticket's assignee in `live-orders`). **(E) WALLET:** a held or clearing amount links to its ticket (`finance.escrows.ticket_id` → `/projects/{slug}/board?tkv=`); a stage-wide hold keeps its stage row. **FLAGGED — for a human:** (a) Buy Now funds through the CHECKOUT (a card tops the wallet up; the wallet order holds through `projects.fund_ticket`), not the bare escrow intent (`POST /api/finance/escrow/intent` + `ticketId`) the brief named — the checkout already carries the wallet split and the order; the intent door stays unused by the UI; (b) "Payment didn't lock … The money is in your wallet." is true only for a lock refused after settlement — a declined card leaves nothing in the wallet, and the funding state does not say which happened; Retry from wallet re-opens Buy Now rather than a wallet-only hold; (c) "has been notified" rests on #165's `escrow.funded` notification (run through `db:reset` and proven by a rolled-back probe, per the #165/#170 session) — a database-level fact, not a delivery guarantee: `fn_notify` swallows its own failures; (d) narrowing by project means Buy Now on one ticket also charges any other selected line for that project already in the basket (the modal lists them) — there is no per-ticket narrowing token; (e) the wallet's transaction ledger still links escrow lines to the stage, not the ticket; (f) under `dev:mock` the basket, checkout, confirmation and wallet are live-only, so Buy Now, Add to basket and Fund all show their refusal there; (g) focus does not return to Buy now when the `buy` frame pops; (h) Start work is the stage board's status move to `in_progress` — app code calls no claim/start RPC (`projects.claim_ticket` is unused); (i) "Work started {date}" reads `claimed_at` — no start instant is stored; (j) a ticket paid ahead of its claim (#165: `draft` + `none`) reads "Paid" with the server's reason, but only the mock path can produce it — the live schema has no paid-ahead record. | `PRODUCT_MANAGEMENT.md` §3.6 · `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §C.1 / §B.10.9 / §B.11 · Decisions #125 / #153 / #165 / #166 |
| 170 | **The work-start gate built — #165 lands in the database, one derivation reads it everywhere, and a pending card payment can be polled (2026-10-10).** **(A) REFUSAL, NOT SKIP:** `finance.fn_hold_ticket_escrow` raises `PF402` "Needs funding — …" with `DETAIL` `{state: 'needs_funding', reason: no_price \| no_wallet \| insufficient_funds \| spending_limit, amount_minor, currency}`; both wallet types are balance-checked before the escrow row is written; the spending limit moves from `55000` to `PF402`; a missing stage or payee is `check_violation`; "already held" still returns `NULL` (#165(A)). On success it notifies the payee ("Funded — you can start") and the client unless the client made the hold (`escrow.funded`). **(B) THE GATE** lives on `trg_ticket_escrow_sync`: entering `claimed` / `in_progress` / `in_review` from outside work without a `held` escrow raises `PF402` (`reason: 'not_held'`), so `claim_ticket`, `assign_ticket_manual`, `auto_assign_round_robin`, `assign_parallel_stream`, `move_ticket` and a direct row write all meet one rule; moves between work states are not gated. **(C) STORAGE:** `payment_status` gains `needs_funding` (enum edited in place, resolving #165 flag (a)); `projects.fn_mark_needs_funding` (revoked from client roles) writes it and sends the client `escrow.needs_funding` (new catalog key). `fn_assign_ticket_core` runs the assignment in a subtransaction: a refused hold is rolled back, recorded, and the core returns `NULL` — **a refusal cannot be both recorded and raised in one transaction, so `NULL` is the refusal** for `claim_ticket` / `assign_ticket_manual`; round-robin answers `{assigned: false, needs_funding: true}` and orders needs-funding tickets last; the parallel stream leaves them in the pool. **(D) ONE-OFF CHAIN:** `force_complete_stage` keeps the approval and the released installment when the next stage's hold is refused; the ticket waits there as `todo`, `needs_funding`. `fund_stage` and `begin_card_payment` treat `needs_funding` as unfunded; `fund_stage` drops its own `stage_funded` notice (each hold now notifies). `fn_flag_bad_faith_report` no longer resumes an evicted ticket to `in_progress` (that would be work with no hold). **(E) ONE DERIVATION:** `deriveFundingState(ticket, escrow, inboundPayment)` (`packages/backend/services/finance/funding-state.ts`, `FundingStatusSchema` / `FundingRollupSchema` / `PaymentFundingPollSchema` in `@projective/types/finance`, every §3.6 transition unit-tested) with `rollupFundingState`, stamped on `BoardCard.funding`, `BoardStageRef.funding`, `ProjectAssignment.funding`, `ProjectOverview.funding` and `ProjectDetail.funding` on the live and fixture paths (`fixtureFunding`); a ticket paid for ahead of a claim (`paidAhead`) reads Draft with nothing to fund — fixture-only, since no live column records a pre-claim purchase; the board reads the newest escrow-lock card payment per ticket (#166 `ticket_id`) else per stage, under the payer's RLS. `GET /api/finance/payments/[id]/status` (thin, Zod, auth) answers `{paymentId, status, lockStatus, funding}`. The stub mirrors the gate (402, `errors.status = 'needs_funding'`) and `refusalFrom` maps the sentence to 402. **(F) DEV AXIS:** `fundingState` (DevOverrides · DEV_DEFAULTS · `DEV_FUNDING_STATES` · the "Funding state" control · `data-dev-funding-state` set and delete · the seam's `fundingState`), consumed by #169's component. Verified: `db:reset` clean; a rolled-back probe proved the refused claim (unassigned, `needs_funding`, client notified with reason and amount), the refused drag, the `no_price` detail, the funded claim (held, both parties notified) and the start of work. **FLAGGED — for a human:** (a) claim RPCs answer a refusal with `NULL`, not an error — any future caller must read `NULL` as "refused, Needs funding"; (b) the §3.6 **Needs funding → Draft** edge (funds in place, no payee) is not automatic — the ticket stays `needs_funding` until a hold succeeds; (c) `fn_assign_ticket_core` is still `PUBLIC`-executable (#143), so an anonymous caller can now also trigger `escrow.needs_funding` notices through it; (d) one seed ticket (`ecc69a9e…`, `in_progress`, `unpaid`) predates the gate and derives `needs_funding`; (e) the escrow status `funded` (the column default) is read as held; (f) the overview's roll-up covers only the viewer's own tickets, the detail's every ticket for the client side and a provider's own tickets otherwise. | `PRODUCT_MANAGEMENT.md` §3.6 · `PRODUCT_SPEC.md` §Escrow, Wallets & Finance #2 · `database/finance/Functions.md` · `database/projects/Functions.md` / `Tables.md` · Decisions #125 / #143 / #165 / #166 / #169 |
| 171 | **The overlay layer made one family — a pinned `footer` slot on Drawer (and Dialog's made safe-area aware), a visible focus ring inside every overlay as a scoped exception to 152a27a, passive stack claims for persistent windows, and every remaining menu/sheet on BodyPortal + `useOverlayStack` (2026-10-10). AMENDS DESIGN_SYSTEM §B.10.3 / §B.10.6 / §B.10.8 and the §C.1 roster.** At the product owner's direction (the overlay-layer brief). **(A) FOOTER.** `Drawer` gains `footer`, rendered by the stylesheet-free `DrawerRegions` as the scroll body's sibling (`.ui-drawer__footer`: `flex: none`, one `--hairline`, `padding-block-end: max(var(--space-4), env(safe-area-inset-bottom))`); Dialog already had the slot and its footer gains the same safe-area term. The basket's totals + CTAs and the notifications drawer's "View all" move into it; the ad-hoc widths (`min(27rem, 94vw)`, `min(25rem, 92vw)`, `min(20rem, 88vw)`) are dropped for `--drawer-w`. **(B) FOCUS — the owner chose the scoped option.** The product-wide removal in `apps/web/styles/global.css` (152a27a) STANDS; one rule exempts `[data-ui-portal] :is(a[href], button, summary, [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])):focus-visible` (minus the fields that already spend `--fld-focus-glow`) with a new token pair `--overlay-focus-ring` / `--overlay-focus-offset` (2px `--fld-focus-fg` outline; 3px `--on-surface` under high contrast). The package's own close/action buttons spend the same pair. Product-wide restoration was offered and declined. **(C) STACK.** `useOverlayStack` gains `passive` (claims a z-slot, never `isTop`) and `raise()`. `DraggablePopover` drops its module counter for a passive draggable claim, so dropdowns/menus opened inside the chat pop-out or a dev window step above it while a modal beside it keeps Escape; the message menus' `calc(var(--z-draggable) + 1)` is gone. Tooltip and the toast band stay static by design (commented). **(D) PORTALS + ESCAPE.** `ContextMenu`, `Menu`, `MobileMenu`, `CommandPalette` render through `BodyPortal`. `MessageContextMenu` (an Escape layer only while focus is inside it), `FormatBubble`, `ReactionBubble`, `MessageSelectionBar` (the selection's layer — Escape ends it), `CardActions` and the three search-scope dropdowns move onto `useOverlayStack` + `useDismiss`. `InspectorSheet` renders on `Portal` at a `modal` slot and re-provides `InspectorActionsContext` across the portal root (not `Drawer` — Drawer has no half/full snap or swipe). **(E) GLASS.** `.shell-drawer`'s 92% veil + element-level `backdrop-filter` is removed rather than moved to an underlay: a blur behind a solid panel renders nothing. **FLAGGED — for a human:** (a) RESOLVED 2026-10-11 — the marketing `SiteHeader` mobile drawer is rebuilt on `Drawer` (header "Menu", Sign in / Get started in the pinned `footer`; its static `--z-backdrop`/`--z-overlay` layers, `rgba(0, 0, 0, …)` scrim, bespoke Escape/scroll-lock/focus effect and the header's `--menu-open` z lift are deleted), and its search-scope menu joins the stack; (b) RESOLVED 2026-10-11 — `MobileMenu` reads the one `--scrim` recipe, `--drawer-w` and a solid `--surface`; (c) RESOLVED 2026-10-11 — all FOUR search-scope menus (explore results, hero, user shell, marketing header) are body-portalled `.ui-anchored` panels positioned by `useEdgeDetection` at their stack slot, browser-verified anchored 8px under the trigger with Escape / outside-press / keyboard roving intact; (d) the devtools layer stays at `--z-max` on purpose. | DESIGN_SYSTEM §B.10.3 / §B.10.6 / §B.10.8 / §C.1 · `packages/ui/hooks/useOverlayStack.ts` · `apps/web/styles/global.css` · Decisions #159 / #162 |
| 172 | **Business auto-funding, the low-balance warning, the monthly invoice run and paying on account (2026-10-10). Product-owner ruling on four questions put in chat the same day, then built.** PRODUCT_SPEC §Escrow, Wallets & Finance #4 gains Auto-funding, the Low-balance warning, What an invoice asks for and Paying on account; before this they existed only as the words "low balance" in `finance-model.md` §15's notification list. **(A) FROM WALLET IS A QUEUE, NOT A RETRY.** A ticket enters Needs funding only after a hold from the business wallet was refused (no funds, the owner's spending limit, or no payee once the refused claim is rolled back), so a From-wallet hold tried at that instant always fails. Ruling: the ticket joins the business's auto-fund queue (no storage — the Needs-funding tickets of `from_wallet` projects) and every credit to the wallet except an on-account credit retries it, oldest ticket first (`finance.fn_auto_fund_drain`, fired by `trg_transactions_wallet_watch`). A ticket with a payee is held as the project owner (`escrows.auto_funded`); one without returns to Draft so its next claim holds. Holds stay inside the owner's spending limit; at or above the wallet's approval threshold an owner without `approve_spend` gets a spend approval and `decide_spend_approval` retries the queue on approve. `add_to_basket` writes the business's default-basket line in SQL — the same row `BasketBackendService.addTicket` writes — because the refusal happens inside a freelancer's claim, where the owner's basket session is not reachable. Mode on `org.business_profiles.auto_fund_mode`, override on `projects.auto_fund_override`. **(B) THE INVOICE TOTAL IS ESCROW + BONUS + TAX.** The brief asked for the total to include the fee; the platform fee is deducted from the payee at release (`fn_release_ticket_escrow`), so adding it would bill it twice. The fee is a line shown as included. `fn_generate_consolidated_invoice` now selects by the new `escrows.released_at`, issues one invoice per currency with the platform as issuer (`issue_from_profile` NULL, relaxed in place), sets `due_date` (`invoice_due_days` = 14) and `amount_due_cents`, is idempotent on `uq_invoices_business_period_currency`, and notifies `invoice.issued`. **(C) PAYING ON ACCOUNT: THE PLATFORM FRONTS A WALLET CREDIT.** Wallet-funded escrow is already paid, so an invoice owes only what was bought on account. Checkout's Invoice route (`finance.place_on_account_order`) credits the business wallet (`on_account_credit`) and places the wallet order in one transaction, so every hold still comes from a wallet (#165 rule 2); KYB-verified, monthly invoicing, nothing overdue, owed ≤ `on_account_limit_cents` (25,000.00 per currency, product-owner figure). Pay = `finance.pay_invoice` (`invoice_payment` debit). **(D) THE RUN** is `finance.fn_run_billing_cycle` on pg_cron daily 02:20 UTC: each monthly business's last closed period (a missed day catches up), last month's statements for every wallet (`finance.fn_generate_statement`, balances derived from sums), and the overdue sweep (`invoice.overdue`). **(E) LOW BALANCE** is `low_balance_floor_cents` on the default-currency wallet; the debit that crosses it warns `add_funds` holders once (read off the debit itself, so it re-arms on recovery) and runs any `below_floor` deposit rule (`finance.deposit_interval` edited in place); the queue outgrowing the balance warns once too. **(F) CHECKOUT** — `checkout-model.ts` and `PaymentChoice.tsx` said Invoice is never a per-purchase instrument; it now joins the instrument radiogroup only once monthly invoicing is arranged on Details. **(G) UI** — the business console Billing module (`BillingScreen`), the project Details Auto-funding field, FundingStatus's "Auto-funded from wallet" and "Will be invoiced on {date}" lines, the Dev Context Switcher `autoFundMode` / `lowBalance` axes. `chargeback.won` / `chargeback.lost` were already in the catalog. **FLAGGED — for a human:** (a) tax is 0 on every invoice until tax collection lands; (b) the From-wallet queue does not reserve money — a payee-less ticket returned to Draft can be refused again at claim (#165 flag (b)); (c) the project override can be set by anyone with review authority, not only `manage_billing` (the owner's envelope still bounds every hold); (d) an approved auto-fund request is reused if the same ticket is re-pooled later; (e) Pay is from the wallet — paying by card is a top-up first; (f) a business that switches to monthly invoicing mid-period is invoiced for the whole closed period it switched in (#168 flag (b) still open); (g) the floor is one figure on the default-currency wallet only; (h) local `supabase start` has no pg_cron — run `SELECT finance.fn_run_billing_cycle();` by hand. | PRODUCT_SPEC §Escrow, Wallets & Finance #4 + sitemap · `finance-model.md` §13 / §15 · `PRODUCT_MANAGEMENT.md` §3.6 · `database/finance/*` · `database/org/Tables.md` · `database/projects/Tables.md` · DESIGN_SYSTEM §C.1 · Decisions #125 / #153 / #165 / #166 / #168 |
| 173 | **The notification UI — the header drawer and `/notifications` read the real inbox, and live updates come from POLLING `/api/notifications/summary`, not a Realtime socket or an SSE route (2026-10-11).** **(A) THE CHOICE.** The brief offered a server-mediated SSE route (`GET /api/notifications/stream`, Supabase Realtime held server-side) or polling the summary, and asked for one with a reason. **Polling wins:** (1) it works identically under `deno task dev:mock` (fixtures have no Realtime) and live, so the one code path is the tested one; (2) an SSE route would hold one server-side Realtime socket per open tab, bound to an access token that expires mid-stream and must be re-minted inside a long-lived response — a session-refresh path nothing else in the app has; (3) the summary is one `SECURITY INVOKER` RPC over an indexed own-row read, so a 30 s cadence per VISIBLE tab (paused while hidden, immediate on return) costs little; (4) nothing in the inbox is latency-critical — chat has its own live channel (Decision #49), and a notification that lands within 30 s meets the product need. Islands stay free of Supabase either way. **(B) ONE POLLER PER PAGE.** `NotificationDrawer` (always mounted by `UserActions`) runs it and publishes the summary on a shared signal (`notification-state`); the header dot (`unseen > 0`, a dot never a count — DESIGN_SYSTEM §D.1) and the tab counts read it. A rise in `total` or `unseen` pulses open lists, which merge only rows newer than their head and announce them politely, rate-limited to one summed message per 8 s. **(C) API ADDITIONS.** `GET /api/notifications` gains `archived=true` (the Archived view reads `comms.notifications` under the same own-row policy, since the feed view hides archived rows) and every item carries `actor` (`{ name, avatar }` from `org.get_party_cards`, `null` for the platform) — `NotificationInboxItemSchema`. No schema change. **(D) THE SURFACES** follow the new DESIGN_SYSTEM §D.9 (written for this change): category tabs without Marketing, hairline rows with one inline meta line, unread on a solid ramp step (the translucent `.shell-feed__item--unread` wash is gone with the `nav-fixtures` list), mark-all scoped to the tab, per-row archive, bulk select on the page, deep-linked `?category=&view=`. Dev Context Switcher axis `notifications` (None · Many unread · Realtime burst). **Upgrade path:** if a sub-30 s latency is ever required, the same `publishSummary` seam accepts an SSE source without touching a surface. **FLAGGED — for a human:** (a) there is no un-archive (restore) control; (b) SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE's engine diagram still names Supabase Realtime as the in-app channel — true of the table's publication, but the app does not subscribe to it (noted there). | DESIGN_SYSTEM §D.1 · §D.9 · §C.1 · ROUTING.md · SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE §The Notification Engine · Decisions #49 / #57 |
| 174 | **Explore cards gain hover INTENT — one revealed meta line on rail cards, a press scale and a Star pop — while #77(H)'s "no media zoom" STANDS: the proposed hover-intent media scale was declined at sign-off (2026-10-12). AMENDS #77(H) for the reveal only; #77 flag (a) is RESOLVED as "the zooms stay removed".** Part of the brief raising `/explore` to a YouTube-Explore level of polish. **(A) WHAT WAS PROPOSED.** A transform-only `scale(1.03)` on the image inside a clipped media frame after 350ms of hover on a fine pointer, so the card box, hit area and siblings never move (the reason #77(H) gave for removing the lift). **SIGN-OFF (product owner, 2026-10-12): "Approve, no scale"** — the media and profile-cover zooms stay removed; the colour wash (14%, #77(H)) remains the only immediate hover signal and no card media transforms on hover. **(B) THE REVEALED META LINE (approved).** On a fine hovering pointer (`@media (hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)`), after **350ms** of continuous hover (CSS `transition-delay` on the way in, `0ms` on the way out — no timer, no JS, so a sweep across a rail never fires it), one second meta line (`--text-secondary`, middot meta, §3 gate 7) opens through a `grid-template-rows: 0fr → 1fr` row on `--spring-standard`. The line is always in the DOM and in the card's accessible content, so assistive tech never depends on hover; `:focus-within` reveals it with no delay. **RAILS ONLY (flag (a), owner's choice):** the rail scroller reserves the line's block size inside the section gap (`--ex-reveal-reserve`), so a card grows into space already paid for and the page never reflows; grids (`HomeGrid`, the results grid) do not reveal. **(C) PRESS AND STAR (approved).** Press is `transform: scale(.98)` on `--dur-press` (§3 gate 12, already legal); the Star's toggle pop is a scale on `--spring-snappy` (`cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1)` — no overshoot, so §B.5's ζ ≥ 1 holds without a #75(a)-style exception). **(D) REDUCED MOTION.** The global blocks zero durations, which would still JUMP to an end-state transform. So every transform here is `transform: none`, and the reveal row stays at rest (`0fr`, except `:focus-within`), under BOTH channels — `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` and `:root[data-motion="reduced"]` — matching gate 12's precedent. **(E) LIVE SIGNALS (flag (b), owner's choice).** "New" (published < 14 days) is inline middot meta text, not a pill (gate 7); "Available now" is the presence dot with a pulse that runs **three cycles then rests** (WCAG 2.2.2) and is absent under both reduced-motion channels; "0 delivered" is hidden. **(F) CARD → `/view` HERO MORPH (flag (c), owner's choice: ship).** A cross-document View Transition (`@view-transition { navigation: auto }`, Chromium-only, progressive enhancement); the `view-transition-name` is set on the CLICKED card only, because duplicate names abort a transition, and the transition is skipped under both reduced-motion channels. **(G) CHIP FILTERING** reorders server-rendered Home rails in place through `document.startViewTransition` where supported (gate 11: the control acts on the DOM), with the same reduced-motion skip. `.lp-card` on `/` and `/catalogue`'s `--card-hover-lift` (#77 flags (b), (g)) are untouched. **As built (2026-10-12):** the chip bar sits in a new `.ex-shelves` wrapper (so its sticky box spans the Recommended panel and the body), pins at `--ex-chipbar-top` (guest: `--site-header-pinned-h`, measured 68px) and draws its hairline from an IntersectionObserver `data-stuck`; Services · Freelancers · Products · Projects · Articles chips filter `#ex-home-shelves` by `data-shelf` (measured: one shelf left, URL unchanged, "All" restores) while Sessions · Teams · Businesses · People still navigate. The Products rail is the chip-filtered shelf (`RailFrame` `facets`, `RailCell` `facet`). `.ex-rail__cell[hidden]` was added because `display: flex` on the cell beat the UA `[hidden]` rule, which also means the Articles rail's inline search had NEVER hidden a cell before this change. The reveal measured 0px at 150ms, 33.5px after the delay, and the card box did not move. The skeleton is the existing `Skeleton` with a new `shimmer` mode; it shows in ContinueRail only once a stored history exists, and on a refinement only past 200ms (measured: none at 80ms, 8 cells with `aria-busy` mid-flight). AmbientPalette needed no change: it already resolves Home rail cards as they intersect (14 resolved over one scroll), and the reveal panel inherits the extracted `--ex-wash`. Edge-fade masks (chip bar, profile banner) take `--ex-fade-ink`. Mobile audit fixes: R1 the Articles search wraps to its own row; R2 the phone chip row drops its arrows and starts on the page gutter (first chip x = 20, same as the headings); R3 Recommended tabs share the row equally and drop their icons below 480px (no overflow at 390px); R4 media cards take `--card-pad-media: --space-3` below `--bp-md`. Both reduced-motion channels were measured: no reveal on hover, press `transform: none`, no pulse animation, static skeleton. **FLAGGED — for a human:** (e) the brief said the chip bar's fill is `--bg`, but neither shell paints `--bg` (`#f8fafa`): the guest page is `--surface` and the authed canvas is `--frame-surface`, so a literal `--bg` bar read as a grey band. It takes `var(--frame-surface, var(--surface))` instead. (f) `/view` has no script to veto a cross-document transition, so a `/view` → `/view` navigation (a related card) also morphs stage to stage, still removed under reduced motion. (g) the authed shell's sticky offset (`--shell-topbar-h`) was not browser-measured. | `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §B.5 (motion notes) · §C.1 · §3 gates 7 / 9 / 12 · `apps/web/features/explore/**` · `apps/web/features/view/**` · `packages/ui` (Skeleton) · Decisions #74 / #75(a) / #76 / #77 |
| 175 | **The Explore Home's promo blocks become one `PromoSlot` — `sponsored` · `cta` · `editorial` — on the rails' measure, solid `--surface-1`, at the rail-heading register; a paid placement is a featured cell inside its own rail; the "Become a Freelancer" slot is withheld from freelancers and dismissible (2026-10-12). AMENDS #76 (the interleaved promo frames it kept) and DESIGN_SYSTEM §C.1.** (A) **ONE COMPONENT.** `SponsoredFrame`, both `CtaBanner`s and `HelpArticlesStrip` are deleted for `components/promos/PromoSlot.tsx`, typed by the `PromoSlot` discriminated union in `@projective/types/explore` (`SponsoredPromo` · `CtaPromo` · `EditorialPromo`, `PromoAnchor`); `HomeFeed.sponsored` / `helpArticles` / `ctas` become one `promos: PromoSlot[]`. `cta` and `editorial` slots name the section they follow (`after`); the unused `SPONSORED` / `HELP_ARTICLES` / `CTAS` fixtures are removed. (B) **MEASURE AND SURFACE.** Every slot renders inside `.ex-home__section`, so it shares the rail gutter and full content width; the three `--container-xl` caps and the viewport bleed rules are gone. Every variant is solid `--surface-1` (the translucent `--ex-primary-tint` CTA wash and the `--surface-2` neutral tone are gone, §3 gate 6 · #157); titles take `--ex-rail-title-size`, the token `.ex-rail__title` now reads, so no promo title can out-rank a rail heading (§3 gate 8). The stacked ⇄ two-column reflow is a container query on `expromo` (the featured cell or `.ex-home__section--promo`). Media is `ProgressiveImage` in the 16:10 `.ex-media`; no inline `url()`. (C) **SPONSORED IN ITS RAIL.** `live-catalog.ts` reads `promoted_placements.surface` (oldest `starts_at` first) and resolves each placement to its item (`Catalog.placements`); the Home builds one sponsored slot per active `explore_home` placement on a sponsored service or product, any number of them. Each renders as a two-cell featured `RailCell` (`min(2 × --ex-cell-w + gap, 100%)`) at the head of the Services or Products rail, and that listing is removed from the rail's own cells (`featureInRail`, `core/promo-model.ts`, tested). The separate "Sponsored" eyebrow is gone: the slot's disclosure is the card family's AD badge, the same object and the same "Sponsored" name. (D) **GLASS ON AN UNDERLAY (§3 gate 9).** `.ex-ad`'s and `.ex-actions__btn`'s backdrop blur and veil move to a `::before` underlay on an isolated host; the action button's fill becomes `--ex-actions-fill`, and the profile Work tab's hover override now sets the variable. (E) **PRESS, NOT LIFT (§3 gate 12).** `.ex-btn--solid` loses its hover `translateY(-2px)` for the design system's primary hover border; `.ex-btn` compresses on `:active` by `--btn-press-scale`, and the sponsored slot and the editorial links by the card family's `--ex-press-scale` (#174); all `transform: none` under both reduced-motion channels. (F) **AUDIENCE AND DISMISSAL.** `CtaPromo.audience` (`everyone` · `non_freelancers`) and `dismissible` are enforced by the `PromoGate` island, which owns the slot's section, so a withheld slot leaves no gap. "Become a Freelancer" is `non_freelancers` and is withheld when `userContext.isFreelancer` (or, under simulation, the persona can sell). Both CTAs carry an icon-only ghost dismiss control that stores the slot id in `LocalKeys.EXPLORE_DISMISSED_PROMOS` and moves focus to the next section. The Dev Context Switcher gains the `promoDismissal` axis (`auto` · `visible` · `dismissed`, `data-dev-promo-dismissal`). **FLAGS for a human:** (a) dismissals are applied after hydration, so a dismissed slot paints once before it hides. Both gated slots sit below the fold, but a scroll-restored reader can see one vanish; a pre-paint hide needs a per-id rule or an injected style. (b) only service and product placements get a slot; `freelancer` · `team` · `project` placements (legal under `promoted_placements_entity_type_check`) are read and skipped. (c) `explore_results` and `view_rail` are read but not rendered, and `ExploreItem.sponsored` still lights the AD badge on every surface for a placement bought for any one surface. (d) the editorial slot still lists the newest seller-authored catalogue articles under "New here? Start with these" linking to `/help`; these are not platform help reads (pre-existing). (e) "Launch a Team" was made dismissible too; there is no team-membership signal on `UserContext` to withhold it from someone already in a team. (f) the number of featured slots per rail is uncapped. (g) the CTA action stays teal `.ex-btn--solid`; #157's tier 1 (amber, "the act that starts" a conversion flow) could claim "Start freelancing". |
| 176 | **Explore/View P0 fixes — the guest basket redirect, the Home error state, the desktop footer columns, a scoped focus ring on the discovery controls with a guest skip link and inert megamenus, full team/business names in "More by", and the card Star persisted to `org.user_bookmarks` (2026-10-12). Below 768px they are accordions (previously ≤640px). **(D) FOCUS (owner's direction, a scoped exception to 152a27a).** `global.css` draws `--overlay-focus-ring` at `--overlay-focus-offset` on `.ex-railbtn`, `.ex-promo--sponsored`, `.ui-carousel__nav` and `.ui-carousel__indicator`, and inside the card edge (offset × −2) on `.ex-card__link`, because the rail scroller clips vertically. `packages/ui/styles/index.css`'s zero-specificity `:where(…)` floor is unchanged: the effective neutraliser is `global.css`'s `!important` element layer, so the exception lives beside #171's. **(E) KEYBOARD PATH.** `GuestShell` gains a "Skip to content" first Tab stop to `<main id=PAGE_CANVAS_MAIN_ID tabIndex=-1>` (the signed-in `AppShell` already had one). So the Star renders only on `SaveableEntity` types (service, project, freelancer/team/business profile); product, article and buyer cards carry Share and the kebab alone. **FLAGS for a human:** (a) should products and articles become saveable?
