(c) Report and Add to project in the card kebab, and the `/view` lane's "Save to custom list", are still local-only stubs (§3.11). (d) the Star paints unfilled until the page's one bookmark read lands. |
| 177 | **The `/view` cross-sell rides Explore's rail module — `HomeRail` → `RailFrame` in a `contained` variant, project blocks in `HomeGrid`, and the `@projective/ui/display` `Carousel` retired from view rails (2026-10-12). REVERSES Decision #43's choice of `Carousel` (`RelatedCarousel`) for the view rails, and with it #106(B)'s "`/view` `RelatedCarousel` passes `fixed`" — FLAGGED FOR HUMAN SIGN-OFF (a).** **(A) COMPOSITION.** `RelatedSection` (`view/components/RelatedRail.tsx`) keeps its two-block cap (`MAX_GROUPS`) and `groupItemsByType`, but each block is now Explore's section module. A non-project block is the `HomeRail` island → `RailFrame` with `variant="contained"`, its cards server-rendered `RailCell` + `EntityCard productLayout="fixed"` children (the Home contract: the first byte carries the cards, the island carries only the gesture). A project block is `HomeGrid` with a new `columns: 2 | 3` (a project page's "Similar" keeps its three-up from #142(D); default two). A section with ONE format is that rail, heading level 2. A section with TWO is a titled `.vw-related__set` whose blocks take a level-3 heading. "See all" goes through the module's `action` slot (`.ex-viewall.ex-rail__action`, the Results feed's "Show all" idiom), and the heading links to the same place, so the destination survives the phone header where the action folds away. Call sites pass a stable `id` (`more-by` · `similar` · `suggested`); the article's `columns={2}` is gone because cells now size to the column. **(B) THE MODULE GROWS FIVE OPTIONAL PROPS AND ONE VARIANT.** `RailFrame`: `tail` optional · `subtitle` (a Meta-register `.ex-rail__sub`, pulled up to its heading) · `level` · `variant: "bleed" | "contained"`. `RailHeading`: `level: 2 | 3` and an optional `tail`; level 3 renders `h3.ex-rail__title--sub` in the §A.4 section-header register (`--text-xs`, uppercase, `--tracking-wide`, `--fw-semibold`, `--text-secondary`), so the two levels differ by size, case and tracking, never by weight alone. `HomeGrid`: `columns` · `subtitle` · `level` · optional `tail`. `contained` = no trailing bleed (the track stops at its column, because past it is the conversion lane, not the viewport) and `--ex-cell-w: clamp(17rem, 30cqi, 19rem)` against `.vw-related { container-type: inline-size }`, so a rail in the article's `--measure-wide` column and one in the full main stage each show as many cards as fit, with the next one peeking. Every existing Home / Results / Continue call site is unchanged. **(C) EDGE STATES, ALL RAILS ON BOTH SURFACES.** The arrows are withheld (`visibility`, so the header never reflows) whenever `data-scrollable="false"`, and are pinned to the trailing edge (`margin-inline-start: auto`) so a phone header without its progress track no longer leaves them beside the title. A single-cell rail is known to fit before the probe runs, so `:has(> .ex-rail__track > .ex-rail__cell:only-child)` withholds the arrows and the progress track from the server render on: a one-item group is a rail with no arrows, never two dead ones. The track gains `overscroll-behavior-x: contain` (a trackpad swipe past the last card no longer fires browser back/forward). **RTL:** `useCarousel` took `atStart` / `atEnd` from the signed `scrollLeft`, which runs 0 → −max in a right-to-left track, so Prev was permanently disabled and Next never disabled. Both now read the absolute distance travelled. That alone left Next paging BACKWARDS, because `pageScroll` (`marketing/core/drag-scroll.ts`) scrolled by a physical `left` delta, so `pageScroll` now treats `dir` as logical and negates it under `direction: rtl` (`RecommendedPanel` is the third caller and gets the same fix). The chevrons and the action arrow flip under `[dir="rtl"]`. **(D) DELETED.** `view/islands/RelatedCarousel.island.tsx`, and `view.css`'s `.vw-related__head` … `.vw-slide--projects .ex-projrow` (95 lines, including the dead `.ex-projrow` rules: nothing has rendered that class since the project row became a card, per `ResultsGroupRow`'s own note). **(E) CSS.** The new rules live in a new `explore/styles/rail.css`, loaded after `explore-home.css` by `RailFrame` and by `ViewStyleAnchor` (a cross-sell made only of project grids mounts no rail island, so the sheets need a carrier of their own). **The full extraction of the rail region out of `explore-home.css` was deferred:** that sheet was under concurrent edit by the #174 / #175 sessions, and moving ~400 lines out from under them would have clobbered their hunks. Moving the base rail region into `rail.css` is a follow-up with no behaviour change. **No new `@projective/ui` primitive** → no §C.1 change; no schema change; no lifecycle change; no new simulatable axis → nothing for the Dev Context Switcher (§5). `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §D.7.9 (new) + the §D.7 merge gate updated. **FLAGGED — for a human:** (a) **this reverses #43's Carousel choice for the view rails** and needs the product owner's sign-off. The brief asked for it, but #43 is a logged decision and §8 does not let one be overturned silently. (b) **`.ex-rail__lead` was `--fw-bold`, a pre-existing §A.4 breach on `/explore`** ("a heading is never `--fw-bold`"). Adopting the Explore heading on `/view` as-is would have re-introduced the breach #78 removed from the view page's rail headings, so the shared lead is now `--fw-medium` (§A.4's title weight) on BOTH surfaces. The italic and the `--on-surface` / `--on-surface-variant` split still carry the two-tone heading, but every `/explore` rail heading is visibly lighter, and that is a product call. (c) **Project grids are capped at two rows** (4 two-up, 6 three-up). The carousel paged through every brief. On "More by" the rest is behind See all; "Similar" has no See all, so briefs past the sixth are unreachable from this page. (d) Withholding the arrows on a non-overflowing rail also changes Home, Results and "Continue": a short Home rail used to show two disabled arrows and now shows none. The brief said "whenever", so it applies everywhere. (h) **The cell floor is 17rem, not the brief's 14rem.** Measured at 1440, the article template's column is 728px, so `30cqi` sat on the floor: at 14rem (224px) every service card in it truncated its kind or delivery ("Pipeline service · 4-week delivery" needs 258px, "1:1 session · 6 × 45-minute sessions" 263px; the widest in the corpus, "Direct deliverable · 7-day delivery", needs 269px). 17rem (272px) clears all of them, which is the brief's own acceptance criterion, and still peeks on a phone (one 272px card + 86px in a 358px column). The margin is 3px, so longer delivery copy will truncate the right-aligned fact first, as `.ex-card__aside` is designed to. (i) The order-dependent rules in `rail.css` (`.ex-rail__grid--3`, `.ex-rail__title--sub`) are doubled-class selectors: on a project page only `ViewStyleAnchor` carries the sheets, and the bundle put `explore-home.css`'s equal-specificity `.ex-rail__grid` after `rail.css`, so the first build rendered the three-up "Similar" two-up (measured 500px cells in a 1016px column). (e) `RelatedSection`'s `columns` now affects only project grids; the carousel's viewport-keyed `responsiveOptions` have no successor because `cqi` replaces them. (f) The `pageScroll` RTL fix goes beyond the brief's `useCarousel.ts:33-34`, for the reason in (C). (g) **Verification.** `deno check` + `deno lint` are clean on every touched file, and the explore · view · marketing unit tests pass (86). Every dev server on the machine was hung by the in-flight work of other sessions, so the browser checks ran on a PRODUCTION build (`USE_MOCKS=true`, headless Chromium) of a scratch worktree at HEAD with only this change applied. **CLS at 900px on all five `/view/art-*` pages:** 0.0005 with the rails scrolled into a 900px viewport, and 0.017–0.022 whole-page (900×9000). HEAD measured the same (0.0005 / 0.017–0.022), and the remaining shift is the article body's lists and gallery, not the rails. **The brief's 0.27 did not reproduce on a production build at HEAD**, most likely a dev-server reading, where sheets land after hydration. So the CLS gate is met, but this change is not shown to be what fixes it. **Peek:** at 390 every scrollable rail shows one whole card + a partial; at 900 and 1440 a partial follows the last whole card. **Truncation at 1440:** zero `.ex-kind` / `.ex-card__aside` overflow on every rail (after (h)). **Heading register:** level 2 is `--ex-rail-title-size` / `--fw-medium` on both `/explore` and `/view` (21.6px at 1440, 16.8px at 390); level 3 is 12px / 600 uppercase. **Edges:** single-cell rails show no arrows from first paint; the document has zero horizontal overflow at 390 / 900 / 1440; LTR and `dir="rtl"` paging both reach the true end with Prev/Next enabling and disabling correctly (HEAD in RTL: Prev permanently disabled, Next scrolled the wrong way). **NOT verified in a browser:** service and product `/view` pages, because at HEAD a guest is redirected to `/login` (the bug #176 fixes), so their rails were checked only through the SSR markup (three contained rails each). The final two CSS follow-ups ((i) and the trailing-edge arrows) were verified by injecting the shipped rules into the built page rather than by a fourth rebuild. | `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §D.7.9 (new) + §D.7 merge gate · `apps/web/features/view/{components/{RelatedRail,EntityViewPage,ArticleViewScreen}.tsx,islands/{ViewStyleAnchor.island.tsx,RelatedCarousel.island.tsx (deleted)},styles/view.css}` · `apps/web/features/explore/{components/{RailFrame,RailHeading,HomeGrid}.tsx,styles/{rail.css (new),explore-home.css}}` · `apps/web/features/marketing/core/{useCarousel,drag-scroll}.ts` · Decisions #43 / #76 / #77 / #78 / #106 / #142 |
| 178 | **Personalised discovery ranking — one quality score behind every "recommended" order (a cold-start prior · workload against the Global Intensity Cap · the newcomer Discovery Boost · Standing's multiplier · the trust penalty, every coefficient from `search.search_weights`), the anon-cached catalogue plus a viewer-scoped pass under the viewer's own JWT, `org.recent_views`, and the Home's "Because you viewed" / "Saved" / Trending rails (2026-10-12).** **(A) THE SCORE** (`packages/backend/services/explore/ranking.ts`, pure, 12 tests). `total = standing × (sentiment + volume + availability + boost) + penalty`: sentiment is the item's rating shrunk toward its track's corpus mean by 5 pseudo-reviews (a newcomer starts at the average, one 5★ cannot outrank fifty 4.9★s) × `review_sentiment`; volume saturates logarithmically at 50 reviews × `review_count`; availability is the free share of the owner's cap × `workload` (zero AT the cap — the spec's Discovery Loop); the Boost (`discovery_boost`, NEW seed row, 1.25) needs utilisation 0, no penalty, no rating under 4.0, and joined ≤ 90 days OR still on rung 1; Standing's `discovery_weight_bp` scales the earned terms; `discovery_penalty` (≤ 10) subtracts unscaled × `penalty`. The catalogue is assembled best-first and `getResults` / `rankRecommended` / the Home lists read `catalog.scoreById` (`compareRecommended` without scores keeps the handed order, so the typeahead's tie-break needed no change). **Verification is no longer a ranking term** — the old comparator put verified owners first, contradicting PRODUCT_SPEC §Reputation & Discovery 5 / #155 ("no discovery boost is attached to verification; discovery weight comes from Standing alone"); PRODUCT_SPEC wins, so this corrects code drift rather than a doc conflict. A project speaks with its CLIENT's reviews and carries the worse of its poster's and client business's penalty ("their project posts are de-ranked"). **(B) TWO PASSES.** The shared pass stays one anon read cached 30 s and adds three public-by-construction signals: penalised rows of `search.profiles_index`, `search.ranking_weights()` (a definer projecting key · weight · is_active only) and `search.trending_entities()` (distinct people per entity over 7 days — views 1, saves 2 — floored at THREE people so no row traces to one person's history). The viewer pass (`viewer-signals.ts`) reads `org.recent_views`, `org.user_bookmarks`, `search.user_affinity` (decayed, 14-day half-life) and `users_public.interests` + the seller's own skills with `getUserClient(jwt)`, per request, never cached; a failure is logged and the shared feed is served. `/explore` renews a lapsed session in place (the `[handle]` middleware's move) so a returning reader is not served a guest's Home; `landing()` stays shared. **(C) SCHEMA.** `org.recent_views` (00000011: one row per person per entity, `view_count`, `first_viewed_at` / `viewed_at`, CHECK vocabulary = `org.bookmark_entity_type` + product · article · user), read-own + clear-own policies only (00002010), INSERT/UPDATE revoked and nothing for `anon` (00002520), two indexes + two bookmark indexes (00004001); its one writer is `search.record_view` (00001400, definer, `auth.uid()` never a parameter, server-stamped times — a client-written future `viewed_at` would pin an item in Trending — topic affinity bumped with the same decay; EXECUTE revoked from `anon`, 00002510). `org.profiles_index` appends `discovery_weight_bp` and `workload_cap` (the person's `max_workload_intensity`, else `global_workload_cap_default`; a team's = its active members' defaults combined). Zod: `@projective/types/explore` `signals.ts`. **(D) RECORDING A VIEW** is server-side where the opening happens — `/view/[entity]`, `/[handle]/view/[item]`, `/[handle]` (fire-and-forget; a guest or a crawler records nothing; your own work is not recorded, so nobody nudges it toward Trending) — plus the results drawer's in-place open through `POST /api/explore/views` (a background beacon). **(E) THE RAILS** (`personalise.ts`, pure, 10 tests): "Because you viewed …" = the newest of the last three views with ≥ 3 same-family relatives (Jaccard over topics + skill families), excluding everything opened and the viewer's own; without one, "Picked for you" from decayed affinity, else stated interests + skills — briefs for a seller (the spec's Recommender Logic), services · products · talent for anyone else; "Saved" = bookmarks newest first; Trending past the floor, else "New on Projective" (published ≤ 30 days), else no rail. Ranked `quality + interest_history × relevance`; 3 to fill, 12 at most; the app words each heading from `DiscoveryRail.kind`, so a heading never claims more than its data. **(F) THE CARD METER FIX.** `ProfileItem.workload.level` carried the raw summed W_i on a 0–100 scale, so every live freelancer read "Light" / "Available now"; it is now utilisation of the cap (`workloadPercent`). **(G) DEV CONTEXT SWITCHER:** `discoveryViewer` (Auto · No history · No signals) and `discoveryActivity` (Auto · Quiet platform) through the `pj.dev.discovery` cookie, honoured server-side in development only (`DENO_ENV`), `/explore` reloads on change. **FLAGGED — for a human:** (a) **Pro's `discovery_boost` entitlement flag (00005030) is not read by ranking** — the Boost is earned only, per the free plan's own note and §16.5 "no pay-to-win rank"; the paid flag now means nothing: retire it or repoint it (e.g. sponsored-placement credits). (b) **`search.ranking_weights()` makes the active coefficients world-readable** (labels and `updated_by` stay admin-read); the alternative, a service-role read, is reserved for edge functions by §6. (c) `search.fn_search_entities` still runs its own model (a verification boost, linear volume, workload ÷ 100) and has no app caller — reconcile or retire. (d) Trending counts each account once per entity; sybil accounts could still push an item past the floor. (e) A team's `current_workload_intensity` has no writer (only freelancers' is synced), so a team always reads fully available. (f) The local database's `org.profiles_index` carries a stale trailing `verification_stamp` no migration declares; `db:reset` clears it and was NOT run here (it wipes local data). (g) Two item ↔ entity resolvers now exist — `catalog.entityById` / `itemIdByEntity` (this change) and `bookmark-target.ts` (the concurrent bookmark work) — consolidate. (h) The SQL was proven in a rolled-back transaction against the running local Postgres (RLS, grants, the definer write, repeat counting, topic dedupe, the floor, the CHECK, the view), then the same statements, extracted from the migration files, were applied to the SHARED local database in one transaction (the `org.profiles_index` drop-and-recreate takes `search.platform_stats` with it; both ACLs verified identical), so concurrent sessions' `/explore` kept working — `db:reset` remains the canonical rebuild. The dev world seeds twelve views (`03_entities.sql`, regenerated; only that file changed). (i) A person's view history has no clear-history control yet (the DELETE policy is ready). (j) "Trending" is shared, so a seller can see their own work there; the personal rails never recommend it. | `packages/backend/services/explore/{ranking,personalise,viewer-signals}.ts` (+ tests) · `{live-catalog,query,ExploreBackendService}.ts` · `packages/types/explore/{signals,discovery}.ts` · `apps/web/routes/(public)/explore.tsx` · `apps/web/routes/api/explore/views.ts` · `apps/web/routes/{(public)/view/[entity],[handle]/view/[item],[handle]}/index.tsx` · `apps/web/features/explore/{components/{DiscoveryRails,ExploreHome}.tsx,core/{record-view,discovery-sim,discovery-rails,ExploreService}.ts (+ tests),islands/SearchDashboard.island.tsx}` · `apps/web/features/devtools/*` · `apps/web/utils/dev-seam.ts` · `supabase/migrations/{00000011,00001400,00002001,00002010,00002510,00002520,00003001,00004001,00005050}_*.sql` · `supabase/seeds/{gen/world.ts,gen/emit-entities.ts,03_entities.sql}` · `documentation/database/{org,search}/*` + `Seed.md` · PRODUCT_SPEC §Reputation & Discovery · `finance-model.md` §16.7 · SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE §Discovery (Explore) services · `ROUTING.md` · Decisions #12 / #58 / #76 / #155 |
| 179 | **Voyage AI voyage-4 embeddings in discovery, behind `EMBEDDINGS_ENABLED` and a development-only switcher override — search re-ranking and the `/view` "Similar" rails (2026-10-12).** **(A) THE VENDOR.** Voyage AI `voyage-4`: 1024 dimensions, unit-length vectors, so inner product ranks exactly as cosine. Plain `fetch`, no SDK (`services/search/voyage-client.ts`): `input_type` always sent (`document` for listings, `query` for searches), `output_dimension: 1024`. Two hosts serve the same API and refuse each other's keys with a 403: MongoDB Atlas `https://ai.mongodb.com/v1/embeddings` (Atlas model API keys — the brief's endpoint and the default) and `https://api.voyageai.com/v1/embeddings` (Voyage platform keys, `pa-…`); the client picks the host from the key unless `VOYAGE_API_BASE` names one (see flag (a)). Limits checked against the Atlas API reference: 1,000 inputs and 320K tokens per request for voyage-4, 32K per text, a free-trial key held to 3 RPM / 10K TPM — so batches stay at ≤ 128 texts and ≈ 8K estimated tokens. Each call has a per-attempt timeout and an overall deadline (3 s for a query, 2 min for a backfill batch) and at most two retries with exponential backoff + jitter (or `Retry-After`) on 429 / 5xx / timeout / network, none on 400 / 401 / 403. The key goes into the `Authorization` header and nowhere else, and is scrubbed from any echoed error. **(B) THE DIMENSION CHANGE.** `vector(1536)` → `vector(1024)` on `search.profiles_index` · `projects_index` · `services_index` and `query_logs.query_embedding` (00000018, edited in place), each index table gaining `embedding_model` · `embedding_hash` (SHA-256 of the embedded text) · `embedded_at`, all nullable until the backfill; the HNSW indexes are rebuilt with `vector_ip_ops` (00004004). **(C) THE MOCK EMBEDDING IS GONE.** `search.fn_mock_embedding`, `fn_fill_mock_embedding` and the three `trg_fill_embedding_*` triggers (a hash-derived vector on every index row) are deleted (00001400 / 00001800 / 00002510); the `sync_*_to_index` triggers that keep `fts` stay. `fn_search_entities` and `fn_log_query` take a trailing nullable `p_query_embedding vector(1024)`: NULL scores the vector term 0 / stores NULL. **(D) COVERAGE: A NEW `search.catalogue_index`, NOT AN EXTENDED TABLE.** Of the five things `live-catalog.ts` loads, listings-as-services (`services_index`), public projects and listed profiles had index rows; products and articles had none. Extending `services_index` was ruled out — its key is `service_id REFERENCES marketplace.service_blueprints`, so a product or an article cannot be a row without dropping that FK (and §1 forbids reshaping it into a polymorphic key in passing); `projects_index` is project-keyed the same way. So one table keyed `(entity_type ∈ product · article, entity_id)` — the shape `profiles_index` already uses for four kinds — with `title` · `fts` · `is_public` · the embedding columns, kept by two new definer sync triggers (`sync_product_listing_to_index` on `catalogue.listings`: a product's title, description and status ride its LISTING; `sync_article_to_index` on `catalogue.articles`), visibility = the source is `published`, RLS "Public can view catalogue search index", backstopped in `seeds/09_search.sql`. Every one of the 49 public discovery entities in the seed world now has an index row. **(E) THE DOORS.** `search.fn_set_embedding(entity_type, entity_id, embedding, model, hash)` — definer, the only writer of the vectors, EXECUTE revoked from PUBLIC / anon / authenticated and granted to `service_role` alone. `search.fn_nearest(p_embedding, p_types, p_exclude, p_limit)` — definer, `(entity_type, entity_id, score)` best first under each index's own anon visibility rule (the USING clause of its SELECT policy), so it answers nothing anon could not already read and never a vector. `search.fn_nearest_to_entity(…)` — the same, seeded with one PUBLIC entity's STORED vector read inside the database: the "Similar" rail needs the item's vector, and fetching it in the app would mean either a service-role read on a public page (the alternative #178(b) declined) or vectors on the wire. 00002520 takes back the table-level SELECT 00002500 gave `anon` / `authenticated` on the four index tables and `query_logs` and re-grants every column except `embedding` · `embedding_model` · `embedding_hash` · `embedded_at` (`query_embedding` on `query_logs`): a PostgREST select of them, or of `*`, is refused. Zod: `@projective/types/explore` `embeddings.ts`. **(F) THE SWITCH.** `EMBEDDINGS_ENABLED` (default false) in `core/env.ts`; `isEmbeddingsLive(req?)` in `core/supabase.ts` = not `USE_MOCKS` ∧ Supabase configured ∧ a real `VOYAGE_API_KEY` ∧ the effective setting, which is the env flag unless the request carries `pj.dev.embeddings` (`on` | `off`). The cookie counts only under `DENO_ENV=development` AND reaches the gate only from a development build (routes pass `IS_DEV ? req : undefined` — the `pj.dev.landing` guard), so production ignores it twice over; an override can turn the switch on but never without the key; callers without a request (writes, the backfill) get the environment's answer. The pure gate (`core/embeddings-gate.ts`) is unit-tested over the full env × cookie × dev/prod × key table. Dev Context Switcher (§5): an `embeddings` axis (Follow env · On · Off) in `DevOverrides` + `DEV_DEFAULTS` + `DEV_EMBEDDINGS`, the panel control, `reflect()` set / delete, the seam (`data-dev-embeddings`, `DevEmbeddings`), writing the cookie and reloading `/explore` and `/view/*`; the field's status line reads the effective state from the development-only `GET /api/explore/ranking` (404 in a production build). In development `/explore`, both `/view` page routes and every `/api/explore/*` response carry `x-discovery-ranking: semantic | heuristic` — what actually ordered that response, so a fallback reads heuristic. **(G) THE READ PATH.** Off: byte-for-byte the heuristic path — no Voyage call, no extra RPC, `fn_log_query` called with exactly its previous arguments. On, a search with a query embeds it (a process-wide LRU + TTL cache keyed by the normalised query, concurrent calls shared), asks `fn_nearest` for the category's kinds, and the pure `services/explore/semantic.ts` keeps every facet, the category and the JS candidate set, re-ranks the candidates on the default "recommended" sort by `(1 − SEMANTIC_BLEND_WEIGHT) × position + SEMANTIC_BLEND_WEIGHT × similarity` (weight 0.5; position 1 − i/n, similarity normalised to the best) and adds up to 12 neighbours the text match missed that clear max(0.30, 0.8 × the best) and pass the same facets — the floors measured on the seed world, where voyage-4 query-to-listing cosines run ≈ 0.34–0.42 for a match and ≈ 0.19–0.30 for an unrelated item. An explicit sort the reader picked still decides the order. The `/view` "Similar" rail takes the nearest same-type items to the item's stored vector, excluding the item and everything its owner published, topped up from the heuristic; no vector → the heuristic rail. Any Voyage or database failure → the heuristic path for that request, one `[embeddings] … fell back` server-log line, no user-facing error. Home personalisation and user vectors stay out (the ranking-v1 task): a `// #region` hook point in `ExploreBackendService.home` only. **(H) THE WRITE PATH.** `deno task embeddings:backfill [--dry-run] [--force]` reads the public catalogue as `anon`, builds each item's text (`core/embedding-text.ts`, pure and tested: type label · title · category · skills · summary, plus model and delivery for a service) and its hash, skips a matching hash + model, embeds in chunks and writes through `fn_set_embedding` with the service-role key; a rate limit or an outage waits and resumes, a refusal stops. `--dry-run` prints the count, a token estimate (≈ 4 characters a token) and the cost at $0.06 / 1M without calling Voyage. After a committed write — `CatalogueBackendService.update` / `setStatus`, `ProfileBackendService.save`, `ProjectBackendService.updateProject` (live; also the publish path), `WorkspaceBackendService.update` — `reembedInBackground` re-embeds the item fire-and-forget when `EMBEDDINGS_ENABLED` holds; a failure logs and never fails the write. Measured on the seed world: 49 public entities, an estimate of 2,307 tokens, 1,648 billed (≈ $0.0001); a second run embeds nothing. **FLAGGED — for a human:** (a) **The brief's endpoint refuses the configured key.** `ai.mongodb.com` answers 403 ("Voyage AI API keys work with Voyage AI endpoints, and MongoDB Atlas API keys work with MongoDB endpoints") to the `.env.local` key, a Voyage platform key, which `api.voyageai.com` accepts. Built: host chosen by key kind, `VOYAGE_API_BASE` to pin one; decide which account the platform standardises on. (b) **That key is on Voyage's free trial (3 RPM / 10K TPM).** A fourth new search in a minute falls back to the heuristic (logged, by design); add a payment method before relying on semantic ranking. (c) **Stale `EXPLORE_BACKEND_LIVE` text, flagged per §8, not fixed.** The brief points at SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE.md:747–759, but those lines — at HEAD too — already describe the live-only catalogue. The stale claims are §The Entity View ("`viewPage(id)` behind `EXPLORE_BACKEND_LIVE`", now carrying a flag callout), the Environment Variable Contract's gate comment (each gate "flips ONE fat service from its deterministic fixtures to its live Supabase path" — discovery reads have no fixture branch; the flag now gates only the interest-event and query-log writes and stub-mode saves) and Decision #12 ("stub-first behind `EXPLORE_BACKEND_LIVE`"). (d) Writes re-embed on `EMBEDDINGS_ENABLED` alone: the switcher cookie is a read-path override, so a developer testing with "On" over an env-off server picks up their own edits by running the backfill. (e) No live article write path exists yet; articles are embedded by the backfill until one lands (the hook is one call). (f) `search.fn_nearest` is anon-executable over public rows, so repeated probing could approximate a public item's vector — the text it was made from is public anyway. (g) `documentation/database/search/Functions.md` "Current state" and the comment above `sync_project_to_index` still say five sync triggers are `INVOKER`; the migration has all six (now eight) as `SECURITY DEFINER` — doc drift, left to the domain owners. (h) Verified: the SQL in a rolled-back transaction against the reset local database (widths, opclasses, grants, anon refusals, nearest ordering, exclusion, NULL vectors); the backfill and its idempotent re-run; a retitled listing re-embedded and restored; and in the running app `/api/explore/search?q=logo` 0 results heuristic → 5 semantic, `q=brnd identity` 0 → 4, the Similar rail re-ordered, and the switcher flipping Follow env → On → Off → Follow env without a restart (cookie, reload, header and status line checked in a browser). | `packages/backend/core/{env,supabase,embeddings-gate,embedding-text}.ts` (+ tests) · `packages/backend/services/search/{EmbeddingBackendService,voyage-client,embedding-index,embedding-sync,query-log}.ts` (+ test) · `packages/backend/services/explore/{semantic (+ test),ExploreBackendService,live-view,query,live-catalog}.ts` · `packages/backend/services/{catalogue/CatalogueBackendService,profile/ProfileBackendService,projects/ProjectBackendService,workspace/WorkspaceBackendService}.ts` · `packages/types/explore/{embeddings,mod}.ts` · `scripts/embeddings-backfill.ts` + `deno.json` · `apps/web/features/explore/core/{discovery-ranking,ExploreService}.ts` · `apps/web/features/devtools/{core/dev-context.ts,components/{DevContextPanel,DevEmbeddingsStatus}.tsx}` · `apps/web/utils/{dev-seam,state}.ts` · `apps/web/routes/api/explore/{_middleware,ranking,search,view}.ts` · `apps/web/routes/{(public)/explore.tsx,(public)/view/[entity]/index.tsx,[handle]/view/[item]/index.tsx}` · `apps/web/features/view/core/view-ssr.ts` · `supabase/migrations/{00000018,00001400,00001800,00002001,00002014,00002510,00002520,00004004}_*.sql` · `supabase/seeds/09_search.sql` · `documentation/database/search/*` + `Seed.md` · SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE §Discovery (Explore) services / §Searching & Recommender System / §The Entity View / §Environment Variable Contract · `.env.example` · Decisions #12 / #178 |
| 180 | **"Save to custom list" on the entity lanes is a BOOKMARK with named collections, not a basket (2026-10-12; RESOLVED by the human the same day).** **(A) THE CONFLICT.** Decision #70 and PRODUCT_SPEC's `/checkout` step 1 make a list "a named basket row, not a separate table", and `finance.baskets`' comment says "a wishlist is a non-default basket". But a basket line (`finance.basket_items`) needs a `finance.purchasable_item_kind` and a `unit_price_minor` snapshot, so only purchasables fit: a person, a project the viewer cannot buy, a message or a file cannot be put in a basket. The saved/favourites system (one `/saved` page across services, people, projects, messages and files) therefore cannot be built on baskets. Meanwhile `EntityLane.island.tsx` and `ProjectLane.island.tsx` offer "Save to custom list" as a session-only toggle (UI gate 11). **(B) PROPOSAL.** "Save" is a bookmark (`org.user_bookmarks`, enum extended to the real entity set) shown on `/saved`; "Add to list" stays a basket action for purchasables only (#70 unchanged). The lane item is relabelled "Save" / "Saved". No bookmark collections in this pass (one flat saved set, filtered by type) unless the human asks for named collections. **RESOLVED (human, 2026-10-12): bookmarks + collections.** Save writes `org.user_bookmarks`; a user may file a bookmark into named collections (`org.user_bookmark_collections`, nullable `collection_id` — NULL is the unfiled default); "Add to list" stays a basket action for purchasables (#70 unchanged). **As built (2026-10-12):** ONE saved system — `SavedBackendService` behind `/api/saved` (`GET ?tab=&collection=&cursor=` · `POST` · `DELETE`), `/api/saved/state`, `/api/saved/collections[/id]`, `/api/messages/[id]/pin`; a save stays in its kind's store (bookmarks for listings · people · project channels; `projects.user_preferences.is_starred` for a project; `comms.set_dm_starred` (new) for a conversation; `comms.message_favorites` for a message; `files.items.starred` for a file). Schema edited in place: `org.bookmark_entity_type` is now `service_blueprint · product · article · user · team · business · enterprise · project_channel` — **`freelancer` became `user`** (the follow graph's vocabulary, so one person is one target; the brief's "client" is the same person) and **`project` was removed** (a project's save is its star, one store per kind; the seed's one project bookmark went with it); `org.user_bookmark_collections` + `user_bookmarks.collection_id` (composite FK `ON DELETE SET NULL (collection_id)`); `org.get_profile_cards` (new, visibility-filtered cards for any profile kind). `projects.user_preferences` needed no new grant or RPC — its own-row policy and the schema grant already allow the upsert; the file star writes under the existing owner-only `UPDATE` policy. Live reads fixed: `toSummary` / `fetchProjectDetail` map `is_starred`, every project room carries `starred`. UI: every fake listed in the brief now writes through one optimistic discipline (`features/saved/core/optimistic.ts`); `CardActions` is Share · Save (Report and Add to project removed, as #140); the lanes' Report removed; `/saved` (`features/saved/`), the account menu's **Saved**, and the profile's follower / following counts + lists. Dev Context Switcher axis `saved` (Auto · Nothing saved · Every type). **FLAGGED — for a human:** (a) the chat message menus' **Report** (`ChatFeed` / `PopoutChat`) is still a stub with no backend — outside this brief's list, left for its own change; (b) the saved service is live-only, so under `dev:mock` a star rolls back with "Saving needs the live backend" unless the `saved` axis is set; (c) PRODUCT_SPEC §Reputation & Discovery's Recommender Logic names "Saved" preferences but specifies no weighting, so the saved signal does NOT feed Explore ranking — a follow-up needing a product rule; (d) `/saved` reads up to 200 rows per store and pages in memory (shortcut — move to SQL paging if a person outgrows it); (e) project, conversation and file stars carry no saved-at instant, so they sort after dated saves; (f) a file star is the OWNER's mark (the star is hidden for anyone else) — a per-viewer star would need a pivot table; (g) the brief said the profile's follower counts already rendered — they did not; they render now, on the hero's meta line; (h) collections can be created and deleted but not renamed. | Decision #70 · PRODUCT_SPEC `/checkout` step 1 · PRODUCT_SPEC §Saved & Favourites · `00000017_tables_finance.sql` `finance.baskets` · `00000011_tables_org.sql` `org.user_bookmarks` · ROUTING.md §Saved API · DESIGN_SYSTEM §C.1 Saved · `database/org/*` · `database/comms/Functions.md` |

| 182 | **The Search Results detail drawer becomes an at-a-glance preview of the page it opens — design-system header controls, Save / Share as ghost header tools, a neutral outlined "Open full page", the service's own rating, and a drawer keyboard (2026-10-13).** At the product owner's direction; the plan was approved before any file changed. **(A) HEADER.** The stepper's raw `<button>`s were unstyled — `.ex-detail-step*` and `.ex-detail-actions*` never had CSS (since 811496d), so the chevrons and "n of m" rendered in browser defaults. They are now muted icon-only `Button`s (`variant="text"` · `severity="secondary"` · `size="sm"` · `rounded`, `--btn-accent: var(--on-surface-variant)`, Tooltip + `.ui-hit`, `aria-disabled` at either end so focus stays put), with "n of m" at `--text-xs` / `--fw-medium` / `--on-surface-variant` / `tabular-nums` between them. `Drawer` gains a `tools` slot rendered before Close — its text Buttons take `--overlay-action-size` and the close button's muted ink and hover — and `initialFocusRef`, focused ITSELF (`useFocusTrap` `initialFocusSelf`, opt-in so `PreviewStage`'s `tabindex=-1` stage keeps resolving to its first tabbable). **(B) A SCOPED EXCEPTION TO §B.8.1 TIER 4.** Save and Share leave the footer for `tools` as tier-5 ghost icon buttons, although §B.8.1 lists Share among the utility actions that must be `severity="neutral" variant="outlined"` (a B.8 merge gate). The owner chose ghost: a header bar beside Close is a toolbar, and #160 already put icon-only ghost tools beside a drawer's Close. The exception covers icon-only tools in an overlay's header only; a utility action in a body or a footer stays tier 4. A guest's Save is the same control as a sign-in anchor; a type `org.user_bookmarks` cannot name (products) gets Share alone. **(C) FOOTER.** Only "Open full page": an anchor carrying `ui-button ui-button--neutral ui-button--outlined ui-button--rounded ui-button--fluid` (tier 4, the owner's choice of a neutral grey pill over a teal outline), so a middle-click still opens a tab. Explore's private `.ex-btn--ghost` is deleted (`.ex-btn--solid` stays for `PromoSlot`). **(D) BODY.** `DetailPanel` dispatches to `components/detail/{Service,Product,Project,Profile}Detail.tsx` over shared `DetailParts`, in the page's reading order: 16:10 media (a brief's client banner at 7:2) → a category eyebrow and the one title (`--text-xl` / `--fw-medium`) → the provider (`OwnerBadge`) → a two-column `<dl>` of key metrics (meta-register labels; Price · Delivery · Type · rating for a service) → a five-line summary excerpt → skills / phase plan / roles. A converted price discloses its origin on the line beneath through the page's own `PriceOrigin` (gains a `class` prop), as the lane does. A profile shows `@handle · specialism · location` under its name and its tracks as facts. Token-only CSS replaces the `clamp(1.4rem, 0.95rem + 1.5vw, 1.9rem)` title, the raw `1.5rem` / `50%` phase index and the never-styled banner / avatar / handle / price parts; media frames take `min-block-size: 0` so a portrait cover cannot outgrow 16:10. Rendered from the `ExploreItem` alone — no `/api/explore/view` fetch, so stepping stays instant; a product's format, files and licence stay on the page. **(E) RATING.** `subjectRating(item)` (`card-signals.ts`) reads a service's or product's OWN track (`asHelper`, the blueprint / product denormalised average in `live-catalog.ts`) and never `asClient`; a project reads its owner's client track as `ProjectHero` does; a profile keeps its tracks (a freelancer's helper track only, §4a). The drawer labels it "Service rating" / "Product rating" / "Client rating" instead of the person-centric "As helper / As client" pair it printed for a service. The mock corpus gave every non-team service a client track (`dual([4.9, …], [4.7, 8])`); it is `helperOnly` now, matching the live projection. **(F) KEYBOARD.** In the drawer (`useDetailKeys`: one document listener while open, removed on close / unmount; never from a field, `isEditableTarget`, nor from a widget that owns the arrows): ←/→ step (mirrored under `rtl`); Shift skips 5, clamped to either end; Ctrl or Cmd (exactly one) jumps to the first item of the next / previous section in PAGE order, looping, and on a single-category feed moves the page to the neighbouring scope in `SEARCH_SCOPES` order (articles excluded), skipping empty scopes and restoring the start when none has results; ↑/↓ scroll the body by a fifth of its height (instant under either reduced-motion channel); Enter opens the full page unless focus is on a control; Escape stays the Drawer's and returns focus to the opening card. The reading region (`role="document"`, `tabindex="-1"`) takes focus on open, so the keys act at once. Globally (`useSearchShortcut`): `/` focuses the explore page's search (the results header, the Explore Home hero via `SearchBar slashFocus`) outside a field; Ctrl+/ (Cmd+/) focuses the header search from anywhere, a field included (`isFocusHeaderSearchShortcut`, matched by the produced `/` so a layout whose Slash key types `-` keeps Ctrl+- as zoom out); both stand down while a modal makes the page `inert`. **(G) VERIFIED.** `deno task check` 0 errors; `deno lint` clean but for `BookingBackendService.ts:20` `plural` unused, unchanged from HEAD; `deno task test:unit` 3,062 passed, only the four `theme-engine.test.ts` failures (#96(a)); `detail-nav.test.ts` covers the key intents, the clamped skip, the section loop and the scope loop, `card-signals.test.ts` `subjectRating`, `shortcuts.test.ts` the header predicate, `DrawerRegions.test.tsx` the tools CSS contract. Browser (`dev:mock`, guest, Chromium): 31 / 31 probe checks — no raw button in header or footer; tools 36×36 in the close's ink; the footer one neutral outlined pill; "Service rating 4.9 (3)" equal to `/api/explore/item`'s `asHelper` 4.93 / 3; →, ←, Shift+→ (1 → 6), Shift+← clamped at 1; Ctrl+→ services → freelancers, Ctrl+← back, Ctrl+← from the first section looping to the last; ↓ / ↑ scroll; Enter on Share opens Share, Enter on the region opens `/view`; Escape strips `?item` and focuses the card; `/` and Ctrl+/ focus their fields and `/` typed in a field stays text; `?category=services` Ctrl+→ → products and back; light and dark; a 390px sheet with no overflow. **FLAGGED — surface, do not silently resolve:** (a) Cmd+←/→ is the browser's Back / Forward on macOS outside a field; the drawer claims it while open, verified in Chromium only (not Safari); (b) Ctrl+←/→ on macOS is the OS's Spaces switch and never reaches the page — Cmd is the Mac binding; (c) each scope switch pushes one history entry, so Back walks the categories; (d) the §B.8.1 tier-4 exception in (B) is the owner's and is recorded in §B.8.1's tier-5 examples; (e) a profile's specialism moved from the fact grid to the byline and is dropped when it repeats the bio. | `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §B.8.1 · §C.1 (Drawer) · `packages/ui/{feedback/islands/Drawer.tsx,feedback/styles/drawer.css,feedback/components/DrawerRegions.test.tsx,hooks/useFocusTrap.ts}` · `apps/web/features/explore/{components/{ResultDetailDrawer,DetailPanel,DetailTools,DetailActions,ResultsHeader,ExploreHomeHeader}.tsx,components/detail/*,core/{detail-nav,card-signals}.ts + tests,hooks/useDetailKeys.ts,islands/SearchDashboard.island.tsx,styles/{explore,explore-results}.css}` · `apps/web/features/shell/{core/shortcuts.ts + test,hooks/useSearchShortcut.ts,islands/NavSearchBar.island.tsx}` · `apps/web/features/marketing/islands/{SearchBar,SiteHeader}.island.tsx` · `apps/web/features/view/components/entity-view-parts.tsx` · `packages/backend/services/explore/fixtures.ts` · Decisions #157 / #160 / #171 |
