| 215 | **A deterministic settlement mismatch is acknowledged into reconciliation, and a scheduled deposit that never reached a PaymentIntent is retried under its own key (2026-10-13).** Post-wave-7 gap closure items 4–5. RESOLVES #200 flag (b); BUILDS ON #126, #186, #200, #203. **(A) MISMATCH.** `finance.settle_card_payment` released its event claim on an amount or currency that disagreed with the payment row, so the webhook 5xx'd for Stripe's ~3-day retry window (one ops row per delivery) and the money then sat uncredited. Now the claim is COMPLETED with `outcome = 'unmatched'`, `reconciliation = 'settlement_mismatch'` (plus the received amount/currency), and `stripe-webhook.ts` `requireLedgerRow` lets exactly that marker through (`SETTLEMENT_MISMATCH`, on `unmatched` / `replayed` only, with a `console.warn`), so the webhook answers 2xx. ONE `ops.log_entries` error row per event (`scope = 'finance.settle_card_payment'`, `client_id = 'stripe:<evt>'`, `NOT EXISTS`-guarded) and the `RAISE WARNING` stay; a redelivery finds the completed claim with the marker and replays it, writing nothing. The payment stays `processing`, uncredited, and `finance.v_ops_unledgered_payments` lists it as `problem = 'settlement_mismatch'` (new trailing columns `stripe_event_id`, `reported_amount_cents`, `reported_currency`) until a human settles or closes it. #200's rule stands for everything transient: no payment bound yet still releases the claim, a settlement that raised still rolls its claim back, both 5xx. **(B) DEPOSITS.** `claim_due_deposit_rules` advanced `next_run_at` at claim time; a transient Stripe/network error left the run `requires_payment` with no PaymentIntent and nothing picked it up again, so the period was lost. Each sweep now first re-offers every unbound scheduled payment (`deposit_rule_id` set, `requires_payment`, `provider_ref IS NULL`) as the SAME payment (`retry = true`), and `processDueDeposits` → new `scheduled-deposits.ts` `chargeScheduledDeposit` re-sends it under the same idempotency key `scheduled_deposit:<payment id>` — Stripe returns the intent it already made or makes it now, so the period is charged exactly once; the rule is not advanced again and no second run row is created. Lease: `updated_at` older than 15 minutes, re-stamped, `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` (index `idx_inbound_payments_unbound_deposits`). A run older than 23 hours is never re-sent (Stripe keeps a key 24 hours) — `failed` with an ops error row (`reason = 'unbound_expired'`), no strike; a run whose rule stopped or whose author, card or Customer no longer passes is `canceled` with a warn row (`unbound_canceled`). The re-authorisation moved into internal `finance.fn_deposit_charge_target` (revoked from every role). Only a declined card or required authentication counts a strike; transient, misconfigured and `StripeIdempotencyError` (key still in flight) leave the run for the next sweep; a bind that fails after Stripe created the intent leaves it pending and the next sweep binds the same intent; the sweep never throws (a refusal's failed bind used to abort it). No enum, column or Zod change. **VERIFIED:** `supabase/probes/settlement_reconcile_deposit_retry.sql` 12/12 PASS after `db:reset` (the agent reports it fails 6 checks against HEAD); `card_settlement_ledger.sql` region 5 amended, 12/12; `function_grants`, `scheduled_jobs`, `ledger_immutability` pass; `stripe-webhook.test.ts` (+2) and `scheduled-deposits.test.ts` (7); 178 finance unit tests pass; all re-run after `deno task db:reset`. **FLAGS for a human:** (a) a mismatched payment stays `processing`, so `PaymentsService.waitForSettlement` polls until its own timeout — a distinct status would need an enum value and UI handling; (b) there is no admin door to resolve a mismatch (credit by hand + set the status, or close the payment) and the alert channel is still only `ops.log_entries` + a WARNING (as #186(b)); (c) if an expired or canceled unbound run's first attempt did reach Stripe and succeed, its webhook finds no bound payment and 5xxs until Stripe gives up — the ops row says to search Stripe by `projective_payment_id`; a sweep reconciling against `paymentIntents.search` would close it; (d) `updated_at` doubles as the retry lease; (e) a re-check failure during a retry cancels the run but does not pause the rule until its next due run. | `supabase/migrations/{00001230_functions_finance_stripe,00001240_functions_finance_money_movement,00002510_permissions_function_grants,00003004_views_finance_integrations_analytics,00004005_indexes_finance}.sql` · `supabase/probes/{settlement_reconcile_deposit_retry,card_settlement_ledger}.sql` · `packages/backend/services/finance/{stripe-webhook.ts + test,scheduled-deposits.ts + test,live-payments.ts,PaymentBackendService.ts}` · `documentation/database/{finance/{Functions,Tables},ops/Tables}.md` · `SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE.md` §Stripe + scheduled jobs · `PRODUCT_MANAGEMENT.md` (Inbound card payment, Recurring deposit rule) · Decisions #126 / #186 / #200 / #203 |
| 216 | **An operator rules from a page, and a case under review has an SLA that alerts but never rules (2026-10-13).** Post-wave-7 gap closure item 2. RESOLVES #212 flags (b) and (c). **(A) THE DESK.** `/admin/disputes` (`(dashboard)`, under the already-reserved `admin` handle) is the platform operator's dispute queue. Its gate is its read: new `finance.list_disputes_for_review(p_status)` refuses anyone but `security.is_admin()` (`ops.admin_users`) with `42501`, `DisputeBackendService.queue` maps that to 403, and the controller answers 401 / 403 / 404 / 503 with a 404, so a non-operator never learns the desk exists or receives a ruling control (§3 gate 11); `resolve_dispute` refuses them too. Live cases are listed oldest-due first (`under_review` by `review_by`, `awaiting_response` by `respond_by`, then `open`; at most 200) under tabs Under review · Awaiting response · Open · All live with whole-set counts; rows show the parties, the amount held, the deadline that applies, an **Overdue** tag past the SLA, and statement/evidence counts. A case opens in `DisputeRulingDialog` over the queue and is read and ruled through the existing `GET /api/finance/disputes/:id` and `POST …/resolve` — no dispute is addressed by URL, so no `dsp-` slug was minted (#88). The form offers release · refund · split (share entered in %, sent as basis points — no money math in the island) and reasons (3–2,000 characters); **Record ruling** confirms first; the queue re-reads after a ruling. New thin `GET /api/finance/disputes[?status=]` (`DisputeQueueQuerySchema`), Zod `DisputeQueue*` in `packages/types/finance/disputes.ts`, `features/admin/` (`DisputeDeskScreen`, `DisputeDesk.island`, `DisputeRulingDialog`, `core/dispute-desk.ts` + test, `DisputeDeskService`, `operator-preview`, `styles/dispute-desk.css`), DESIGN_SYSTEM §C.1. Dev Context Switcher axis **Operator desk** `operatorDesk` (auto · queue · empty; `pj.dev.operator`, development builds only) renders sample cases and grants nothing — a ruling on a sample still reaches the database, which refuses it. **(B) THE REVIEW SLA.** `finance.disputes` gains `review_by`, `review_alerted_at` and `ck_disputes_review_deadline` (`under_review` requires `review_by`; 00000017 edited in place; partial index `idx_disputes_review_by`). Every way into `under_review` — the respondent's statement, a party's escalation, the sweep's auto-escalation — stamps `review_by = then + dispute_review_hours` (**72**, 00005001) via `finance.fn_dispute_review_by`. Per #212(c) the platform never rules on money without a human, so the SLA is a target, not a transition: `finance.sweep_disputes` step (d) leaves an overdue case under review with its escrow held, writes one `ops.log_entries` warn row (`client_id 'dispute:review_overdue:<id>'`), notifies every operator with new catalog key `dispute.review_overdue` (→ `/admin/disputes`; `00005010` + `catalog.ts`), at most once a day per case (`review_alerted_at`), raises a WARNING and returns `overdue`. `get_dispute` carries `review_by`. **(C)** The stale `/api/disputes/…` comments in `disputes.ts` now name `/api/finance/disputes/:id/…`, and the evidence-file comment names `/api/files/object/:id`. **VERIFIED:** `supabase/probes/dispute_review_queue.sql` 30/30 and `dispute_resolution.sql` 59/59 after `deno task db:reset`; (b) there is no nav entry for the desk (the shell has no `isAdmin` fact) — operators reach it by URL or the overdue notification; (c) operators are told only when a case goes overdue, not when it first enters review; (d) the overdue alert repeats daily until someone rules — whether to escalate to a second tier is a product call; (e) parties are deliberately not shown the review deadline; (f) PRODUCT_SPEC's party-facing `/disputes` pages remain unbuilt; | 217 | **Catalogue rebuild — console views, item workspace tabs, 6-slot showcase, archetype forms, scheduling presets with RRULE recurrence, listing analytics and client pipeline — RESOLVED (2026-10-14).** Plan: `documentation/flows/CATALOGUE_REBUILD.md` (§2 lists the collisions); work orders C0–C17 in `documentation/flows/CATALOGUE_REBUILD_BUILD_PROMPTS.md`. Logged by C0, which writes no code. AMENDS #53 (the deep analytics dashboard is no longer "a separate future surface": Analytics and Clients become catalogue tabs and views, and the bespoke `listing_availability` editor gives way to scheduling presets), #56 (named availability presets and compiled occurrences sit above the weekly bands) and #108 (the `ServiceCreation.md` §1.1 intake editor is built, inside the item workspace). **FLAGS for a human (each with its proposal):** (a) **Delivery-model vocabulary** — the brief's `pipeline` / `one_off` / `session` / `cohort` / `product` is not the enum (`marketplace.service_delivery_model` is `pipeline`, `one_off`, `direct_deliverable`, `session`, `group_session`; products are `catalogue.listing_kind = 'product'`). Proposal: never fork the enum; form archetypes come from `resolveArchetype()` (`direct_deliverable` → the `one_off` form plus a Team-roles section, `group_session` → the `cohort` form) through one pure `formArchetypeOf(kind, serviceType)` in `@projective/types/catalogue`. (b) **#53** keeps deep analytics a separate future surface and gives the catalogue its own availability editor. Proposal: amend #53 — Analytics and Clients become catalogue tabs and views; availability moves to presets (c). (c) **Per-service availability vs one schedule per owner** — `scheduling.schedules` is `UNIQUE (owner_type, owner_id)` and `listing_availability` is a parallel model the booking gate never reads. Proposal: named rule sets in `scheduling.availability_presets` under the owner's schedule; a blueprint binds one with `availability_preset_id` (NULL = the schedule's default); "Override" forks a service-scoped preset (`blueprint_id` set); `listing_availability` removed in place. (d) **Recurrence authority** — `fn_band_covers` / `fn_slot_is_free` understand only weekday bands, and RRULE expansion in SQL would be a second evaluator. Proposal: one pure TypeScript evaluator (`@projective/types/scheduling/evaluate.ts`); the service compiles each preset into `scheduling.availability_occurrences` (timestamptz spans, rolling 120-day horizon) through a service-role door, extended nightly by a pg_cron job (#203 registry); the gate reads occurrences when the blueprint is bound to a preset and weekday bands otherwise; discovery calls and `/[handle]/availability` stay on weekly bands; the default preset's weekly-expressible rules are mirrored into `availability_rules`; retiring the weekly model is a follow-up. (e) **Media** — the brief asks for 6 file-only slots with video in slots 2–6; today there are 24, URLs are allowed and positions start at 0. Proposal: edit `catalogue.listing_media` in place (`position` 1–6, `file_id NOT NULL`, `url` dropped, `kind` image/video, `rendition_file_id`, `poster_file_id`); slot 1 must be an image and is mirrored to `service_blueprints.cover_file_id`; seeds and fixtures with more than 6 items or URL media are rewritten (`db:seed:generate`). (f) **Search tags** — the brief says at most 10; the schema allows 24 and tags are in neither fts nor embeddings. Proposal: cap at 10 (Zod + a DB count-trigger CHECK); tags enter `services_index` / `catalogue_index` fts at weight B and the embedding input text. (g) **Inquiries cannot be tied to a listing** — `comms.dm_threads` has no subject. Proposal: add `subject_listing_id uuid NULL → catalogue.listings` in place with `CHECK (subject_listing_id IS NULL OR kind = 'service_inquiry')`; the `/view` Contact path sets it. (h) **"Standing Impact"** has no definition in `PRODUCT_SPEC.md`. Proposal: the count of reviews and completions this listing contributed to the seller's Standing (`org/standing.ts`) in the listing's CREATE category over the window, shown as a count with a direction, never a fabricated score — or drop the tile. (i) **Product licences** — `catalogue.products.licence` is free text defaulting to `'standard'`; the brief asks for commercial / personal; buyer file delivery after purchase is specified nowhere. Proposal: CHECK `licence` to `personal` / `commercial` / `extended` and rewrite `standard` rows to `personal`; the workspace attaches library files and ZIP bundles to `file_manifest`; buyer fulfilment is out of scope (confirm shipped or log as a gap). (j) **Preview vs §3.11** — an owner previewing their listing must not be able to buy it. Proposal: `/catalogue/[id]/simulate` renders the real `EntityViewPage` in simulation mode inside an `<iframe>` (so the real `--bp-*` breakpoints apply to the desktop/mobile toggle); every CTA opens its real modal; the commit button reads "Simulated: nothing is booked or charged", shows the would-be outcome and never posts. (k) **Analytics privacy** — keyword impressions and per-item booking heatmaps. Proposal: aggregates only, never a buyer-level trail; a keyword row needs at least 5 distinct sessions (k-anonymity) and a rate at least 20 in its denominator; views land in `search.interest_events` (`'view'` added to its `event_type` CHECK in place; one per session per listing per day). (l) **`?scope=`** — proposal: meaningful only on `/catalogue` (`all`, `product`, `service`, `model:<delivery>`, `collection:<slug>`); a listing slug passed as scope 303s to `/catalogue/<slug>?tab=…`, so per-item analytics and clients have one home. (m) **Charts** — `@projective/ui` has none; §C.5 resolves "D3 (SVG/math) → Canvas2D → PIXI"; the wallet has a private `FlowAreaChart`. Proposal: promote it to `@projective/ui/display` (`AreaChart`, `FunnelChart`, `HeatmapGrid`, `Sparkline`, `StatTile`), SVG tier only, on the already-pinned `d3-scale` / `d3-shape` (math only), with the §C.1 roster updated. **No ruling needed (existing rules):** metric cards are unboxed stat tiles (§3.6); only lifecycle status chips are containers (§3.7); client-pipeline lanes are derived groupings over existing statuses; presets are archived, never deleted; minors (#208) see the scheduler and publish controls hidden behind one `InlineNotice`. **RULINGS (human, 2026-10-14):** (a) **as proposed** — map through `formArchetypeOf`, never fork the enum. (b) **as proposed** — #53 amended. (c) **as proposed** — presets under the owner's schedule; `listing_availability` removed in place. (d) **as proposed** — one TypeScript evaluator compiling to occurrences; discovery calls and `/[handle]/availability` stay on weekly bands; retiring the weekly model stays a follow-up. (e) **as proposed** — 6 file-only slots, `url` dropped, seeds and fixtures rewritten. (f) **as proposed** — cap 10, tags indexed in fts (weight B) and embeddings. (g) **as proposed** — `dm_threads.subject_listing_id` with its CHECK. (h) **as proposed** — Standing Impact is a count with a direction, as defined above; the tile stays. (i) **as proposed** — `licence` CHECK `personal` / `commercial` / `extended`, `standard` → `personal`, workspace attaches to `file_manifest`; buyer fulfilment out of scope and **logged as a gap**: `finance.place_wallet_order` (00001210) writes the download `finance.order_lines` row without `asset_id`, so `live-orders.ts` resolves every bought product to "The seller hasn't attached the files yet" — no prompt in C1–C17 closes it. | 218 | **Listing analytics are written and the client pipeline is one read — `analytics.fn_rollup_listings` (daily job `analytics-listing-rollups`), `fn_subject_visible('listing')`, and `catalogue.get_client_pipeline` with a narrow definer for the buyer's order lines (2026-10-14).** Work order C5 of #217. BUILDS ON #203 (job registry), #217 flags (g) / (k). **(A) ROLLUPS.** `analytics.daily_rollups` had no writer, so every catalogue figure was fabricated (`API_BACKLOG.md` P1 #33 gap (a), P2 #1). `analytics.fn_rollup_listings(p_day)` (definer, service role only) writes one UTC day as `subject_kind = 'listing'` rows: `listing.impressions` · `clicks` · `views` (distinct sessions) · `checkout_starts` · `orders` · `gross_minor {currency}` · `bookings` · `booking_slot {dow, hour}` in the seller's timezone (owning schedule → profile → UTC) · `query_impressions {q}` and `query_clicks {q}` (5+ distinct sessions only; `query_clicks` is ADDED beyond the brief because `KeywordRow.clicks` (C1) needs it) · `cancellations` (refunded / cancelled non-session lines by the order's last change, plus sittings cancelled). It upserts on the rollup key AND deletes the day's listing rows it no longer produces, so a re-run equals one fresh computation; `fn_backfill_listing_rollups(p_from, p_to)` loops days (≤ 2 years). Registered through the #203 seed (`00005060`), `SELECT analytics.fn_rollup_listings(current_date - 1)` daily 02:10 UTC; `scheduled_jobs.sql` expects it. Zod `ListingRollupMetric` + `LISTING_ROLLUP_DIMENSIONS`, pinned to the SQL (names, dimensions, the threshold = `KEYWORD_MIN_SESSIONS`) by `listing-rollups.contract.test.ts`. **(B) VISIBILITY.** `fn_subject_visible` treated `listing` as admin-only; it now answers through new `catalogue.fn_manages_listing`: the owner of a personal listing, or a holder of `publish_listings` on the owning team (a plain member does not see a team listing's analytics — narrower than `get_listing_sales`, which admits any active member). **(C) PIPELINE.** `catalogue.get_client_pipeline(p_listing, p_limit)` is INVOKER over six arms (inquiry thread with `subject_listing_id`, quote request, discovery call, order line, session seat, pipeline project), returning each source table's OWN status (lanes stay in `laneOf`), `action_reasons` (`inquiry_unanswered`, `reschedule_open`, `handshake_pending`, `dispute_open`, `revision_requested`, `submission_pending` = a drafted, unsent submission) and `href`. **RULED by the human (2026-10-14):** the buyer's `finance.orders` / `order_lines` are invisible to a seller under RLS, so the order arm reads through a NARROW definer, `catalogue.fn_seller_order_lines` (buyer, order status and instants, line total, currency — never payment method, card, promo, totals or other sellers' lines), rather than new seller policies on the buyer's whole order row. `catalogue.fn_candidate_listings` (definer) finds the caller's listings by index. `ClientEngagementRowSchema` mirrors the columns (contract test); `ClientEngagement` gains `nextEventAt` and renames `updatedAt` → `lastActivityAt`. **(D) INDEXES.** Every arm checked with `EXPLAIN` under `enable_seqscan = off` on seeded data; seven sequential scans closed: listings by owner / owning team and quote requests by blueprint (new `00004012_indexes_catalogue_marketplace.sql` — neither domain had an index file), order lines by item and three day-range indexes for the rollup (`00004005`), open reschedule rounds and a sitting's calendar mirror (`00004007`), open revision requests (`00004003`). **VERIFIED** on a private stack (`main` + C3's `00000016` / `00000018` / `00000033` + this change): `supabase/probes/listing_analytics.sql` 34/34 (every metric, both day edges, idempotency, stale-row removal, k at 4 → 5, backfill, grants, the job, owner / stranger / team manager / plain member reads, one row per arm for the seller and none for the buyer or a stranger); `function_grants` and `scheduled_jobs` probes pass. **FLAGGED — for a human:** (a) **`href` is not slug-only.** DM threads and calendar events have no slugs, so the inquiry arm links `/messages/<thread uuid>` (ROUTING's own conversation address), quotes and orders `/messages/dm-<handle>`, and calls / sittings `/calendar?event=<event uuid>` — and the calendar does not yet open an event from `?event=` (its modal stack is not URL-synced); C16 must wire it or change the link. (b) **No app path writes `view` events yet**: C3 added `'view'` to the CHECK and the rollup counts distinct sessions, but neither `/view` nor the discovery beacon (`interest-rows.ts`) emits one; no C-prompt names the writer. (c) The plan's seed row ("90 days of synthetic interest events, then the backfill") is not in C5's work order and was not done; seeded analytics stay empty until it is. (d) An order placed by a team, business or enterprise has no `buyer_user_id` (orders record no placing user). (e) A seller sees a buyer's instantiated pipeline project only once engaged on it (`has_project_access`); a draft nobody has invited them to is invisible, by RLS. (f) The shared local DB has none of this DDL until `deno task db:reset`. (g) No Dev Context Switcher axis here: C17 owns `catalogueAnalytics`. | `supabase/migrations/{00001170_functions_catalogue,00001600_functions_analytics,00002510_permissions_function_grants,00004003_indexes_projects,00004005_indexes_finance,00004007_indexes_scheduling,00004012_indexes_catalogue_marketplace,00005060_seed_scheduled_jobs}.sql` · `supabase/probes/{listing_analytics,scheduled_jobs}.sql` · `packages/types/analytics/{events.ts,listing-rollups.contract.test.ts}` · `packages/types/catalogue/{clients.ts,clients.test.ts,client-pipeline.contract.test.ts}` · `documentation/database/{analytics/{Tables,Functions,Policies},catalogue/Functions}.md` · `SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE.md` §Scheduled jobs · `API_BACKLOG.md` · Decisions #203 / #217 |
| 219 | **The buyer simulation is built: `/catalogue/[id]/simulate` renders the real `EntityViewPage` in `simulation` mode, read as the owner, in `bare` chrome, framed only by its own origin; every commit goes through one gate that never reaches the network (2026-10-14).** Work order C14 of #217, flag (j). **(A) ROUTE.** `features/catalogue/routes/simulate.tsx` (handler in `simulate-handler.ts`): a non-slug id is a 404 before any read. `CatalogueBackendService.previewView` reads the listing as its owner, a draft included; a stranger's listing is a 404, worded as a missing one; an unreachable marketplace is a 503. `resolveEntityCommerce` gains `simulation: { owner }`, which calls the new `BookingBackendService.simulatedOffer` (the real offer for a signed-in buyer holding no draft; `offer()` and it share one `composeOffer`) and `ScheduleBackendService.simulatedEntitySchedule`. The Book modal's grid comes from the new `GET /api/catalogue/simulate/slots` (`BookingBackendService.simulatedSlots`), a read. No interest event is recorded. **(B) CHROME.** `ShellChrome` gains `"bare"` (§D.6): no top bar, rail, lane, bands, `BottomNav` or skip link. The dashboard layout resolves no slots for it and mounts none of its floating hosts. **(C) FRAMING.** A third CSP profile, `framed`: the platform policy with `frame-ancestors 'self'`, sent on every simulate answer. The middleware sends `x-frame-options: SAMEORIGIN` only when a response's policy is EXACTLY that header, and `DENY` otherwise (`frameOptionsFor`). Inside the frame, `PreviewFrameBridge` opens any link that leaves the page in a new tab, and hands the scroll position across reloads (`pj:preview-scroll`: report · ready · restore). **(D) THE GATE.** `features/view/core/simulation.ts` (`commitOrSimulate`, `simulatedOutcomeFor`) and `booking-commits.ts` route every buyer write through one function: pipeline draft, archive, session or seat, scoped brief, quote, question, discovery call, basket, instant checkout, plus the lane menu's save · collection · share. In a simulation the live call is never made; the outcome ("A buyer would now pay £120.00 into escrow at checkout.") is announced and shown beside the control (`data-simulation-outcome`). `booking-commits.test.ts` stubs `fetch`, presses every commit and asserts no request to `/api/(basket\|checkout\|booking\|services)`; with the simulation off the same commits reach their endpoints. The owner's own basket is never read. **(E) PREVIEW TAB.** `PreviewCanvas`: device `SelectButton` → `?device=`, a 1280px desktop frame scaled by `--cat-preview-scale` (pure `previewScale`, written from a ResizeObserver), a 390 × 844 phone in a token-drawn bezel, reload 1.5 s after `saving → saved`, and "Open in a new tab". **VERIFIED** in the browser as `marisdelacroix` on a DRAFT session listing: own listing 200 + SAMEORIGIN, a stranger's 404, the rest of the app still DENY; the framed Book session modal drew 6 slots and committed with zero basket/checkout/booking/services requests; scroll 480 → 480 across a reload; Mobile laid out at 390 × 844 with the buy bar in place of the lane. **FLAGGED — for a human:** (a) **Commit label.** The C14 prompt says a commit "keeps its label and tier"; ruling (j) says it "reads 'Simulated: nothing is booked or charged'". The ruling was followed for every MODAL commit (Book, Request call, Continue, Send request, the question's Send). The page-level CTAs keep their real label and tier — including the three that commit directly (Add to projects, Add to basket, Buy now) — so the page is what a buyer sees; they resolve locally and show the outcome. (b) **Buy now opens no panel in a simulation.** Opening `BuyNowModal` IS a write (it adds the basket line and opens a checkout session on mount), so "every CTA opens its real modal" cannot hold for instant checkout. It resolves on press instead. (c) **Recommendation rails are left out** of the simulation (their cards navigate away from the listing); reviews stay. (d) **Security.** `framed` + `SAMEORIGIN` loosens the "non-negotiable" `x-frame-options` floor for one route; ruling (j) requires it. (e) The scheduler stage's in-calendar booking (`ScheduleView` `onBook`) was already session-local with no network call, and still paints "You're booked" in a simulation. (f) A restricted (13–17) owner's simulation shows the adult buyer's page, not the `RestrictedLaneNotice`. (g) The dev-only Dev Tools launcher renders inside the frame (C17 owns devtools). (h) In the hidden built-in browser pane, a frame present at first paint did not start loading until its `src` was set again; not seen in a visible window. C17's Playwright run should confirm. | `apps/web/features/catalogue/{routes/{simulate,simulate-handler,simulate-handler.test}.ts(x),core/{simulate-path,preview-model,preview-model.test}.ts,components/preview/PreviewCanvas.tsx,islands/{PreviewFrameBridge,ListingWorkspace}.island.tsx,styles/preview.css}` · `apps/web/features/view/{core/{simulation,booking-commits}(.test).ts,core/booking-ssr.ts,components/{EntityViewPage,BookingCtaRig,SimulationNotices}.tsx,islands/*}` · `apps/web/routes/{(dashboard)/{_layout,catalogue/[id]/simulate}.tsx,api/catalogue/simulate/slots.ts,_middleware.ts}` · `apps/web/utils/csp(.test).ts` · `packages/ui/navigation/{components/AppShell.tsx,styles/app-shell.css}` · `packages/backend/services/{booking/BookingBackendService,scheduling/ScheduleBackendService}.ts` · `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §C.1 / §D.6 · `SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE.md` §Runtime & API Security · `ROUTING.md` · Decision #217 (j) |
| 220 | **Catalogue chrome alignment: one header band for `/catalogue` and `/catalogue/[id]`, lane facets with counts, the circular selection check, and analytics that always draw their charts (2026-10-15). AMENDS DESIGN_SYSTEM §B.6.2 at the product owner's direction; REFINES #217.** The redesign brief asked for counts on the status facets; §B.6.2 and CATALOGUE_REBUILD §3.1 said presence dots, never counts. Asked and ruled: **counts, as plain text**. It also read the lane's ⚠ ★ ↗ `LaneToggleRow` as per-item alerts; they were the console's quick filters. Asked and ruled: **move them into the facets**. **(A) HEADER BAND.** `CatalogueHeader` and `ListingHeader` share `.cat-band` (`styles/header-band.css`), the `chan-header` three-region flow: identity at the start (title over a meta line), the `TabMenu` centred between two `flex: 1 1 0` regions, actions at the end. Inside the band the TabMenu loses its own border and sliding ink; each tab draws a 2px `::after` underline (`scaleX`, `--text-sm` `--fw-medium`) at `inset-block-end: -1px`, over the page canvas's block-start hairline. The scroll box gets 1px of padding and a -1px margin so it does not clip that pixel. The console's end region holds search (and, below `--bp-md`, the type segment); its meta line is the count, then the rollups. The workspace starts with the title over `slug · status Tag · save state` (Back is the lane head's, below); `.cat-ws-head__id` is the slug in the meta register (`inline-flex`, `line-height: 1`, `tabular-nums`). It ends with the Card toggle as a 32px circular icon button (`panel-right`, `data-on` while the panel is shown). **(B) LANE.** Console: `LaneTabs` alone in the head; the lane `SortControl` is deleted (sort is the footer rig's only); the `LaneToggleRow` is deleted. Facets (`CatalogueFilterPanel`): Status · Highlights (the three former quick filters, which combine) · Delivery model · CREATE category. Each row shows its glyph, label, a right-aligned count and a `SelectionCheck` (hollow on hover, solid while on), on a solid `--surface-2` fill (no translucent wash, §B.11.6). Outline mode (owner correction, same day): a `.cat-lane__head` row at `--shell-midnav-header-h` holds Back to the console as the `/projects/[slug]` lane head's pill (arrow + "Back", `--text-secondary`, `--surface-2` on hover), so it sits level with the header band; `.cat-lane__pinned` is deleted and "Your listings" lists EVERY listing, the current one marked like the projects lane's active channel (`--surface-2`, a 2px `--on-surface` inline-start edge, semibold, `aria-current="page"`) so it no longer reads as merely hovered. **(C) COUNTS.** `CataloguePage.facetCounts` (`CatalogueFacetCountsSchema`: `model` and `create` partial records, `needsAttention`, `promoted`, `bestSelling`; default empty) is computed in `listListings` over the whole catalogue, as `statusCounts` already was. No DB change. **(D) GLYPHS.** Six `category-*` glyphs join `@projective/ui/icons` (§B.7.1): CREATE is platform taxonomy, not one feature's vocabulary. **(E) SELECTION CHECK.** New `@projective/ui/fields` `SelectionCheck` (20px `md` · 16px `sm`, decorative). The card select is now a `<button role="checkbox" aria-checked>` wrapping it, with Shift-range kept. **(F) ANALYTICS.** A successful read with nothing in it still renders `AreaChart` / `FunnelChart` / `HeatmapGrid` on zeros, with a centred non-interactive `InlineNotice` overlay. A funnel with some data but below `RATE_MIN_DENOMINATOR` keeps its sentence, because the rates would be noise. A failed read keeps the error and Retry and never shows a zero scaffold. **(G) PACKAGE.** `LaneSection`'s header now reads the §A.4 section-header tokens (`--text-xs`, `--fw-semibold`, `--tracking-wide`) instead of `0.72rem`/600/`0.03em`, for every lane. **(H) MONEY FIELDS** (owner direction, same day). Every catalogue money field — Pricing's price (incl. "Price per seat"), "Each extra round" and each Pipeline stage's "Ticket price" — is now the project setup form's control: `NumberInput` in `currency` mode with the `MONEY_FIELD` preset. The preset moves from `projects/components/setup/setup-format.ts`, which re-exports it, to `apps/web/utils/money-field.ts`, together with `moneyDigits(currency)` (exponent-aware fraction digits and the Ctrl precision step). Listing prices bind in their stored major units; stage `priceCents` converts through `toMajorUnits` / `toMinorUnits`. `pricing-model`'s `wholeOf` is deleted. `MoneyInput` has no consumers left (flag (g)). FLAGS: (a) withdrawn — Back returned to the lane head, as on `/projects/[slug]` (#133 (A)); (b) the list/table view keeps native checkboxes, including its indeterminate select-all; (c) below 36rem the console's search narrows to about its icon so the view tabs keep a 7rem scroller; (d) facet counts ignore the other active facets and the type tab, as status counts do; (e) the `apk-cell__tick` in the asset picker could adopt `SelectionCheck` and has not; (f) the `LaneSection` tracking change is visible on every lane; (g) `@projective/ui/fields` `MoneyInput` and its `core/money-input.ts` are now unused — delete them, or keep them for a non-stepper money field. | `apps/web/features/catalogue/{islands/{CatalogueLane,CatalogueHeader,ListingHeader}.island.tsx,components/{CatalogueFilterPanel,ListingCard,analytics/AnalyticsCanvas}.tsx,core/{console-store,catalogue-ssr,catalogue-lane-slot}.ts(x),styles/{header-band,console,workspace,lane,listing-card,analytics}.css}` · `packages/types/catalogue/catalogue.ts` · `packages/backend/services/catalogue/live-catalogue.ts` · `packages/ui/{fields/components/SelectionCheck.tsx,fields/styles/selection-check.css,fields/mod.ts,icons/core/paths.tsx,navigation/styles/lane.css}` · `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §B.6.2 / §B.7.1 / §C.1 · `flows/CATALOGUE_REBUILD.md` §3 |
| 221 | **Catalogue console filtering, toolbar and deletion — multi-select facets that open on everything but Archived, the sticky `.fx-toolbar` back on `/catalogue`, the band's meta split by what it answers, and a no-history hard delete beside archive (2026-10-15). At the product owner's direction; REFINES #217 and #220; APPLIES the #163 hard-delete carve-out from root §5 to catalogue listings.** **(A) FACETS.** `ConsoleUrlState.status` / `model` / `create` are lists (`?status=draft,paused`, comma-joined in canonical order, unknown values dropped, `parseConsoleUrl` total as before; a single-value link still parses). `status` is never empty and defaults to draft · published · paused — Archived off — a default `consoleHref` omits; an empty set normalises back to it. `CatalogueListParams.status` / `model` / `createCategory` are arrays, sent as repeated query params to `GET /api/catalogue/list` and filtered as sets in `listListings`. The lane rows use the file explorer's model, pure in `core/facet-select.ts` (`facetClickOf`, `pickFacet`, 7 tests): a press selects only that row and pressed again returns the facet to its fallback; Ctrl/Cmd toggles one; Shift selects the run from the facet's anchor; Ctrl/Cmd+Shift adds the run. One visually hidden hint is every row's `aria-describedby`. Status's header action is Reset, the others' Clear. **(B) TOOLBAR.** `components/CatalogueToolbar.tsx` renders the SHARED `.fx-toolbar` (`projects/styles/fx-toolbar.css`, imported as before 93020d92) at the top of the console canvas, pinned under the band (`--fx-chrome: var(--cat-chrome)`). The type is a bare `Select` (All · Products · Services) on every view; on Listings it adds the search (moved from `cat-hdr__search`), the match count (`aria-live`, moved from the band), and `SortControl` + New listing (moved from the footer's `cat-rig__end`; New listing below `--bp-md` only, as before, the lane footer holding it above). The console lane's `LaneTabs` head is removed (the primitive stays — Calendar, Messages and Projects use it) and the band's mobile type segment with it. The footer rig is the zoom alone (its unused `url` prop dropped). **(C) BAND META.** `cat-band__meta` held count · revenue · delta · orders · conversion · live · rating in one line. Now: the count is the toolbar's; under the title, `CatalogueHealth` ("9 of 14 live · Rated 4.8", middot meta text); at the band's end, `CatalogueSales`, an unboxed `<dl>` of gross-with-change, orders and conversion, each a `tabular-nums` figure over its label (the idblock's own shape), hidden progressively by the `cat-band` container (gross only under 64rem, none under 48rem). **(D) DELETE.** `catalogue.delete_listing(p_listing)` (DEFINER, creator only like `set_listing_status`) locks the subject, counts `catalogue.fn_listing_engagements` (internal, `service_role` only) and refuses `PE409` (DETAIL the count) above zero; else it deletes the listing (children cascade), its blueprint or product (minted for it alone), a product's `search.catalogue_index` row, and the subject's `org.user_bookmarks` / `org.recent_views` rows. `catalogue.get_listing_engagement(uuid[])` (DEFINER, ≤ 200, the caller's own listings only) feeds the new `ListingSummary.deletable` on the list AND the workspace detail. Engagement = inquiry threads, quote requests, discovery calls, instantiated projects, `service_instantiations`, waitlist places, basket and order lines of the subject's kinds, reviews and paid placements. Indexes `idx_discovery_calls_blueprint`, `idx_promoted_placements_entity`. Delete is `DELETE /api/catalogue/item?id=` → `CatalogueBackendService.remove` (409 `HISTORY_REFUSAL`), and the batch gains `{ action: "delete" }` (per-listing, refusals reported per row); Archive stays `POST /api/catalogue/status`, so the two never share a payload. Offered in the card menu, the batch bar (while any selected listing is deletable; the dialog counts the ones it will skip) and the workspace rig (a `text` danger action, then back to the console). Archive and Delete both confirm first through `components/ListingRemovalDialog.tsx`, each worded for what it does (archive: reversible, history kept; delete: permanent, `danger` accept). Archive is no longer styled `danger` in the card menu. VERIFIED in a rolled-back transaction on the shared DB (stranger 42501, engaged listing PE409, a service and a freshly created product deleted with their subjects and index row, the internal count not executable by `authenticated`), then the additive DDL applied with `NOTIFY pgrst`; 264 catalogue unit tests green (3 new write tests). **FLAGS for a human:** (a) **What counts as engagement** — views, saves and analytics events are NOT (a never-bought listing with views is deletable; its saves and recent views go with it); say if any should block. (b) The brief made every **draft** deletable; ruled here that a draft is deletable only with no history (a listing that was published, sold and moved back to draft is archive-only). (c) **Race**: count and delete share one transaction under a `FOR UPDATE` on the subject, but basket lines have no FK and checkout does not lock the subject, so a basket add committing between the count and the delete would dangle. (d) `analytics.daily_rollups` / `search.interest_events` rows for a deleted listing stay as unresolvable soft references. (e) Delete is **creator-only**, like every write door, while `fn_manages_listing` lets a `publish_listings` holder manage a team listing. (f) **Dev Context Switcher**: no new axis — `deletable` is derived from live data and the catalogue reads have no fixture branch; say if a `catalogueDeletable` override is wanted. (g) The brief asked for "chips" in the band; gate 7 (anti-tagification) keeps them inline text and unboxed figures. (h) This puts search back in the body toolbar, which #217's rebuild had deliberately moved into the band. (i) `fx-toolbar.css` is imported across features (projects → catalogue); a `packages/ui` home would be cleaner. (j) DONE by #222/#224: the card kebab is `ActionMenu` over the shared `core/listing-menu.ts` (also the table rows), still routed through `requestAction`, so both removals confirm. (k) BROWSER-VERIFIED on the live console as marisdelacroix (1440 and 390 wide): the defaults, Ctrl / plain / Shift presses and their URLs, a reload and Back keeping the set, the type dropdown, the pinned toolbar, the split band, Delete absent on engaged listings and present on a fresh one, both dialogs, and a real DELETE removing it; no page errors, no horizontal overflow. |
| 222 | **One menu per entity, however it is asked for: right-click, the ContextMenu key and Shift+F10 on a card or list item open the same rows as its kebab (2026-10-15).** Owner direction. **(A) PRIMITIVE.** `ActionMenu` gains `surface` (a ref or getter naming the card/row) and an optional `trigger`. A `contextmenu` on the surface opens the model at the pointer (`bottom-start`, RTL `bottom-end`, flipping and clamping through `useFloating` off a 0×0 `.ui-action-menu__point` anchor) or, when the ContextMenu key / Shift+F10 raised it, under the focused element; Escape returns focus to whatever held it before. The browser keeps the event in editable text, over a live selection, on Shift+right-click, and when an inner surface already took it (innermost wins) — the pure rule is `navigation/core/context-surface.ts`. A right-click inside any open `ActionMenu` or `ContextMenu` panel or flyout is swallowed (`preventDefault` + `stopPropagation`), so the native menu never opens over ours; outside press and Escape close through the existing `useDismiss` + escape stack. `ContextMenu`'s `global` binding now yields to a surface that took the event. **(B) LINK ROWS.** `linkActionItems` (`navigation/core/link-actions.ts`) builds Open · Open in new tab · Copy link · Share · Save for a linkable entity; Share appears only with an app share flow or the device share sheet, Save only with save state, and a failed device share falls back to copying (UI gate 11). `composeMenu` stacks groups with separators. **(C) ADOPTED.** `/projects` `ProjectCard` (AMENDS #140: Open · Open in new tab · Copy link · Share · Archive project; its hand-built `Popover` and the lane's `.proj-cardmenu` rules removed), the project roster's `MemberActionsMenu` (card and table row, surface found from the kebab), Explore cards (context-only — Open · Open in new tab · Copy link · Share · Save; still no kebab, per the `CardActions` ruling), messaging `ConversationRow` / `InboxRow` (`.conv-menu*` removed), and workspaces `MemberActionsMenu` + `WorkspaceKebab` on their cards and table rows (`.wsp-menu*` removed). FLAGS: (a) catalogue `ListingCard` adopted after #221 released it — its whole model is #224's shared `listingMenu` (Open in new tab · Copy link · Edit · … — Edit is the plain Open), so card and table row cannot drift, and Archive/Delete still confirm through the canvas's `requestAction`; its Copy link is silent until `listingMenu` threads an `onCopied`; (b) headers — the project sidebar header, channel and conversation headers, the ticket view, the wallet hero, the view lane — keep their kebabs without right-click: they are not cards or list items; (c) basket shelf cards stay kebab-less by their own ruling and get no context menu; (d) conversation Favourite / Mute lose `menuitemcheckbox` — `ActionMenuRows` renders only `menuitem`, so state rides the flipped label; (e) workspace member Copy link is silent until `MembersScreen` (mid-edit under #223) passes `onCopied`; (f) iOS Safari raises no `contextmenu`, so touch there keeps the kebab only; (g) the brief named an Angular directive — this stack is Preact, so the directive is the `surface` prop. | `packages/ui/navigation/{islands/{ActionMenu,ContextMenu}.tsx,core/{context-surface,link-actions}.ts(+tests),styles/action-menu.css,mod.ts}` · `apps/web/features/projects/{components/{ProjectCard,MemberActionsMenu}.tsx,islands/ProjectsLane.island.tsx,styles/projects-lane.css}` · `apps/web/features/explore/islands/CardActions.island.tsx` · `apps/web/features/messaging/{components/{ConversationMenu,ConversationRow,InboxRow}.tsx,islands/{InboxView,MessagesSidebar}.island.tsx,styles/messages.css}` · `apps/web/features/workspaces/{components/{MemberCard,MemberTable,WorkspaceCard,WorkspaceTable}.tsx,styles/workspace-tokens.css}` · `apps/web/features/catalogue/{components/ListingCard.tsx,styles/listing-card.css}` · `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §C.1 |
| 223 | **Zoom overhaul: one ZoomSlider, Ctrl/Cmd + wheel over the whole content viewport, continuous card scaling, and an interactive list ⇄ grid mode button (2026-10-15).** Owner direction. **(A) SLIDER.** `@projective/ui/fields` `ZoomSlider` is the only zoom slider. Its buttons are `zoom-out` / `zoom-in` lens glyphs on ghost-circle `Button`s (`iconOnly rounded text`) that step on press and repeat while held (`useHoldRepeat`; Ctrl/Cmd at press gives a fine step). The rail is muted with NO progress fill and the handle is neutral (`--on-surface`), never `--primary`. Segment and centre markers are optional (`segments={0}`, `center={null}`). Ctrl/Cmd + wheel steps on hover. `onStep` lets a non-linear surface keep its own step maths, and `onValueChange(v, source)` reports `"drag"` vs `"step"`. It replaces the crop stage's `pf-media__stepped` (`HoldStepButton` deleted) and the inspector image panel's ToolButtons + `ToolSlider`. The timeline rig picks up the new look unchanged. **(B) SMOOTHING.** `createZoomStore` adds `displayZoom`, the target `zoom` followed by `SPRING_STANDARD` (`@ui/core/motion.ts`). Layout reads `displayZoom`. Steps, held buttons and wheel notches ease; a drag, a pinch, a restore and the mode toggle land at once. This is the media viewers' instant-vs-animate split. Reduced motion jumps. `gridColWidth` is no longer rounded. **(C) CTRL + WHEEL.** `useCtrlWheelZoom` registers into ONE non-passive `document` listener. A gesture goes to the innermost registered element containing it, so a ticket tab in a dialog keeps its own zoom. Otherwise it goes to the latest surface mounted in `.ui-app-shell__content`, excluding `.ui-app-shell__sidebar`, `.ui-shell-topbar`, `.ui-middle-nav__lane` and `.ui-middle-nav__panel`, which keep the browser's behaviour. An already default-prevented gesture (a canvas viewer, the Gantt) is left alone. The step is proportional to the wheel delta (one 100px notch = `ZOOM_WHEEL_STEP`, capped at two notches per event). **(D) CONTINUOUS CARDS.** `VirtualGrid` gains `fit="exact"`: each cell is the zoom width itself (capped at the viewport), left-aligned. Cells are keyed by item and placed with `transform`, so a column-count change glides every mounted card from its old slot to its new one (WAAPI, `--dur-medium` / `--ease-standard`, none under either reduced-motion channel). The item at the top of the view stays anchored as rows rescale (`overflow-anchor: none` on the grid). The layout `Grid` gains the same `fit="exact"`. The members, ticket-tab, event-attachment and skeleton CSS grids move from `minmax(var(--x), 1fr)` to `min(var(--x), 100%)` tracks. **(E) MODE BUTTON.** `ui-viewrig__mode` is now a ghost-circle `Button` ("Switch to grid/list view") calling `store.toggleViewMode()`. That mirrors the zoom to the same relative density in the other half, instantly, so the slider and the view never disagree. Scroll is preserved by the bodies, which stay mounted. **(F) VERIFIED.** `deno task check` 0 errors; lint and fmt clean on the changed files; `deno task test:unit` 3,922 passed, only the four `theme-engine.test.ts` failures (#96(a)). Browser, `dev:mock` `/projects/prj-tm2bjk9mdq/files` at 1440px: one `.ui-zoom`, no fill, both buttons 32×32 `999px` radius with lens glyphs, handle `--on-surface`; Ctrl + wheel over the content and the footer stepped 0.62→0.66→0.70, over the lane and the main nav unchanged; a two-notch wheel eased the card width 183→210px over ~300ms through intermediate frames; a 900ms hold on zoom-out stepped 0.78→0.48 and crossed into list; the mode button toggled list 0.48 → grid 0.98 → list 0.48 instantly; no page errors. FLAGS: (a) this REVERSES the documented "the glyph is deliberately not a toggle" rule in `ViewZoomRig` / `zoom-store` and the view-state modules (comments updated); (b) exact-fit grids leave a trailing gap of less than one card at the inline end, the price of card sizes that never jump; (c) the crop steppers were `.pf-media__iconbtn`, which `global.css` exempts for a focus glow (#159(a)); as `.ui-zoom__btn` they follow the no-ring convention; (d) only `VirtualGrid` surfaces glide on reflow; the CSS-grid surfaces scale continuously but re-pack without a glide; (e) the inspector slider's Arrow keys now move 1/50 of the logarithmic range instead of 1/1000; (f) `core/motion.ts` is now imported from `apps/web` through the `@ui/` alias; (g) the DataTable work (#224) consumes `displayZoom` for list row heights. | `packages/ui/{fields/{islands/ZoomSlider.tsx,styles/zoom-slider.css},display/{islands/VirtualGrid.tsx,styles/virtual-grid.css},layout/{components/Grid.tsx,styles/grid.css}}` · `apps/web/utils/zoom-store.ts` · `apps/web/features/shell/{components/ViewZoomRig.tsx,hooks/useCtrlWheelZoom.ts,styles/view-zoom-rig.css}` · the four `view-state.ts` modules and their bodies · `files/components/media-crop/CropControls.tsx` · `inspector/components/viewers/image/ImageControls.tsx` · `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §C.1 · Decisions #32 / #159 |

| 224 | **One table for every tabular surface: `DataTable` in `@projective/ui/display` (2026-10-15). SUPERSEDES #34(B) ("Files/Submissions keep the bespoke `FileTable`") at the product owner's direction.** The brief: unify the tables on `/messages/[id]/files`, `/catalogue`, `/projects/[id]/files` and `/projects/[id]/submissions` behind one component that looks like the `/files` list, with typed cell formatters, None / Single / Multiple selection (Shift range, Ctrl/Cmd toggle, the circular catalogue check), virtualization for fixed and variable heights, infinite scroll, single and multi-column sort, column filters and search, resize and drag reorder, smooth on zoomable pages. The capability reference was Micronclean's Angular `lib-table` (features only); three of its defects were designed out: a selection anchor kept as an index (it pointed at another row after a re-sort), O(n) offset updates per measured row with no scroll anchoring, and a header reorder that moved the wrong column when some were hidden or pinned. Owner rulings (asked 2026-10-15): **(1)** Shift+click on a row body REPLACES the selection with the range (OS file-manager semantics), Ctrl/Cmd toggles, Ctrl+Shift adds the range; Shift on a check applies the clicked row's new state to the range (the catalogue's existing check behaviour). **(2)** Every header cycles asc → desc → none; "none" returns the `/files` hub to date-newest and the console to recently-edited, and on that default column the cycle just flips. **(3)** A plain click opens (`onRowActivate` / the row link); selection comes from the check, modifier keys and the keyboard. **(A) PACKAGE.** `islands/DataTable.tsx` (props, data pipeline, layout, render) over `components/DataTable{Head,Row,Cell,Filter,ColumnMenu}.tsx`, `hooks/useTableViewport.ts` (window or scroller; Fenwick-tree heights in `core/table-metrics.ts`; a row above the fold that grows scrolls the page by the same amount; a signal row height rescales the scroll so the top row holds through a zoom spring) and `hooks/useTableInput.ts` (delegated pointer + keyboard, roving row focus, the anchor), pure `core/table-{format,sort,selection,filter,columns,metrics}.ts` with 56 unit tests, `types/data-table.ts`, `styles/data-table.css` (the `/files` look on tokens: section-header register, one hairline per row, solid hover / selected mixes, sticky cells inheriting the row). Column types text · number · date · file · fileSize · image · status · tags · money (income / outgoing) · person · people · rating · duration · boolean · custom. **(B) ADOPTION.** `FileTable` is a preset (every file surface: the projects and messages explorers, Submissions, the `/files` hub, ticket and event attachments); `SubmissionNodeList` is a preset (headerless above the hub's files); the catalogue `ListingsTable` is a preset bound to the console store's `selection` + `selectionAnchor`, with the card's menu (`core/listing-menu.ts`, Archive / Delete through `requestAction`'s confirmation) on a kebab and right-click; `WorkspaceTable` (`workspaceMenu`), the workspace `MemberTable` (`memberMenuRows`) and `AnalyticsTables` are presets. The old `display/islands/Table.tsx` + `table.css`, `projects/styles/file-table.css`, `catalogue/styles/listing-table.css` and the `.subm-noderow` rules are deleted; `TableColumn` stays for `TreeTable`. The `/files` hub's list now selects like its grid, its header shows the live sort (the stale mirror signals are gone), and its sticky header pins under the top bar (its `--fx-chrome` calc was undefined there). `LocalKeys.FILES_COLUMNS` now holds a versioned `ColumnLayout`; `CATALOGUE_COLUMNS` is new. Verified in a headless browser on `/projects/[id]/files` (fixtures) and the live `/files` and `/catalogue`: header labels align with cells, sort / resize / persistence / chooser / row open, Ctrl + Shift + Ctrl+Shift selection, select-all and its mixed state, Escape, keyboard extend, kebab and right-click menus, the URL sort cycle, the row link; no page errors. FLAGS: (a) the `/files` hub re-parents its workspace into a `Splitter` when Inspect opens or closes, which remounts the table and drops keyboard focus (the grid has the same loss; the Shift anchor survives through `files-state.anchorId`) — a stable hub layout needs a collapsible `SplitterPanel`; (b) dates are formatted on the client from `createdAt` / `updatedAt` in `en-GB` and the browser's zone instead of the server's UTC labels, and the app does not yet pass the enterprise's date preference (`formatDate`); (c) the server-sorted file and catalogue tables enable no column filters, because their APIs have no per-column parameters (search and kind / status filters stay in the header bands); (d) `outgoing` money keeps `MoneyView`'s neutral debit tone, not red; (e) a narrow table hides columns by `priority` (Size before Sender on a 640px files pane), where the old file table overflowed; (f) hand-rolled tables outside this brief remain: `projects/components/MemberTable.tsx` (Members · Requests · Invitations), `TicketListView`, `EventFinanceTab`, `TicketFinanceTab`, wallet `CashFlow`, `NotificationsSection`, `RolesMatrix`, the inspector's `TableStage` and `entity-view-parts`; (g) deleting the package `Table` is a breaking change for any external copy of `@projective/ui`; (h) sticky cells take a row's new tone only after its 150ms fade. | `packages/ui/display/{islands/DataTable.tsx,components/DataTable{Head,Row,Cell,Filter,ColumnMenu}.tsx,hooks/{useTableViewport,useTableInput}.ts,core/table-{format,sort,selection,filter,columns,metrics}.ts (+ tests),types/data-table.ts,styles/data-table.css,mod.ts}` · `packages/ui/styles/index.css` · `apps/web/features/projects/{components/{FileTable,SubmissionNodeList}.tsx,hooks/useFileTiles.ts,styles/submission-card.css}` · `apps/web/features/files/{islands/FilesHub.island.tsx,core/files-state.ts}` · `apps/web/features/catalogue/{components/listings/{ListingsTable,ListingsCanvas}.tsx,components/analytics/AnalyticsTables.tsx,core/listing-menu.ts,islands/CatalogueConsole.island.tsx}` · `apps/web/features/workspaces/{components/{WorkspaceTable,WorkspaceCard,MemberTable,MemberCard}.tsx,styles/{workspace-roster,workspace-people}.css}` · `apps/web/utils/storage-keys.ts` · `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §C.1 / §C.5 · Decisions #32 / #34 / #67 / #220(b) / #222 / #223 |
| 225 | **Settings → Verification & payouts conformed to the Button Interaction Matrix, and the settings surface's status pills become inline status marks (2026-10-16). WIDENS Decision #157's tier-1 test by the product owner's direction: starting the personal payout account is tier 1.** **(A) Tier split, owner-ruled.** The brief asked for tier-1 amber on every financial / payout action of `/settings/verification`. DESIGN_SYSTEM §B.8.1 limits tier 1 to a commit inside a payment or conversion flow (or the act that starts one) and holds wallet configuration at tier 3, so the conflict was put to the owner, who ruled a SPLIT: the payout setup (*Set up payouts* / *Continue setup* — it creates the Connect account money is transferred to) is tier 1, `<Button severity="accent" rounded>`; the identity check (*Verify your identity* / *Try again* / *Verify again*) and a business's KYB (*Verify business* / *Continue verification*) are tier 3 `severity="primary"`. *Update payout details* on a verified account, *Refresh status* and every other action are tier-4 `neutral` outlined. **(B) One fill per view (§B.8.2).** `nextVerificationStep` (`verification-model.ts`) names the ONE filled action — a freelancer's identity check first, then the payout setup; nothing while the processor is not connected — and every other action renders tier 4. Business rows are repeated actions and never take the fill. **(C) Crests.** Each section's status is a `StatusMark` (inline Meta text + a per-tone glyph, §B.11.2) and an earned check shows its `VerificationStampBadge` crest in the glyph's place: `seal-check` for a verified identity, `shield-check` only when the identity AND the payout account are verified (`verificationStamps`; `VerificationStatus.payoutReady` is the account half only, so it never earns the crest alone — the rule `finance.fn_freelancer_payout_ready` and the standing stamp apply), `keystone-check` for a verified business. **(D) Kept as built, owner-ruled.** The Connect `return_url` / `refresh_url` stay `/api/finance/connect/return|refresh`, which re-read the account from Stripe and land on `/settings/verification?payouts=|?kyb=<status>` (the brief's `?status=return|refresh` was declined); identity checks stay on `POST /api/finance/verify/kyc`, the settings-return twin of `POST /api/finance/identity/session`. **(E) Settings-wide.** The `.stg-pill` family (Primary · Verified · Unverified · Default · Current · Acting as, filled with a `--primary` / `--warning` colour-mix wash §B.11.6 forbids) is deleted for `StatusMark`; teal outlined utility controls become tier-4 `neutral` outlined (Keep my freelancer profile · Become a freelancer · Connect · Email me a link · Keep my account · Resume now · Act as); the handle form's *Change and lock* drops its filled `warning` severity (outside the matrix's three fill tiers) for tier-3 primary, its hint already stating the lock. FLAGS: (a) the KYB start stays tier 3 although it opens a Stripe Connect onboarding like the payout setup — by ruling, because a business's KYB gates its vault rather than receiving money; (b) the meta rows (email, card, plan) separate marks by gap, not the §B.11.2 middot. | `apps/web/features/settings/{islands/VerificationConsole.island.tsx,core/verification-model.ts (+ test),components/SettingsParts.tsx,components/sections/{BillingSection,NotificationsSection,WorkspacesSection}.tsx,components/sections/account/{AccountTypeBlock,ConnectedAccountsBlock,DeleteAccountBlock,EmailsBlock,HandleBlock,PasswordBlock}.tsx,components/sections/billing/PlanBlock.tsx,styles/{settings,verification}.css}` · `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §B.8.1 · Decisions #126 / #155 / #157 / #196 |
| 226 | **Teams surfaces audit (`/teams`, `/teams/:teamHandle`) — new doc conflicts and gaps FLAGGED for a human (2026-10-16).** Full audit, defect log, refactor plan and proposals: `documentation/architecture/TEAMS_AUDIT.md`. No code changed. **FLAGS (each with a proposal):** (a) **Role vocabulary** — `PRODUCT_SPEC.md:189-198` names a `Contributor` role and an RBAC table that matches neither the DB presets (`owner · admin · lead · member`) nor their bundles. *Proposal:* the DB/types vocabulary wins (technical SSOT); rewrite the spec table from `database/org/Functions.md:604`; a contributor is a custom role on `base_preset = member`. (b) **Who edits the public team profile** — `PRODUCT_SPEC.md:591` says owner or lead; `fn_profile_manages` grants `edit_profile` (owner + admin). *Proposal:* follow the DB. (c) **Restore target** — `PRODUCT_MANAGEMENT.md:214` allows restore to `draft` but also says a published entity never returns to draft. *Proposal:* restore returns to `active` if the entity was ever published, else `draft`. (d) **Split templates have no writer** — `finance.split_rules` is seeded only; `save_team_split` writes stakes. *Proposal:* `finance.save_split_rule` (audit §4.4); which templates are Pro-only ("Advanced vault splits", `finance-model.md:482`) needs a ruling. (e) **Vault projection has no backstop trigger** on `team_members` / `team_roles`. *Proposal:* add one (audit §4.4). (f) **Team member preset is `view_analytics` only** after the kind filter — intended? (g) **Operator mode** — no Teams gate in DB or app; the spec only says freelancer-only. *Proposal:* hide in operator mode like client. (h) **Dead team W_i** — `org.teams.current_workload_intensity` has no writer. *Proposal:* drop from the view's team figure or derive from members. (i) **Route table** — `PRODUCT_SPEC.md:2975-2981` still lists `/teams/[team id]` with a `vault` module. *Proposal:* replace with `ROUTING.md:98-101` and the `ModuleKey` list. (j) **Mis-cited decision** — the 2026-09-28 live console is cited as "Decision #122" in five docs, but #122 is the wallet. *Proposal:* re-cite to #61 or log the change. Also found (not doc conflicts): apply paths ignore team `status`; `apply_to_seat` lacks the two-member check; uuid→handle canonicalisation answers 303, not 308; Create Team offered to client contexts. |
| 227 | **One verification crest per owner, everywhere — `/[handle]` and `/explore` draw the same mark in the same ink (2026-10-12). RESOLVES Decision #155(g); AMENDS DESIGN_SYSTEM §B.7.8 / §C.1.** Bug report: the verified badge differed between the profile page and the explore feed. Audit: both already rendered the one crest component (`VerificationStampBadge`), so the disparity was in what they FED it and how they INKED it. **(A) THE GLYPH.** The profile read (`org.get_profile_view`) carries the owner's stamp, so a payout-verified freelancer showed `shield-check` on their profile; every discovery surface knew only `verified` + kind and drew `seal-check` (#155(g)). `org.profiles_index` (00003001) now APPENDS `verification_stamp` under the profile read's own rule — a person's `org.entity_standing.verification_stamp` (`none` without a row, read whether or not they sell), a business's `corporate_verified` from its KYB, `none` for a team — and `ExploreOwnerSchema` gains optional `verificationStamp`, filled by the live catalog, the review bylines (`authorOf`, the testimonials read) through one `stampOf` parser (an unreadable value is ABSENT, not `none`). **(B) ONE RESOLVER.** `ownerVerificationStamp(owner)` (`@projective/types/org`) — the projected stamp, else `stampForVerifiedOwner` — is the only path from an owner attribution to a crest: the app's `VerifiedBadge` now takes the OWNER (not `kind` + a guarded boolean) and every direct `VerificationStampBadge` call site in the entity view (lane, seller line, sticky header) uses the resolver. **(C) THE INK.** `.pf .ex-verified { color: var(--on-surface) }` monochromed the crest on the explore cards reused inside the profile, so the same owner's crest was teal on `/explore`, teal in the profile hero and black on a card one scroll below it; removed — the ink belongs to `verification-stamp.css` alone and a call site sets only the knockout ground and the size. The dead `.pf-stickyhead__verified` rule (no renderer; a `--primary` ink over an `--on-primary` knockout, the wrong ground) is deleted. **(D) FIXTURES.** The four verified explore fixture owners carry explicit stamps (one payout, one corporate), and the profile fixture's crest now prefers the discovery row's stamp, so mock mode keeps the parity live data has; an UNVERIFIED fixture business no longer claims `corporate_verified` (live never does). **Not changed:** the certification and notable-client marks (`ExperienceTab`, `ClientProofStrip`, `ListEditors`) are platform-check marks on a credential or a client, not an owner's crest (#155(i) stays open). **Flagged:** (a) the migration was not applied to a database in this session — `deno task db:reset` is needed before the live column exists, and until then the live explore read fails on the unknown column, as any appended view column does; (b) `ExploreView.ownerVerified` (`packages/types/explore/view.ts`) has no renderer — retire or re-home alongside #155(j). | DESIGN_SYSTEM §B.7.8 · §C.1 · `database/org/Tables.md` (`profiles_index`) · `supabase/migrations/00003001_views_profiles_business.sql` · `packages/types/{explore/items.ts,org/standing.ts}` · `packages/backend/services/{explore/{live-catalog,live-view,ExploreBackendService,fixtures}.ts,profile/profile-fixtures.ts}` · `apps/web/features/{explore/components/{VerifiedBadge,OwnerBadge,cards/ProfileCard}.tsx,view/**,marketing/**,profile/styles/{profile-work,profile-skeleton,profile-band}.css}` |

| 230 | **Businesses owner rulings — who may create a business, roles and the manager cap, the pool-short rule, an owner-granted billing right, Pro gates behind a switch that ships on, `businesses_owned` 3, business visibility, the Talent bench and the business hue (2026-10-16). RESOLVES Decision #229 BX-a, BX-b, BX-c, BX-d, BX-e, BX-f, BX-h, BX-i, BX-j and Decision #61 (a), (d).** Source: the product owner's answers to `architecture/BUSINESSES_BUILD_PROMPTS.md` §BZ0, put to them as one message on 2026-10-16; evidence in `architecture/BUSINESSES_AUDIT.md` §1 / §4. Recorded as #230 because the Teams owner rulings took #228 when the two branches merged, moving the audit to #229; BZ0's "row 229" is this row, and the BZ/BB/BU/BP series is renumbered with it. #229 has no flag (g). Docs only, plus one comment correction; each ruling is built by the prompt that cites it. **OWNER RULINGS (question → answer quoted verbatim → what it settles).** **(a) Who may create a business?** [1] any adult account · [2] client persona only · [3] Operator Mode only; and, with [1], may operator accounts create one too? → "As recommended" → **[1], operators included**: any adult account — client, freelancer or operator — may create and join a business. A business is buyer-side, so Teams #228 (A)'s operator refusal does not carry over. PRODUCT_SPEC's Client / Operator Mode gate is retired; dropping the app's `isFreelancer` gate is BB1's. Resolves #61 (a). **(b) Business roles** — [1] keep owner · admin · member, "Project Manager" / "Observer" as custom-role templates, `business_managers` counting admins + custom roles holding `manage_projects` · [2] add PM / Observer presets → "As recommended - businesses are also able to hire freelancers for temporary or contractual roles for project management both internally on projective and externally on third parties (currently out of scope for the MVP as it requires integrations and extensions with third parties and may require businesses to share credientials in some way, eventually this will tie in with another future implementation for tooling for VA's)" → **[1]**. The contracted project-manager role (a hired freelancer managing the business's projects on Projective or on third-party tools) is recorded in PRODUCT_SPEC as **post-MVP**; nothing in this series builds it. **(c) Pool short at checkout or funding (#61 d)** — [1] refuse with a one-click Contribute prompt for `contribute_funds` holders, never a personal card · [2] fall back to on-account credit · [3] both, on-account first → "As recommended" → **[1]**. Resolves #61 (d). **(d) Billing and Verification modules** — [1] gate both on `manage_billing` and keep it owner-only · [2] add `manage_billing` to the admin preset → "The business owner must give explicit specific permission to admins, business owners can give their exact same permissions to an admin, essentially making them a co-owner, if they want but with explicit warnings." → **neither option as put.** Both modules are gated on `manage_billing` (as [1]); the admin PRESET does not gain it (not [2]); the owner may grant it to a named admin, explicitly and behind a warning, and may grant an admin the owner's own permission set ("co-owner") behind an explicit warning. Today the right is not grantable: `org.fn_vault_capabilities_for` derives `manage_billing` (with `manage_members`) from `owner_user_id` alone (`00001020`), so BB6 adds the grant — owner-grantable only, a schema change landing with its Zod type and `database/*`. A co-owner is an admin seat holding the owner's grants, never a second owner seat: `fn_member_role_sync`'s single-owner invariant (`23514`) stands. Business-only (`kind === "business"`); a team's `manage_billing` is unchanged. **(e) Plan gates** — are intervaled invoicing and spending caps (`pooled_wallet_full`) Pro-only, as finance-model §16.1 says? [1] yes, enforce now · [2] free during the MVP, enforce later → "Enforce now for development testing and future proofing but place in a flag to enable or disable for dev and production." → **[1], behind a switch**: monthly invoicing terms (`intervaled_invoicing`) and per-member ceilings or an approval threshold (`pooled_wallet_full`) are refused without Business Pro (`PK402`), behind one `security.platform_params` switch (BB7 names it) that is **seeded on** and set per environment. This is deliberately the reverse of the fail-open family (`proposal_allowance_enforced`, `footprint_caps_enforced`, `storage_quota_enforced`, all seeded `false`). The switch is a new simulatable axis, so BB7 also adds its Dev Context Switcher control (root §5). **(f) `businesses_owned` on personal Pro** — 3 (the table) or 5 (finance-model §1.3)? → "3 as the table says" → **3**. finance-model §1.3's "5 businesses" is corrected; the seed (`00005030`, Individual Pro) already says 3. **(h) Business visibility** — [1] add `visibility` like teams · [2] every active business stays listed → "As recommended" → **[1]**: `org.business_profiles` gains a team's visibility values (`public` · `unlisted` · `invite_only`), read by the directory in place of `true as listed` (BB5). **(i) Talent module** — [1] build the bench · [2] remove it → "As recommened" → **[1]**: the freelancers and teams this business has hired, with re-hire and message actions (BU5). **(j) Business kind hue** — [1] anchor business marks to `--tertiary` in every mode · [2] keep `--secondary` with an inverse halo on the focus ring → "As recommended" → **[1]**: `--wsp-business` is `--tertiary` in every mode — today only the colour-vision overlay's value (`workspace-tokens.css`). BU1 builds it and updates DESIGN_SYSTEM. **FACTUAL CORRECTIONS (not rulings — stale prose aligned with the code).** PRODUCT_SPEC §Sitemap's `/business` rows become `/businesses`, `/businesses/create` and `/businesses/[businessHandle]` with the kind-business modules of `features/workspaces/core/module-registry.tsx`; PRODUCT_SPEC's Intervaled Invoicing summary now says released escrow with the fee shown, not added, as §Escrow, Wallets & Finance's Consolidated Invoice already did; `database/finance/Tables.md`'s "individual clients cannot lock escrow by card yet" note and `database/finance/Functions.md`'s `fund_stage` "NULL → PS501" are aligned with #126 / #165 (D) and the code (`finance.begin_card_payment`, `projects.fund_stage` and `fund_ticket` take an individual project's escrow from its owner's personal wallet, and only the owner may fund it); `packages/types/org/enterprises.ts` no longer calls a business seller-side. **RULED by Decision #238 (2026-10-17) — was STILL OPEN, BB6 stopped on it:** (d.1) the edges of the co-owner grant — whether it carries `withdraw_funds` and `archive_entity` (outside the admin preset today; "exact same permissions" reads as yes), whether a co-owner may grant billing or co-ownership to others, and who may revoke it. Transferring ownership stays the owner's act either way. **FLAGGED:** (a) BZ0's acceptance grep for the stale business-id console route still matches `BUSINESSES_AUDIT.md` §0.1, the BZ0 prompt text and row #229, which quote the defect; they are left verbatim (the Teams #228 FLAGGED (c) precedent). | `business/PRODUCT_SPEC.md` (Businesses · Shopping cart & Checkout · §Sitemap) · `business/finance-model.md` (§1.3 · §16.1) · `database/finance/{Tables,Functions}.md` · `packages/types/org/enterprises.ts` · `architecture/BUSINESSES_BUILD_PROMPTS.md` (BB6 · BB7) · Decisions #61 / #126 / #165 / #228 / #229 |

| 240 | **One business spend gate on every door that moves business money — `finance.fn_assert_business_spend`, the actor's envelope counted once, the threshold with the platform fallback, KYB (2026-10-17). BUSINESSES_AUDIT BF19 / BF21 / BF22, work order BB3 of #229; CLOSES #54(e)'s open KYB-on-funding sign-off.** (A) **`finance.fn_business_threshold(wallet)`** = the wallet's `approval_threshold_cents`, else the platform's `vault_approval_threshold_cents`, else 0 — the one resolver the business basket, the gate, `request_spend_approval`, the redeemer's DETAIL and `fn_auto_fund_drain` read (BF19: the basket and requests used to ignore the fallback). (B) **The gate** `fn_assert_business_spend(wallet, actor, amount, ref_table, ref_id, count_envelope = true)`: no-op for a non-business wallet (team and personal spending unchanged); KYB else `PK403 kyb_required:`; the ACTOR's envelope via `fn_check_spending_limit`; the threshold. Codes stay `PK403` / `PA403`; the sub-code is the MESSAGE prefix and the DETAIL is jsonb (`wallet_id`, `ref_table`, `ref_id`, `amount_minor`, `threshold_minor`). (C) **Over the envelope routes through an approval, keeping the `over_limit` sub-code.** The prompt said (c) raises `PA403 over_limit`; finance-model §14 and the `evaluateSpend` SSOT (`types/workspace/policy.ts`) say an over-cap spend queues for a second approver, and `request_spend_approval` files such requests — so an approved request for the ref is redeemed (BB4's `fn_redeem_spend_approval`) and the spend counted past the cap; with none it is `PA403 over_limit:` with the redeemer's DETAIL. One approval clears envelope and threshold for one spend, consumed once (agreed with the BB4 session). (D) **Counted once, by a transaction-local cover, not a flag argument.** The prompt's "downstream holds call with `p_count_envelope = false`" would re-run the threshold at the hold and redeem a second approval; instead the earliest door makes the full check and leaves `finance.spend_cover` (bound to `pg_current_xact_id()`): a basket or stage opens a budget its door closes (`finance.fn_close_spend_cover`), `fund_ticket` / `fund_paid_revision` leave one ref for their hold; a covered call re-asserts KYB only. `count_envelope = false` therefore means a CHECK (KYB + threshold through the redeemer with `p_consume = false`; nothing counted, consumed or covered) — used by the card escrow lock at `begin_card_payment`; settlement's fund_* doors run the real gate. (E) **Call sites**: `place_wallet_order` (business, ref `baskets`), `transfer_funds` (business source, `transfers`), `fund_stage` (the stage total, `project_stages`), `fund_ticket` (`tickets`), `fund_paid_revision` (`revisions`), the card lock (check), and both escrow holds. `place_on_account_order` reaches the gate through the `place_wallet_order` it calls in the same transaction — one count, on what the order really charges — rather than a second call of its own (a refusal takes the on-account credit back with it). (F) **Automatic holds** (the claim-lock trigger, the Chain Reaction, the auto-fund drain) spend as the project owner, as before; a gate refusal there becomes the hold's `PF402` needs-funding refusal with `reason` `kyb_required` / `needs_approval` / `spending_limit` and the gate's DETAIL merged, so `fn_mark_needs_funding` and the drain (which files the `tickets` request) still read one vocabulary. (G) TS: `@projective/types/finance` `SpendGateReason`, `SPEND_GATE_REFUSAL`, `SpendGateRefTable` (= `SpendRefTable` minus `invoices`), `SpendGateDetailsSchema`; `packages/backend/core/spend-gate.ts` `spendGateRefusal` maps the refusal to a 403 without the prefix, wired into `live-payments.ts` `refusalOf`, `revision-purchase.ts` `walletFundingRefusal` (whose PA403/PK403 fell to a 500) and, by the BB4 session, `wallet-actions.ts`. **FLAGGED for a human:** (a) a TEAM or enterprise basket still reads only its wallet's `approval_threshold_cents` (no platform fallback): the prompt says every threshold read uses the resolver, and also that team paths stay unchanged — kept unchanged; a non-zero platform figure would otherwise refuse team baskets that have no way to file a request. **RULED 2026-10-17 by the product owner: keep it** — team and enterprise baskets stay on their wallet's own threshold; the platform fallback governs business wallets only. (b) The TS pre-flight readers `finance/live-buyer.ts` (checkout) and `workspace/live-money.ts` still read the wallet column only, so the pre-flight can say "allowed" where the database says `needs_approval` (BF19 mirror, follow-up). (c) The card lock's check does not test the envelope (nothing is counted before the charge), so an over-cap card lock is charged and settles into the wallet as `settled_unapplied`. (d) The early KYB refusals in `place_wallet_order` / `place_on_account_order` stay beside the gate for their own copy. (e) Pre-existing, unchanged: a hold that loses the `uq_escrows_ticket_base_held` race keeps the envelope count its gate made. (f) Business Pro gating of caps and the threshold (BF34) is BB7's. | `supabase/migrations/{00001150,00001200,00001210,00001230,00001250,00002510}` · `supabase/probes/business_spend_gate.sql` (+ fixtures in `approval_settlement`, `chain_reaction`, `stage_funding_prepaid`) · `packages/types/finance/spend-gate.ts` · `packages/backend/core/spend-gate.ts` · `packages/backend/services/finance/{live-payments,revision-purchase}.ts` · `database/finance/Functions.md` · `business/finance-model.md` §14 · `business/PRODUCT_SPEC.md` §Escrow #5 · `PRODUCT_MANAGEMENT.md` §3.6 · Decisions #54 / #165 / #229 / #230 |
